> 执行摘要
Executive summary About the Singapore Consensus执行摘要 关于《新加坡共识》
Frontier AI systems have advanced rapidly in capability and real-world impact. Revenues from frontier models have grown by over 400% from a year ago and AI invest-ments this year are on track to exceed the GDP of a top 30 national economy. At the same time, AI safety incidents now impact millions of users globally. To drive investment by private and public actors in AI safety research so that it keeps pace with capabili-ties advancement, Singapore organised the 2nd International Scientific Exchange on AI Safety (ISE) in May 2026, continuing the dialogue on global AI safety research priori-ties which began at the inaugural ISE in April 2025. 4
前沿人工智能系统的能力及其对现实世界的影响迅速提升。前沿模型的收入较一年前增长了超过 400%，而今年的人工智能投资预计将超过全球经济规模排名前 30 的一个国家的国内生产总值（GDP）。与此同时，人工智能安全事件如今已影响全球数百万用户。为推动私营和公共部门主体投资人工智能安全研究，使其跟上能力发展的步伐，新加坡于 2026 年 5 月举办了第 2 届人工智能安全国际科学交流会（ISE），延续了 2025 年 4 月首届 ISE 开启的关于全球人工智能安全研究优先事项的对话。4
> E x EC uTI vESu MMAR y
> 执行摘要
The 2026 Singapore Consensus on Global AI Safety Research Priorities ,which is an outcome of the 2026 convening, presents a global understanding on scientific research problems that are of top priority to advance AI safety research. Building on the Singapore Consensus 2025 report, the 2026 Main Report has been updated based on key developments in the AI risk landscape in the past year, such as the increasing deploy-ment and capabilities of autonomous AI agents and the growth of misuse and incidents. The report is developed as an agenda-focused complement to the International AI Safety Report with more than 100 contributors spanning 13 countries from frontier AI developers, government safety institutes, academics and civil society. At the same time, recognising that agentic deployment has become a major indus-try focus in the past year, ISE 2026 extends scientific discussion and consensus from research priorities to emerging risk management practices in the development and deployment of autonomous agents. Given that industry practices on agentic deployment are constantly evolving, there is tremendous value to achieving a shared understanding of the state-of-the-art practices to mitigate and manage these risks, draw-ing on the expertise and experiences of the global experts and world-leading organisations present at ISE 2026. This deep dive enriches the discussions at ISE 2026 and addresses a very timely and pertinent need from the industry. The discussions and findings are captured in the Companion Report on Agentic Risk Management , which incorporates and analyses input from global frontier AI developers on leading agentic safety practices .
《2026 年全球 AI 安全研究优先事项新加坡共识》是 2026 年会议的成果之一，提出了全球范围内对于推进 AI 安全研究所需优先解决的科学研究问题的共同认识。2026 年主报告以《2025 年新加坡共识》报告为基础，根据过去一年 AI 风险态势的重要变化进行了更新，例如自主 AI 智能体的部署日益广泛、能力不断增强，以及滥用行为和相关事件增多。本报告旨在从研究议程的角度补充《国际 AI 安全报告》，由来自 13 个国家的前沿 AI 开发机构、政府安全研究机构、学术界和公民社会的 100 多位贡献者共同编制。与此同时，鉴于智能体的部署已成为过去一年业界关注的重点，ISE 2026 将科学讨论与共识的范围从研究优先事项扩展到了自主智能体开发和部署过程中新兴的风险管理实践。鉴于业界在智能体部署方面的实践不断演变，借助出席 ISE 2026 的全球专家和世界领先组织的专业知识与经验，就缓解和管理这些风险的最先进实践形成共同认识，具有极大价值。这一深入探讨丰富了 ISE 2026 的讨论，并回应了业界一项十分及时且切合实际的需求。相关讨论与结论收录于《智能体风险管理配套报告》，该报告汇集并分析了全球前沿 AI 开发机构就领先的智能体安全实践提供的意见。
We hope to facilitate international dialogue and scientific collaboration among scientists, developers, and policymakers. Companies and nations often compete on AI capabilities research and development (R&D). However, we believe that research on managing AI’s risks is often an area of mutual interest (Bucknall et al, 2005), at least outside areas of sensitive intellectual property. Due to their global nature, many societal-level AI risks can only be addressed by coalitions of companies or countries. As the leaders of the global AI powers and companies convene this year, we believe it is in their interest to agree on informa-tion sharing and research collaboration for the global public good.
我们希望促进科学家、开发者和政策制定者之间的国际对话与科学合作。企业和国家往往在 AI 能力的研究与开发（R&D）方面展开竞争。然而，我们认为，管理 AI 风险的研究往往是各方具有共同利益的领域（Bucknall et al, 2005），至少在涉及敏感知识产权的领域之外是如此。由于许多社会层面的 AI 风险具有全球性，只有通过企业或国家之间的联盟才能加以应对。在全球 AI 强国和领先企业的领导者于今年齐聚之际，我们认为，就服务于全球公共利益的信息共享与研究合作达成一致，符合他们的利益。
Key updates主要更新
Growing Emphasis on Societal Resilience as Risk Prevention Shows Gaps
风险预防显现不足，社会韧性日益受到重视
Adopting a defence-in-depth model, the original report groups scientific AI safety research topics into three broad areas from risk assessment that informs sub-sequent development and deployment decisions, to technical safety methods in the system development phase, and tools for control after a system has been deployed. Grow-ing misuse and malfunction incidents show that harm prevention efforts alone are insufficient. We have therefore elevated societal resilience as a distinct fourth pillar to emphasize the growing importance of preparing and hardening societal systems for failures and misuse. 5
原报告采用纵深防御模型，将 AI 安全科学研究主题分为三大领域：为后续开发与部署决策提供依据的风险评估、系统开发阶段的技术安全方法，以及系统部署后的控制工具。日益增多的滥用和故障事件表明，仅靠预防危害的努力还不够。因此，我们将社会韧性提升为独立的第四大支柱，以强调使社会系统做好应对故障与滥用的准备、增强其抵御能力的重要性日益凸显。5
> E x EC uTI vESu MMAR y
> 执行摘要
Updated AI Safety Research Priorities
更新后的 AI 安全研究优先事项
The Main Report highlights new technical developments since ISE 2025, and outlines the research priorities that are especially important in light of these developments. The following research priorities are critical given developments over the last year: Prevention and resilience for growing misuse incidents. AI misuse incidents have grown precipitously, particularly for cyber attacks, nudification and other sexual abuse material. Biochemical misuse capabilities have surpassed PhD-level experts on some benchmarks and are being distributed with safeguards that can be con-sistently circumvented. New cyber model capabilities have abruptly increased the number of real-world vulnerabilities found in some critical software by over 1000%. Addressing this requires research on prevention and resilience methods targeted to these areas (Section 2, 3 and 4). Safety for open-weight models whose capabilities are nearing frontier closed models. According to common benchmarks, open weight models are estimated to be 3 to 12 months behind the frontier (varying across capabilities). Powerful AI models with publicly-downloadable weights are implicated in many misuse incidents. These models pose unique benefits for research and customization, but they can also be tampered with and used without oversight for malicious purposes. Manag-ing these risks requires unique tools for open-weight model safety (Section 2.2.5) and ecosystem monitoring (Section 4.1). Since prevention is challenging, research-ing societal resilience to misuse is essential (Section 4.2). Evaluations and alignment/control methods for emerging oversight-resistant and control-undermining AI. Control-undermining AI capabilities and behaviors are now a major challenge to safety testing in practice. They also mark early warn-ings for loss of control scenarios. Additionally, frontier developers now increasingly use AI to oversee AI internally - including for safety evaluations - meaning that human operators may struggle to understand and verify the system of oversight itself. Addressing this requires research on evaluations and methods for both align-ment and control that are more resistant to growing control-undermining AI tendencies such as evaluation awareness (Section 1.1), and methods to verify over-sight when it is outsourced to AI systems (Section 3.2 and 4.1).
主报告重点介绍了 ISE 2025 以来的新技术进展，并概述了鉴于这些进展而尤为重要的研究优先事项。鉴于过去一年的发展，以下研究重点至关重要：针对日益增多的滥用事件开展预防并增强韧性。AI 滥用事件急剧增加，尤其是在网络攻击、生成虚假裸体图像及其他性虐待材料方面。生物化学领域的滥用能力在一些基准测试中已超越博士水平的专家，而具备这些能力的系统正被广泛分发，其安全防护措施可被反复绕过。模型新增的网络能力使一些关键软件中发现的现实世界漏洞数量骤增，增幅超过 1000%。应对这一问题，需要研究针对这些领域的预防和韧性增强方法（第 2、3 和 4 节）。能力正接近前沿闭源模型的开放权重模型的安全性。根据常用基准测试，估计开放权重模型落后于前沿水平 3 至 12 个月（因能力类别而异）。权重可公开下载的强大 AI 模型涉及许多滥用事件。这些模型为研究和定制提供了独特益处，但也可能被篡改，并在缺乏监督的情况下被用于恶意目的。管理这些风险，需要专门针对开放权重模型安全的工具（第 2.2.5 节）和生态系统监测工具（第 4.1 节）。由于预防具有挑战性，研究社会抵御滥用的韧性至关重要（第 4.2 节）。针对新出现的、能够抵抗监督并破坏控制的 AI 的评估与对齐/控制方法。AI 破坏控制的能力和行为如今已成为实际安全测试中的一项重大挑战。它们也构成了失控情景的早期预警信号。此外，前沿 AI 开发者如今越来越多地在内部使用 AI 来监督 AI，包括开展安全评估，这意味着人类操作人员可能难以理解和验证监督体系本身。要应对这一问题，需要研究用于对齐与控制的评估手段和方法，使其更能抵御日益增强的、破坏控制的 AI 倾向，例如评估感知（第 1.1 节），同时还需要研究在将监督工作外包给 AI 系统时验证监督有效性的方法（第 3.2 节和第 4.1 节）。
Deep Dive into Agentic Risk Management
深入探讨智能体风险管理
2025 saw a sharp increase in deployment of AI agents, with releases accelerating sharply and agents’ autonomy levels rising in parallel. This momentum has continued into early 2026, with OpenClaw and its derivatives becoming the fastest-growing open source projects in history. Organizations are actively experimenting with ways of deploying autono-mous AI agents as they become more powerful. However, uncertainty over their reliability, security, and trustworthiness have emerged as barriers to adoption. Addressing this requires research on and trusted best practices for agent risk management, which are examined in depth in the Companion Report on Agentic AI Risk Management .6
2025 年，AI 智能体的部署量大幅增长，发布速度显著加快，智能体的自主程度也同步提高。这一势头延续到了 2026 年初，OpenClaw 及其衍生项目成为历史上增长最快的开源项目。随着自主 AI 智能体的能力不断增强，各组织正在积极尝试部署它们的方式。然而，对其可靠性、安全性和可信性的疑虑已成为采用这些智能体的障碍。要应对这一问题，需要开展智能体风险管理研究，并形成可信赖的最佳实践；《智能体 AI 风险管理配套报告》对此进行了深入探讨。6
> E x EC uTI vESu MMAR y
> 执行摘要
AI agents are becoming increasingly autonomous, capable, and widely applica-ble. They differ from earlier generative AI models in one important way: they can act, with direct effects on their environment and on the systems they connect to. These capabilities produce distinct risks and failure modes, yet governance mecha-nisms for agentic systems are only just beginning to emerge. As a result, uncertainty over their reliability, security, and trustworthiness has become a barrier to adoption. The 2026 International Scientific Exchange (ISE) on AI Safety therefore identified agentic risk management as a key direction for technical and governance research. The companion report analyses emerging practices in agentic AI risk management, draw-ing on input from leading model and agent developers, state-of-the-art academic research, and agentic governance frameworks. It identifies ten foundational principles for manag-ing agentic risk: least privilege , traceable identity , auditability , validated deployment ,
AI 智能体正变得越来越自主、能力越来越强，适用范围也越来越广。它们与早期生成式 AI 模型存在一个重要区别：它们能够采取行动，直接影响所处环境及其连接的系统。这些能力带来了独特的风险和失效模式，但针对智能体系统的治理机制才刚刚开始形成。因此，对其可靠性、安全性和可信性的疑虑已成为采用这些智能体的障碍。因此，2026 年 AI 安全国际科学交流会（ISE）将智能体风险管理确定为技术与治理研究的一个关键方向。这份配套报告结合领先模型和智能体开发者的意见、前沿学术研究以及智能体治理框架，分析了智能体 AI 风险管理领域正在形成的实践。报告确定了管理智能体风险的十项基本原则：最小权限、身份可追溯性、可审计性、经验证的部署、
adversarial resilience , multi-agent stability , runtime assurance , interruptibility , leg-ibility , and human oversight .
For each principle, the report sets out the governance context from jurisdictions including the US, China, Singapore, and the EU, and highlights technical practices for risk mitigation drawn from industry. The practices and evidence base underpinning these principles vary considerably in maturity. Some principles are better established and easier to implement in practice, with interoperable tools that make shared safety standards easier to achieve. Others, espe-cially around multi-agent systems and agentic supply chains, are still an area of ongoing research. The report surfaces open problems and shows how responsibility for agen-tic risk management is spread across developers, deployers, cloud platform providers, and the open source ecosystems. No single actor can ensure the reliability, security, and trustworthiness of AI agents. Therefore, this companion report provides a shared foun-dation for both technical and governance efforts to manage agentic risks.
针对每项原则，报告阐述了美国、中国、新加坡和欧盟等司法辖区的治理背景，并重点介绍了来自业界的风险缓解技术实践。支撑这些原则的实践及证据基础，其成熟度存在显著差异。一些原则已较为成熟，在实践中更容易落实，而且已有可互操作的工具，使共同安全标准更容易实现。另一些原则，尤其是涉及多智能体系统和智能体供应链的原则，仍处于持续研究阶段。报告揭示了尚未解决的问题，并说明了智能体风险管理责任如何分布于开发者、部署者、云平台提供商和开源生态系统之间。任何单一主体都无法确保 AI 智能体的可靠性、安全性和可信性。因此，这份配套报告为管理智能体风险的技术与治理工作提供了共同基础。
Contributing to Global Collaboration and Advancement促进全球合作与进步
Today, safety research and risk management are a priority for frontier AI adoption, innovation, and prevention of large-scale harms. While we are encouraged by a
如今，安全研究与风险管理是推动前沿 AI 应用、促进创新和预防大规模危害的优先事项。尽管令我们感到鼓舞的是，
high level of expert agreement on research priorities across global companies, coun-tries, and institutions, scientific progress must be matched by robust standards and policy frameworks to close the gap between the state of the art and the state of practice. Many recent AI incidents have been preventable with existing methods, and developing better methods alone is not enough without stronger incentives and accountability mechanisms to ensure that AI developers adopt best practices for safety and organizational risk management. Due to their global nature, AI risks are usually best addressed by coalitions rather than individual companies or countries. We hope this consensus can inform research funding, highlight where more transparency and reporting are needed to enable research, spur information sharing and col-laboration even among competitors where needed, and support the development of policy frameworks that allow everyone to embrace general-purpose AI safely and with confidence. 7
全球各公司、国家和机构的专家对研究优先事项达成了高度共识，但科学进步必须辅以健全的标准和政策框架，才能弥合前沿技术水平与实际应用水平之间的差距。近期许多 AI 事件本可通过现有方法加以预防；如果没有更强有力的激励措施和问责机制来确保 AI 开发者采用安全与组织风险管理方面的最佳实践，仅开发更好的方法是不够的。由于 AI 风险具有全球性，通常由多个主体组成联盟来应对，比单个公司或国家独自应对更为有效。我们希望这一共识能够为研究资助提供参考，指出哪些领域需要提高透明度和加强报告以支持研究，在必要时推动包括竞争对手之间在内的信息共享与合作，并支持制定政策框架，使每个人都能安全、放心地采用通用 AI。7
IMPLICATIONS FOR POLIC yMAK ERS
对政策制定者的启示
Research Priorities by Policy Area各政策领域的研究优先事项
The Singapore Consensus is a scientific consensus on technical AI safety research priorities, not a policy document. The Executive Summary sets out why a shared, prior-itised research agenda matters now: capabilities, real-world deployment and revenues are rising faster than our ability to measure and manage risks. Meanwhile, uncer-tainty about safety is itself holding back adoption. Agreeing on where research effort should go, and sharing much of what is learned, is often an international area of mutual interest: it serves every party at little to no competitive cost. This note translates the research areas into policy-relevant domains to inform research funding, standards development, and international coordination. Each area below opens with what is at stake, followed by the research directions experts judge most urgent. Section numbers point to the main report. 8
《新加坡共识》是关于 AI 安全技术研究优先事项的科学共识，并非政策文件。执行摘要阐述了为何当前需要一份共同的、明确优先次序的研究议程：AI 能力、现实世界中的部署规模和收入的增长速度，正超过我们衡量与管理风险的能力提升速度。与此同时，对安全性的不确定本身也在阻碍采用。就研究工作应聚焦何处达成共识，并分享大部分研究所得，往往是国际社会具有共同利益的领域：这既有利于各方，又几乎不会或完全不会使任何一方付出竞争方面的代价。本说明将这些研究领域转化为与政策相关的议题，为研究资助、标准制定和国际协调提供参考。以下每个领域都先说明利害所在，再列出专家认为最紧迫的研究方向。章节编号均指向主报告中的相应章节。8
> IMPLICATIONS FOR POLIC yMAK ERS
> 对政策制定者的启示
Cyber misuse risk网络滥用风险
What is at stake: As frontier systems grow more capable, a central challenge is to ensure evaluation and defence move ahead of offence. Priorities include better ways to anticipate how attackers might use AI (“threat models”) and tougher tests of AI’s offensive cyber abilities (“harder cyber capability evaluations”) that keep pace with frontier systems (1.1, 1.3); develop-ing tools that favor defenders (defense-favoring AI-for-cyber capabilities) and allow vulnerabilities, including in critical infrastructure, to be patched faster (4.2); monitor-ing collections of multiple AI agents once deployed, so coordinated misuse campaigns can be detected and shut down (4.1); stress-testing openly released models to see what they could do if deliberately retrained for harm (“malicious fine-tuning”) (2.2.5); and shared reporting of serious AI-enabled cyber incidents (4.2).
关键所在：随着前沿系统的能力不断增强，一项核心挑战是确保评估和防御走在攻击之前。优先事项包括：改进预判攻击者可能如何使用 AI 的方法（“威胁模型”），并开展更严格的 AI 网络攻击能力测试（“更高难度的网络能力评估”），以跟上前沿系统的发展（1.1, 1.3）；开发有利于防御者的工具（偏向防御的 AI 网络安全能力），使包括关键基础设施漏洞在内的漏洞能够更快得到修补（4.2）；在部署后监测由多个 AI 智能体构成的群体，以发现并制止协同滥用行动（4.1）；对公开发布的模型进行压力测试，考察它们在被蓄意重新训练以实施危害时可能具备的能力（“恶意微调”）（2.2.5）；以及共享由 AI 助长的严重网络事件报告（4.2）。
Biological and chemical security生物与化学安全
What is at stake: The aim is to prevent models from meaningfully advancing weapons development, including pandemic-scale threats; to keep such capabilities out of nov-ices’ hands; and to tilt the balance toward defence across the supply chain. Priorities include: studies and tests that measure how much a model actually increases a would-be attacker’s capabilities (“uplift studies”), paired with prac-tical thresholds for unacceptable levels of risk (1.1, 1.3); shared filters that keep weapons-relevant content out of the data models are trained on (“pretrain-ing data filters”) (2.2.1); methods that aim to durably remove dangerous knowledge from openly released models and tests to confirm it cannot be easily retrained back in (“tamper resistant unlearning”) (2.2.5); and screening further up the supply chain globally, for example, of commercial DNA synthesis orders, to build a defenders’ advantage (4.2).
关键所在：目标是防止模型实质性推进武器研发，包括带来大流行规模威胁的武器研发；防止此类能力落入新手手中；并在整个供应链中使攻防力量对比向防御一方倾斜。优先事项包括：通过研究和测试，衡量模型实际上在多大程度上提升了潜在攻击者的能力（“能力提升研究”），并为不可接受的风险水平设定切实可用的阈值（1.1, 1.3）；共享过滤器，将与武器相关的内容排除在模型训练数据之外（“预训练数据过滤器”）（2.2.1）；开发旨在从公开发布的模型中持久移除危险知识的方法，并通过测试确认这些知识无法轻易通过重新训练恢复（“抗篡改机器遗忘”）（2.2.5）；以及在全球供应链更上游开展筛查，例如筛查商业 DNA 合成订单，以建立防御者的优势（4.2）。
Child safety and image-based abuse儿童安全与基于图像的侵害
What is at stake: Here, the science is comparatively mature; the binding con-straint is standardisation, adoption and safeguards that cannot be easily stripped out of systems. Priorities include: shared data filters that remove child sexual abuse mate-rial (“CSAM”) and other abuse content from training data (2.2.1); reliable methods to remove “nudification” and similar capabilities from models, with safeguards that survive later modification (such as fine-tuning) of openly released models (2.2.3, 2.2.5); detection tools, classifiers, watermarking, and content-metadata standards, where the main barrier is standardization and adoption (4.1); and measuring how often these harms occur and their real-world impact on children (1.3). 9
关键所在：这一领域的科学研究相对成熟；真正的制约因素是标准化、采用推广，以及建立无法轻易从系统中移除的防护措施。优先事项包括：共享数据过滤器，从训练数据中移除儿童性虐待材料（“CSAM”）和其他侵害性内容（2.2.1）；开发可靠方法，从模型中移除“脱衣”及类似能力，并确保防护措施在公开发布的模型经历后续修改（如微调）后仍然有效（2.2.3, 2.2.5）；开发检测工具、分类器、水印技术和内容元数据标准，其主要障碍在于标准化和采用推广（4.1）；以及衡量这些危害的发生频率及其对儿童的现实影响（1.3）。9
> IMPLICATIONS FOR POLIC yMAK ERS
> 对政策制定者的启示
Mental health and consumer protection心理健康与消费者保护
What is at stake: The priority is to detect and prevent growing harms to users, espe-cially vulnerable ones, before and after deployment. Priorities include: specification and validation methods that prevent and measure sycophancy, e.g. models affirming and reinforcing users’ harmful beliefs (2.1); rapid, high-coverage pre-deployment risk identification, e.g. fast, broad-screen-ing for unknown risks before a model is released (1.1); real-world trials of mental-health effects with cautious, graduated rollout, as in clinical trials (1.3); monitoring during use to identify users who may be vulnerable (4.1); and clear pro-cesses inside organisations for escalating concerns and reporting incidents (1.2, 4.2).
关键所在：首要任务是在部署前后发现并预防对用户、尤其是脆弱用户日益增加的危害。优先事项包括：制定用于预防和衡量迎合行为的规范与验证方法，例如模型肯定并强化用户有害信念的行为（2.1）；在部署前快速、全面地识别风险，例如在模型发布前对未知风险进行快速、广泛的筛查（1.1）；借鉴临床试验的做法，通过审慎、分阶段的推出方式，开展关于心理健康影响的现实环境试验（1.3）；在使用过程中进行监测，以识别可能较为脆弱的用户（4.1）；以及在组织内部建立明确的流程，用于将关切上报至更高层级并报告事件（1.2, 4.2）。
AI agents in the economy经济活动中的 AI 智能体
What is at stake: Reliability and security concerns are leading bottlenecks to adop-tion, so progress here is as much an enabler of innovation as a safeguard. Priorities include: designing agents to operate safely and confining them to controlled environments (“sandboxing”) so mistakes stay contained (2.2.6, 2.2.3); benchmarks that close the gap between evaluated and real-world reliability (1.1); defenses against manipulating agents through hidden instructions (“prompt injection”) and the resulting hijacking of agents (2.2.4); real-time monitoring and the ability to intervene while an an agent is running (3.1); multi-agent red-teaming, e.g. adversar-ial testing of multiple agents working together (2.3.1); and infrastructure to identify and authenticate agents so their actions can be traced (4.1). Agent risk-manage-ment practices are discussed in depth in the Companion Report.
关键所在：对可靠性和安全性的担忧是采用推广的主要瓶颈，因此，这方面的进展既能提供保障，也能促进创新。优先事项包括：将智能体设计为能够安全运行，并将其限制在受控环境中（“沙箱隔离”），从而将错误的影响控制在有限范围内（2.2.6, 2.2.3）；建立能够缩小评估可靠性与现实可靠性之间差距的基准（1.1）；防御通过隐藏指令操纵智能体的攻击（“提示注入”）及其引发的智能体劫持（2.2.4）；实施实时监测，并具备在智能体运行期间进行干预的能力（3.1）；开展多智能体红队测试，例如对协同工作的多个智能体进行对抗性测试（2.3.1）；以及建设用于识别智能体并验证其身份的基础设施，使其行为可被追溯（4.1）。配套报告深入讨论了智能体风险管理实践。
Open-weight model safety and security开放权重模型的安全性与安保
What is at stake: Because model risks spread internationally and irreversibly once weights are released, with safeguards that are relatively easy to remove, manag-ing open-weight model misuse is a shared international interest. Priorities include testing for worst-case misuse, including deliberate retraining for harm, before a model is released (2.2.5); careful curation and filtering of training-data, currently the safeguard hardest to strip out after release (2.2.1, 2.2.5); develop-ing effective methods to make models reliably “unlearn” dangerous capabilities (2.2.5); tools to trace the origin of harmful models and their actions circulating in the wild (“provenance”) (4.1); and strengthening broader societal resilience against misuse, since prevention alone cannot be relied upon one weights are public (4.2).
关键所在：一旦权重发布，模型风险就会跨国传播且不可逆转，而其防护措施又相对容易移除，因此，管理开放权重模型的滥用符合国际社会的共同利益。优先事项包括：在模型发布前测试最坏情况下的滥用，包括为造成危害而蓄意进行的再训练（2.2.5）；审慎筛选和过滤训练数据，这是目前模型发布后最难被剥离的防护措施（2.2.1, 2.2.5）；开发有效方法，使模型能够可靠地“遗忘”危险能力（2.2.5）；开发工具，追溯现实环境中流传的有害模型及其行为的来源（“来源追溯”，provenance）（4.1）；以及增强更广泛的社会抵御滥用的韧性，因为一旦权重公开，就不能仅依赖预防措施（4.2）。
Loss of control and oversight of automated frontier AI development对自动化前沿 AI 开发失去控制与监督
What is at stake: AI developers now use AI systems to automate major parts of safety and oversight internally. The priority is to ensure that their systems and processes remain accessible and controllable. Priorities include: evaluations that still work even when a model can determine if it is being tested and may therefore understate its harmful capabilities (“sand-bagging”) or conceal misalignment (“alignment faking”), backed by secure access 10
关键所在：AI 开发者如今已在内部使用 AI 系统，将安全与监督工作中的主要部分自动化。首要任务是确保其系统和流程保持可访问、可控制。优先事项包括：即使模型能够判断自己是否正在接受测试，并因此可能低报自身的有害能力（“藏拙”，sand-bagging）或掩盖未对齐状态（“伪装对齐”，alignment faking），评估仍能有效开展，并得到安全访问机制的支持 10
> IMPLICATIONS FOR POLIC yMAK ERS
> 对政策制定者的启示
for independent third parties (1.1, 1.5); assessing control-undermining capabilities and propensities and defining clear, operationalizable red lines (1.4); verify-ing that AI systems used to supervise other AI are themselves trustworthy, as developers are automating oversight internally (3.2); keeping a model’s step-by-step “chains-of-thought” honest and human-readable so they can be monitored 3.1); using non-agentic AI systems as safer guardrails for agents (3.2); and metrics for AI R&D automation and secure infrastructure giving external authorities awareness of internal deployments crossing regulatory thresholds (4.1).
供独立第三方使用（1.1, 1.5）；评估削弱控制的能力和倾向，并界定清晰、可操作的红线（1.4）；鉴于开发者正在将内部监督自动化，验证用于监督其他 AI 的 AI 系统本身是否可信（3.2）；确保模型逐步展开的“思维链”真实且可供人类阅读，以便对其进行监测 3.1）；使用非智能体式 AI 系统，为智能体提供更安全的防护（3.2）；以及制定 AI 研发自动化的衡量指标，并建立安全基础设施，使外部主管机构能够知悉跨越监管阈值的内部部署（4.1）。
Recurring across areas贯穿各领域的共同方向
Several directions cut across every area above: agreed risk thresholds (Section 1), secure evaluation infrastructure for third-party scrutiny (1.5), organizational system safety (1.2), shared incident reporting (4.2), and hardware-enabled mechanisms that let companies or even state actors prove they are honoring commitments without exposing proprietary information (3.1, 4.1). 11
有几个方向贯穿上述所有领域：商定的风险阈值（第 1 节）、供第三方审查的安全评估基础设施（1.5）、组织层面的系统安全（1.2）、共享事件报告（4.2），以及由硬件支持的机制，使企业乃至国家行为体能够在不披露专有信息的情况下证明其正在履行承诺（3.1, 4.1）。11

> Main Report
> 主报告
Introduction Building a Trusted Ecosystem引言 构建可信生态系统
Rapidly improving AI capabilities and autonomy hold the promise of boosting economic growth and accelerating scientific advances. At the same time, a growing number of real-world incidents highlight the importance of building a trusted AI ecosystem. This will help stakeholders embrace AI with confidence and give maximal space for innovation
AI 能力和自主性的快速提升，有望推动经济增长并加速科学进步。与此同时，越来越多的现实事件凸显了构建可信 AI 生态系统的重要性。这将有助于利益相关方满怀信心地采用 AI，并为创新提供最大空间
while avoiding backlash. Building this ecosystem requires policymakers, industry, research-ers and the broader public to collectively work toward securing positive outcomes from AI’s development. AI safety research is a key dimension . Despite encouraging progress, the state of science today for building trustworthy AI does not adequately cover some severe risks. Investments in AI capabilities continue to outpace safety efforts. As a result, accelerated public and private investment in research is required to keep pace with commercially driven growth in system capabilities, reduce harms, and enable respon-sible adoption. 12
同时避免引发抵制。构建这一生态系统，需要政策制定者、产业界、研究人员和更广泛的公众共同努力，确保 AI 发展带来积极成果。AI 安全研究是其中的一个关键方面。尽管已取得令人鼓舞的进展，但当前构建可信 AI 的科学研究仍未充分涵盖某些严重风险。对 AI 能力的投入持续超过对安全工作的投入。因此，需要加快公共和私营部门对研究的投入，以跟上商业驱动的系统能力增长，减少危害，并促进负责任的采用。12
> INTRO du CTION
> 引言
Goals目标
The 2026 Singapore International Scientific Exchange on AI Safety aims to support research in this space by bringing together scientists across geographies to identify and synthesise research priorities and surface areas that require more investment. The result, The Singapore Consensus on Global AI Safety Research Priorities, offers an agenda-focused complement to the International AI Safety Report-A (IAISR) chaired by Yoshua Bengio and backed by 29 national governments. Through the Singapore Consensus, we hope to highlight points of widespread agreement between AI scientists and AI policymakers for maximally beneficial outcomes. Our goal is to enable more impact-ful R&D efforts to rapidly develop safety and evaluation mechanisms and foster a trusted ecosystem where AI is harnessed for the public good.
2026 年新加坡 AI 安全国际科学交流会旨在汇聚来自不同地区的科学家，确定并综合梳理研究优先事项，揭示需要更多投入的领域，从而支持这一领域的研究。由此形成的《全球 AI 安全研究优先事项新加坡共识》，以研究议程为重点，对由 Yoshua Bengio 主持并获 29 国政府支持的《国际 AI 安全报告-A》（IAISR）形成补充。通过《新加坡共识》，我们希望突出 AI 科学家与 AI 政策制定者之间的广泛共识，以实现尽可能有益的成果。我们的目标是推动更具影响力的研发工作，迅速开发安全与评估机制，并培育一个利用 AI 造福公众的可信生态系统。
The Role of International dialogue国际对话的作用
International dialogue among companies, policymakers, and scientists is particularly important for managing AI, a technology that does not respect borders. Misuse and malfunctions stemming from one place can cause harm anywhere in the world. Addi-tionally, history shows that a few high-profile incidents anywhere in the world can create unease that prevents everyone from realizing the benefits of a technology. It is therefore in the interest of all nations and companies to collaborate on certain safety research and share certain safety information, even when they believe themselves to be competitors.
AI 是一种不受国界限制的技术，因此，企业、政策制定者与科学家之间的国际对话对于 AI 治理尤为重要。源于某一地区的滥用和故障，可能在世界任何地方造成危害。此外，历史表明，世界任何地方发生的少数几起备受关注的事件，都可能引发不安，阻碍所有人享受某项技术带来的益处。因此，即使各国和企业认为彼此是竞争对手，在某些安全研究上开展合作并共享某些安全信息，也符合各方的利益。
Areas of mutual interest: While companies and nations often compete on AI research and development, they also have incentives to find alignment and common interests. This report covers areas where different par-ties may compete, but also highlights examples from the broader landscape of areas of mutual interest - research products and information that developers have minimal downside risks in collaborating on (Bucknall et al, 2005, Blomquist et al., 2025). Cer-tain safety advances offer minimal competitive edge while serving a common interest - similar to how competing aircraft manufacturers (e.g., Boeing and Airbus) col-laborate on aviation safety information and standards. In AI, particular areas for mutually-beneficial cooperation span sections 1-3 of this report and include certain verification mechanisms, risk-management standards, and risk evaluations (Bucknall et al, 2005). The motivation is clear: no organisation or country benefits when AI incidents occur or malicious actors are enabled, as the resulting harm would damage every-one collectively.
共同利益领域：尽管企业和国家经常在 AI 研发方面展开竞争，但它们也有动力寻求协调一致和共同利益。本报告涵盖不同参与方可能展开竞争的领域，同时也从更广泛的共同利益领域中列举了一些例子，即开发者开展合作时潜在不利风险极小的研究成果和信息（Bucknall et al, 2005, Blomquist et al., 2025）。某些安全进展带来的竞争优势极小，却能服务于共同利益——这类似于相互竞争的飞机制造商（例如 Boeing 和 Airbus）在航空安全信息和标准方面开展合作。在 AI 领域，适合开展互利合作的具体领域分布于本报告第 1-3 节，包括某些验证机制、风险管理标准和风险评估（Bucknall et al, 2005）。其动机很明确：AI 事件的发生或恶意行为者获得助力，都不会使任何组织或国家受益，因为由此造成的危害会损害所有人的共同利益。
2026 Process2026 年编制过程
This document is an update of the 2025 Singapore Consensus on Global AI Safety Research Priorities. It represents a comprehensive synthesis of research proposals drawn from the International AI Safety Report and complementary recent research prioritisation frameworks. Scope范围
We limit our discussion to technical AI safety research, focused on making AI more trustworthy rather than merely more powerful. AI policy research is out of scope for this report. We focus primarily on general-purpose AI : Following the International AI Safety Report, the term ‘AI systems’ in this document should be understood to refer to general-purpose AI (GPAI) systems - systems that can perform or can be adapted to perform a wide range of tasks (IAISR). This includes language models that produce text (e.g. chat systems) as well as ‘multimodal’ models which can work with multiple types of data, often including text, images, video, audio, and robotic actions. Narrow systems such as self-driving cars are therefore out of scope.
我们的讨论仅限于 AI 安全技术研究，重点是使 AI 更值得信赖，而非仅仅使其更强大。AI 政策研究不在本报告的范围之内。我们主要关注通用 AI：沿用《国际 AI 安全报告》的定义，本文档中的“AI 系统”一词应理解为通用 AI（GPAI）系统，即能够执行或经过调整后能够执行广泛任务的系统（IAISR）。这包括生成文本的语言模型（例如聊天系统），以及能够处理多种数据类型的“多模态”模型，这些数据通常包括文本、图像、视频、音频和机器人动作。因此，自动驾驶汽车等专用系统不在讨论范围之内。
Structure结构
Inspired by the 2025 International AI Safety Report (IAISR), this document adopts a defence-in-depth model and groups AI safety research topics into four broad areas from risk assessment that informs subsequent development and deployment decisions, 14
受《2025 年国际 AI 安全报告》（IAISR）启发，本文档采用纵深防御模型，将 AI 安全研究主题划分为四大领域，从为后续开发和部署决策提供依据的风险评估，14

> 引言
to technical methods in the system development phase, and tools for control after a system has been deployed, and resilience through that hardening society and responding to incidents that do occur.
到系统开发阶段的技术方法、系统部署后的控制工具，以及通过增强社会抵御能力并应对实际发生的事件来提升韧性。
Risk Assessment : The primary goal of risk assessment is to understand the sever-ity and likelihood of potential harm. Risk assessments are used to prioritise risks and determine if they cross thresholds that demand specific action. Consequen-tial development and deployment decisions are predicated on these assessments. The research areas in this category involve developing methods to measure the
风险评估：风险评估的主要目标是了解潜在危害的严重程度及其发生的可能性。风险评估用于确定风险的优先级，并判断风险是否超过了需要采取特定行动的阈值。具有重大影响的开发和部署决策以这些评估为依据。这一类别的研究领域包括开发用于衡量
impact of AI systems for both current and future AI, and building enablers for third-party audits to support independent validation of these risk assessments. 2. Development : AI systems that are trustworthy, reliable and secure give stakehold-ers the confidence to embrace and adopt AI innovation. Following the classic safety engineering framework, the research areas in this category involve methods for specifying the desired behaviour, designing an AI system that meets the specification, and verifying that the system meets its specification. 3. Control : Monitoring and Intervention: In engineering, “control” usually refers to the process of managing a system’s behaviour to achieve a desired outcome, even when faced with disturbances, uncertainties, or feedback loops. The research areas in this category involve developing monitoring and intervention mechanisms
当前和未来 AI 系统影响的方法，以及为第三方审计建立支持条件，以支持对这些风险评估进行独立验证。2.开发：值得信赖、可靠且安全的 AI 系统能够让利益相关方有信心接受并采用 AI 创新。遵循经典安全工程框架，这一类别的研究领域包括规定期望行为、设计符合规范的 AI 系统，以及验证系统是否符合其规范的方法。3.控制：监测与干预：在工程学中，“控制”通常指管理系统行为以实现预期结果的过程，即使系统面临扰动、不确定性或反馈回路也是如此。这一类别的研究领域涉及开发监测和干预机制
for AI systems. 4. Societal Resilience: Despite prevention efforts, the last year has seen growing inci-dents from AI misuse and malfunctions. Therefore, this year’s report draws out societal resilience as its own section. Societal resilience complements prevention by enabling monitoring, preparation, hardening, and response at an ecosystem level .15
以用于 AI 系统。4.社会韧性：尽管已采取预防措施，过去一年中，AI 滥用和故障引发的事件仍日益增多。因此，今年的报告将社会韧性单独列为一节。社会韧性通过在生态系统层面支持监测、准备、加固和响应，为预防措施提供补充。15

> 引言
The Main Report is complemented by the Companion Report on Risk Management.
《风险管理配套报告》对主报告作了补充。
The Companion Report identifies new AI agents - systems that can act autonomously - as a rapidly growing source of risks, which are also a leading bottleneck to AI agent adoption. A particularly important research priority identified here is to develop and codify best practices for agent risk management. Therefore, this year’s ISE has an additional focus on risk management practices for agents covered in an additional document.
配套报告指出，新兴的 AI 智能体——能够自主行动的系统——正成为一个迅速增长的风险来源，而这些风险也是采用 AI 智能体的主要瓶颈。此处确定的一项尤为重要的优先研究任务，是制定并系统整理智能体风险管理的最佳实践。因此，今年的 ISE 还特别关注智能体的风险管理实践，并在一份附加文件中加以讨论。
Term How it is used in this report
术语 在本报告中的用法
Specification Specific definition of desired system behaviour Validation Ensuring that the specification and the final system meets the needs of the user, developer, or society (did I build the right system?) Validity How well a measurement or assessment tool actually measures what it claims to measure. AI agent An AI which can make plans to achieve goals, adaptively perform tasks involving multiple steps and uncertain outcomes along the way, and interact with its environment - for example by creating files, taking actions on the web, or delegating tasks to other agents - with little to no human oversight. AI model A computer program, often automatically created by learning from data, designed to process inputs and generate outputs. AI models can perform tasks such as prediction, classification, decision-making, or generation, forming the engine of AI systems. Open-weight AI model An AI model whose parameters are publicly available for download by anyone on the internet. Open-weight models can be used and modified in unrestricted ways by downstream users. AI system An integrated setup that combines one or more AI models with other components, such as user interfaces or content filters, to produce an application that users can interact with. Verification Providing qualitative or quantitative justifications or guarantees that a system meets its specification (did I build the system right?) Assurance The broader process of determining if a system performs as intended. As such, providing assurance requires appropriate specification, validation, design, implementation and verification. Control Monitoring a system after it has been created and intervening where needed, often in a feedback loop, to ensure the system behaves as desired. Alignment Creating/modifying AI to meet intended behaviour, goals, and values (current emphasis tends to be on behaviour) Intelligence Ability to accomplish goals Artificial intelligence (AI) Non-biological intelligence 16
规格（Specification） 对期望的系统行为作出的具体定义 确认（Validation） 确保规格和最终系统满足用户、开发者或社会的需求（我构建的是所需的系统吗？）效度（Validity） 测量或评估工具实际测得其声称要测量的内容的程度。AI 智能体 能够制订计划以实现目标，在过程中自适应地执行涉及多个步骤且结果不确定的任务，并在很少或完全没有人类监督的情况下与环境交互的 AI，例如创建文件、在网络上采取行动或将任务委派给其他智能体。AI 模型 一种旨在处理输入并生成输出的计算机程序，通常通过从数据中学习而自动创建。AI 模型可以执行预测、分类、决策或生成等任务，是 AI 系统的核心引擎。开放权重 AI 模型 参数公开可用、互联网上任何人都可以下载的 AI 模型。下游用户可以不受限制地使用和修改开放权重模型。AI 系统 将一个或多个 AI 模型与用户界面或内容过滤器等其他组件相结合，从而形成用户可以与之交互的应用程序的集成系统。验证（Verification） 提供定性或定量的依据或保证，以证明系统符合其规格（我是否正确地构建了系统？）保障（Assurance） 判断系统是否按预期运行的更广泛过程。因此，提供保障需要适当的规格制定、确认、设计、实现和验证。控制（Control） 在系统创建后对其进行监测，并在必要时加以干预，通常通过反馈回路确保系统按期望方式运行。对齐（Alignment） 创建或修改 AI，使其符合预期的行为、目标和价值观（目前往往侧重于行为） 智能 实现目标的能力 人工智能（AI） 非生物智能 16

Key information and updates关键信息与最新进展
Risk assessment aims to understand the severity and likelihood of potential harms, and informs high-stakes deployment decisions today. In many cases, it also represents a global
风险评估旨在了解潜在危害的严重程度及其发生的可能性，并为当前事关重大的部署决策提供依据。在许多情况下，它也代表着一个全球性的
area of mutual interest . Existing AI regulations and AI company commitments require rigorous risk identification and assessment, and consequential deployment decisions are predicated on these assessments (e.g. EU AI Act Website, 2024; OpenAI, 2023; Anthropic, 2023 Google, 2025). Several new developments since SCAI 2025 are important to address. This summary lists research priorities that are particularly important given these new developments.
共同利益领域。现有的人工智能法规和人工智能公司的承诺要求严格开展风险识别与评估，而影响重大的部署决策以这些评估为依据（例如 EU AI Act Website, 2024; OpenAI, 2023; Anthropic, 2023 Google, 2025）。SCAI 2025 以来出现的若干新进展值得重点关注。本摘要列出了鉴于这些新进展而尤为重要的优先研究事项。
Autonomous AI cyber capabilities and incidents have increased. A leading model developer, Anthropic, has reported in detail that their model has been misused for a largely automated set of cyber attacks in 2025. Later, the Claude Mythos Preview model 18
人工智能自主开展网络攻击的能力有所增强，相关事件也有所增加。领先的模型开发商 Anthropic 详细报告称，其模型在 2025 年被滥用于实施一系列高度自动化的网络攻击。随后，Claude Mythos Preview 模型 18

> 1 风险评估
showed significantly increased cyber capabilities. Some model developers report that their evaluations no longer rule out ‘high’ level of risk, referring to automation of end-to-end operations or operationally relevant cyber vulnerability discovery and exploitation. There is a broad expectation in the scientific community that Mythos-level cyber-offense capabilities can be expected to proliferate, in some cases through open models with ineffective safeguards against misuse, causing cybersecurity disruptions within months.
展现出显著增强的网络攻防能力。一些模型开发商报告称，其评估已无法排除“高”等级的风险，这里的风险指端到端行动的自动化，或具有实际行动价值的网络漏洞发现与利用能力。科学界普遍预期，达到 Mythos 水平的网络进攻能力将会扩散，其中部分能力将通过缺乏有效防滥用措施的开放模型传播，并在数月内对网络安全造成冲击。
Research priorities: Understanding the most critical threat models (see Section 1) and building harder cyber capability evaluations (see Section 2). Investing in defense-favoring AI-for-cyber capabilities, deployment of those capabilities, as well as developing technology for rapidly deploying patches for vulnerabilities found, including in critical infrastructure services (see Section 4).
研究优先事项：了解最关键的威胁模型（见第 1 节），并构建难度更高的网络攻防能力评估（见第 2 节）。投资于有利于防御的人工智能网络安全能力及其部署，并开发能够快速部署已发现漏洞补丁的技术，包括用于关键基础设施服务的此类技术（见第 4 节）。
‘High’ levels of biological weapon misuse risks are no longer ruled out. Model’s scientific capabilities have grown rapidly, and multiple model developers report that their evaluations no longer rule out ‘high’ biological misuse risks. Other developers are now on the cusp of releasing similarly capable models but with open weights, meaning they currently lack effective safeguards against misuse, such as refusing to help with biological attacks. A major, AI-enabled biological attack may be possible within months or years.
已无法排除“高”等级的生物武器滥用风险。模型的科学能力迅速提升，多家模型开发商报告称，其评估已无法排除“高”等级的生物领域滥用风险。其他开发商如今也即将发布能力相近但权重开放的模型，这意味着这些模型目前缺乏有效的防滥用措施，例如拒绝协助实施生物攻击。数月或数年内，借助人工智能实施重大生物攻击或将成为可能。
Research priorities: Researching threat models and better clarifying biological misuse capabilities, including with uplift studies (Section 1.3). Improving open weight model safety (Section 2.2.5).
研究优先事项：研究威胁模型，并进一步厘清生物领域的滥用能力，包括通过能力增益研究（uplift studies）开展研究（第 1.3 节）。提高开放权重模型的安全性（第 2.2.5 节）。
Non-consensual AI “nudification” and sexual abuse material incidents have greatly increased. This has been driven by video deepfake capabilities becoming widely accessible through open models, and some commercial providers lacking effective safeguards against nudification.
未经当事人同意、利用人工智能进行“脱衣”及生成性虐待材料的事件大幅增加。这主要是由于视频深度伪造能力通过开放模型变得广泛可及，以及部分商业服务提供商缺乏防止“脱衣”的有效措施。
Research priorities: Evaluations for open models (for safeguards that resist removal) (see Section 2.2.5), including tools for pre-training data filtering. Ecosystem monitoring and evaluations of downstream societal impacts (see Section 4).
研究优先事项：针对开放模型开展评估（评估抵抗移除的安全防护措施）（见第 2.2.5 节），包括预训练数据过滤工具。开展生态系统监测，并评估下游社会影响（见第 4 节）。
Serious psychological safety incidents have occurred. LLMs have been involved in suicides and other incidents among psychologically vulnerable individuals, with LLMs sometimes confirming and actively supporting individuals in harmful beliefs and plans.
已经发生了严重的心理安全事件。心理脆弱人群中的自杀及其他事件涉及大语言模型（LLMs），这些模型有时会认同并积极支持个人的有害信念和计划。
Research priorities: Rapid, exploratory, and high-coverage risk identification for pre-deployment testing to avoid the release of models with risks that their developers are unaware of (see Section 1.1). Adapting established processes for risk management, organization-wide system safety, incident reporting and escalation (see Sections 1.2-1.5).
研究优先事项：开展快速、探索性且覆盖面广的风险识别，用于部署前测试，以避免发布存在开发商尚未察觉风险的模型（见第 1.1 节）。调整现有的风险管理、组织范围内的系统安全、事件报告与升级上报流程，使其适用于相关情境（见第 1.2-1.5 节）。
Existing AI regulations and AI company commitments require risk identification and assessment, and these decisions determine whether systems costing billions of dollars can be deployed or not. (e.g. EU, 2024; OpenAI, 2023; Anthropic, 2023 Google, 2025). The primary goal of risk assessment is to understand the severity and likelihood of a potential harm. Risk assessments are used to prioritise risks and to determine if they cross risk thresh-olds that demand specific action such as mitigation. These thresholds - often defined in terms of measurable key risk indicators such as model evaluations (Campos, 2025) 19
现有的人工智能法规和人工智能公司的承诺要求开展风险识别与评估，而这些决策决定了耗资数十亿美元的系统能否部署。（例如 EU, 2024; OpenAI, 2023; Anthropic, 2023 Google, 2025）。风险评估的首要目标是了解潜在危害的严重程度及其发生的可能性。风险评估用于确定风险的优先级，并判断风险是否超过了需要采取缓解等特定行动的风险阈值。这些阈值——通常以模型评估等可衡量的关键风险指标来界定（Campos, 2025）19
> 1 R ISK ASSESSMENT
> 1 风险评估
serve as early warnings or red lines. For example, if a system is found to have the ability to substantially assist malicious users in conducting cyberattacks, this may be considered an unacceptable risk. Risk assessment also informs safer development practices (Section 2) and control practices (Section 3) needed to mitigate risks. Carefully defined risk thresholds are this report’s first example of a potential area of mutual interest - companies and gov-ernments may find it in their self-interest to share them widely or cooperate on them, even with competitors. This report highlights several further examples of these areas, but does not highlight every example explicitly. The research areas in this category involve developing methods to study the pres-ent harmful impacts of AI systems and forecast their potential future risks .
发挥预警或红线的作用。例如，如果发现某个系统能够为恶意用户实施网络攻击提供实质性帮助，这就可能被视为不可接受的风险。风险评估还为缓解风险所需的更安全的开发实践（第 2 节）和控制实践（第 3 节）提供依据。精心界定的风险阈值是本报告所列潜在共同利益领域的第一个例子——企业和政府可能会发现，广泛共享这些阈值或围绕其开展合作符合自身利益，即使合作对象是竞争对手也是如此。本报告还重点介绍了这些领域的其他若干例子，但并未逐一明确标示所有例子。这一类别的研究领域涉及开发方法，以研究 AI 系统当前造成的有害影响，并预测其未来可能带来的风险。
1.1 System Evaluations1.1 系统评估
> Updates: The AI auditing field is developing but still immature. New benchmarks for AI model capabilities are regularly introduced, improved on, gamed, and saturated. Some models have recently begun to exhibit forms of evaluation awareness and capabilities that could enable acute forms of misuse.
> 最新进展：AI 审计领域正在发展，但仍不成熟。用于评估 AI 模型能力的新基准不断被提出，其上的模型表现不断提升，同时这些基准也不断被钻空子，并趋于饱和。一些模型最近开始表现出某些形式的评估意识，以及可能被用于造成严重危害的滥用能力。
> Advantages: Evaluations of model capabilities are almost ubiquitous (though with greatly varying levels of rigor), and are recognized as a key pillar of AI risk management.
> 优势：模型能力评估几乎无处不在（尽管严谨程度差异很大），并被视为 AI 风险管理的关键支柱。
> Challenges: Current benchmarking and auditing methods have persistent difficulties in evaluating model properties in a way that translates to real-world settings and accurately predict their worst-case risks. Meanwhile, evaluation awareness in models erodes trust in the validity of some assessments.
> 挑战：当前的基准测试和审计方法在评估模型属性时，始终难以使评估结果适用于现实场景，并准确预测模型在最坏情况下的风险。与此同时，模型的评估意识削弱了人们对某些评估有效性的信任。
Techniques for effectively and efficiently studying safety-relevant properties of AI sys-tems are central to studying AI risks (IAISR; Birhane et al., 2024). However, it remains persistently challenging to gain a practical understanding of AI systems’ real world capabil-ities and risks based on evaluations conducted in lab settings.
有效且高效地研究 AI 系统安全相关属性的技术，是研究 AI 风险的核心（IAISR; Birhane et al., 2024）。然而，基于实验室环境中的评估，对 AI 系统在现实世界中的能力和风险形成切合实际的认识，始终是一项挑战。
Benchmarking: Benchmarks refer to standardized assessments that can be used to measure specific model or system capabilities or tendencies. For example, one popular benchmark measures a model’s capabilities in terms of how reliably it can complete soft-ware engineering tasks that take human professionals seconds (e.g. question answering), minutes (writing code functions), or hours (implementing complex protocols) to complete (Kwa et al., 2025). However, developing high-quality assessments is difficult due to gaps between evaluation settings and the real world (Raji et al., 2021, Eriksson et al., 2025 Bean et al., 2025). For example, there are consistent gaps between what benchmarks suggest AI agents are capable of and what real-world tasks they are empirically reliable for (Rabanser et al., 2026). New benchmarks for frontier AI system capabilities are regu-larly introduced (e.g. Jimenez et al., 2023; Chollet et al., 2024; Yoran et al., 2024; Phan et al., 2025; Hendrycks et al., 2025; Mazeika et al., 2025), improved on, gamed, and saturated. Sev-eral persistent challenges include: 20
基准测试：基准是指可用于衡量特定模型或系统能力或倾向的标准化评估。例如，一项广泛使用的基准通过衡量模型完成软件工程任务的可靠程度来评估其能力；这些任务由人类专业人员完成时，分别需要数秒（例如回答问题）、数分钟（编写代码函数）或数小时（实现复杂协议）（Kwa et al., 2025）。然而，由于评估环境与现实世界之间存在差距，开发高质量评估十分困难（Raji et al., 2021, Eriksson et al., 2025 Bean et al., 2025）。例如，基准所显示的 AI 智能体能力，与实证表明其能够可靠完成的现实任务之间，始终存在差距（Rabanser et al., 2026）。用于评估前沿 AI 系统能力的新基准不断被提出（例如 Jimenez et al., 2023; Chollet et al., 2024; Yoran et al., 2024; Phan et al., 2025; Hendrycks et al., 2025; Mazeika et al., 2025），其上的模型表现不断提升，同时这些基准也不断被钻空子，并趋于饱和。一些持续存在的挑战包括：20
> 1 R ISK ASSESSMENT
> 1 风险评估
Continually developing new benchmarks that offer useful signal for frontier systems. Improving benchmarking methodology to close persistent gaps between esti-mated and real-world properties (e.g. Atweh et al., 2025). Improving quality, documentation, and reproducibility of benchmarks (Reuel et al., 2024). Developing benchmarks that evaluate the safety properties of AI systems across many languages (Deng et al., 2024).
持续开发能够为前沿系统提供有用信号的新基准。改进基准测试方法，缩小估计属性与现实世界属性之间持续存在的差距（例如 Atweh et al., 2025）。提高基准的质量、文档完善程度和可复现性（Reuel et al., 2024）。开发能够跨多种语言评估 AI 系统安全属性的基准（Deng et al., 2024）。
Evaluation and auditing methods: Evaluations and audits, especially by third parties, are emerging as a pillar of AI risk management. They can incorporate benchmarks, qualitative testing, analysis of methodology, and other methods to formally assess safety-related properties of AI systems. In addition to scientific challenges involving measuring system properties (see also Section 2.3 - Verification), AI evaluation is an emerg-ing science and immature field. There are numerous challenges pertaining to how audits should be approached, scoped, performed, and reported on (Brundage et al., 2026) in ways that validly assess risks and offer meaningful assurance and accountability in the fron-tier AI ecosystem. As frontier AI systems are increasingly deployed with scaffolding, tools, memory, external interfaces, and long-horizon task execution, evaluation outcomes may depend substantially on the system configuration and evaluation setup. Evalua-tion reports should therefore document the system version, environment and interaction, task specification, elicitation procedure, success criteria, and assumptions used to relate observed behavior to safety-relevant risk. In the cyberphysical ecosystem, sim-ulation evaluations should also test to obtain early warnings – establishing whether general-purpose vision models could enable many actors to use and misuse autonomous drones (see e.g. Liu et al., 2023; Meta, 2026).
评估与审计方法：评估和审计，尤其是由第三方开展的评估和审计，正逐渐成为 AI 风险管理的一大支柱。它们可以结合基准测试、定性测试、方法分析及其他方法，对 AI 系统的安全相关属性进行正式评估。除了衡量系统属性所涉及的科学挑战（另见第 2.3 节——验证）之外，AI 评估本身还是一门新兴科学，也是一个尚不成熟的领域。在如何规划审计、界定审计范围、执行审计和报告审计结果（Brundage et al., 2026），以有效评估风险，并在前沿 AI 生态系统中提供有意义的保障和问责方面，存在诸多挑战。随着前沿 AI 系统在部署时越来越多地配备支撑框架（scaffolding）、工具、记忆、外部接口和长时程任务执行功能，评估结果可能在很大程度上取决于系统配置和评估设置。因此，评估报告应记录系统版本、环境与交互方式、任务规范、能力诱发程序、成功标准，以及用于将观察到的行为与安全相关风险联系起来的假设。在信息物理生态系统中，仿真评估还应开展测试以获取早期预警——确定通用视觉模型是否可能使众多行为主体具备使用和滥用自主无人机的能力（例如参见 Liu et al., 2023; Meta, 2026）。
Managing evaluation awareness: In the past year, some frontier AI systems have exhibited abilities to recognize when they are being evaluated and condition their behavior on noticing this (IAISR 2026). For example, in third-party evaluations of Claude 4.6, Apollo Research noticed that the model exhibited high levels of verbal-ized evaluation awareness and concluded that its evaluations could not offer reliable evidence of actual alignment (Anthropic, 2026). Other recent work on evaluation faking studies this phenomenon as an observer effect in AI safety evaluation, where AI systems may autonomously alter their behavior after recognizing the presence of an eval-uation context, thereby affecting the validity of evaluation results (Fan et al., 2025; Li et al., 2026). It is not clear how reliably future AI systems could be able to infer when they are in well-designed evaluations. However, it is clear that evaluation awareness has confounded the validity of some existing evaluation procedures and that future evaluations must be designed with it in mind. Reducing cues that reveal when a system is being evaluated, developing evaluations that are harder to game, monitoring for suspicious behavior during evaluation, and designing controls that are difficult for future systems to evade offer possible solutions (Duan et al., 2026). 21
应对评估意识：过去一年中，一些前沿 AI 系统表现出识别自身何时正在接受评估，并在察觉这一点后相应调整行为的能力（IAISR 2026）。例如，在对 Claude 4.6 进行第三方评估时，Apollo Research 注意到，该模型频繁通过语言表达出对自身正在接受评估的意识，并据此得出结论：其评估无法为模型的实际对齐状况提供可靠证据（Anthropic, 2026）。近期关于评估伪装（evaluation faking）的其他研究将这一现象视为 AI 安全评估中的观察者效应：AI 系统在识别到自身处于评估情境后，可能会自主改变行为，从而影响评估结果的有效性 (Fan et al., 2025; Li et al., 2026)。目前尚不清楚，未来的 AI 系统能够多么可靠地推断出自身何时处于精心设计的评估之中。然而，明确的是，评估意识已经干扰了一些现有评估程序的有效性，因此未来的评估设计必须将其纳入考虑。可能的解决方案包括：减少暴露系统正在接受评估的线索，开发更难被钻空子的评估方法，监测评估过程中的可疑行为，以及设计未来系统难以规避的管控措施 (Duan et al., 2026)。21
> 1 R ISK ASSESSMENT
> 1 风险评估
Dangerous capability and propensity assessment: To assess certain hazards posed by an AI system, it is necessary to elicit and assess potentially dangerous capabilities (Phuong et al., 2024; Shevlane et al., 2023; IAISR) including cyber, chemical, biological, radiological, and nuclear knowledge, as well as capabilities related to psychologi-cal manipulation, deception, AI research and development, and unconstrained autonomy. For example, researchers have found that frontier LLMs have surpassed PhD-level experts in their ability to assist in developing virology lab protocols (Götting et al., 2025) and that frontier AI models like Claude Mythos are increasingly capable of autonomously execute cyberattacks (Anthropic, 2026). Some AI developers have warned that, based on their evaluations, they have not been able to rule out the possibility of harmful bio, cyber, or chemical capabilities in their models (e.g., Google, 2025; Anthropic, 2025; OpenAI, 2025). To assess the likelihood that these capabilities will cause harm, it is also useful to assess a system’s propensities to use them. However, the science of evaluating the propensities and capabilities of frontier AI systems is not fully mature (Apollo, 2024; Reuel et al., 2024). Rigorously assessing them is challenging because frontier AI systems’ capabilities are broad and context-dependent. Unexpected propensities, capabilities, or limitations are often discovered after a system is developed and deployed (IAISR). For example, a recent large-scale competition identified over 60,000 exploits against the safety guard-rails of 19 different production AI systems (Zou et al., 2025). In general, current tests are not yet sufficient to rule out a given harmful capability or behaviour. Frontiers for addi-tional research include methods to more reliably elicit specific harmful model capabilities and propensities,methods for inferring the existence of rare or suppressed system capabilities that may be difficult to elicit in lab settings, and developing practi-cal thresholds for determining when a model’s capabilities pose unacceptable levels of risks in a certain use case. In addition to being an area of shared global interest, work-ing to improve evaluations of dangerous AI capabilities may be critical on the timescale of several months.
危险能力与行为倾向评估：为了评估 AI 系统带来的某些危害，有必要诱发并评估其潜在的危险能力 (Phuong et al., 2024; Shevlane et al., 2023; IAISR)，包括网络、化学、生物、放射性和核领域的知识，以及与心理操纵、欺骗、AI 研发和不受约束的自主性相关的能力。例如，研究人员发现，前沿 LLM 在协助制定病毒学实验室操作规程方面的能力已经超过博士水平的专家 (Götting et al., 2025)，而 Claude Mythos 等前沿 AI 模型自主实施网络攻击的能力也日益增强 (Anthropic, 2026)。一些 AI 开发者警告称，根据其评估，他们尚无法排除模型具备有害生物、网络或化学能力的可能性（例如，Google, 2025; Anthropic, 2025; OpenAI, 2025）。为了评估这些能力造成危害的可能性，评估系统使用这些能力的倾向也很有帮助。然而，评估前沿 AI 系统行为倾向与能力的科学尚未完全成熟 (Apollo, 2024; Reuel et al., 2024)。由于前沿 AI 系统的能力范围广泛且依赖具体情境，对其进行严格评估具有挑战性。出乎预料的行为倾向、能力或局限，往往在系统开发并部署之后才被发现 (IAISR)。例如，近期一项大规模竞赛发现了针对 19 个不同的生产环境 AI 系统安全防护机制的 60,000 多种漏洞利用方式 (Zou et al., 2025)。总体而言，当前的测试尚不足以排除某种特定有害能力或行为的存在。有待进一步研究的前沿方向包括：更可靠地诱发模型特定有害能力与行为倾向的方法，推断可能难以在实验室环境中诱发的罕见或受抑制系统能力是否存在的方法，以及制定实用阈值，以确定模型能力何时会在特定使用场景中带来不可接受的风险水平。改进危险 AI 能力评估的工作不仅涉及全球共同利益，而且在未来数月的时间尺度上也可能至关重要。
Estimating worst-case risks: Typically, the worst behaviors identified in an assess-ment of AI safety can only serve as a lower bound for a model’s worst possible case harms. This makes most AI risk evaluations conservatively biased toward underestimating worst case risks, sometimes with significant consequences. For example in April of 2025, OpenAI’s evaluations reportedly failed to identify excessive levels of sycophancy in GPT-4o which led it to affirm unhealthy behaviors in some users, including self-harm (OpenAI, 2025). Some work has aimed to reduce the conservative bias of evaluations by evaluating “helpful-only” versions of models without refusal-based safeguards (Ko et al., 2025), allowing models access to tools, or evaluating models under minor modifications to their internal weights and activations (Che et al., 2025). Nonetheless, high-confi-dence estimations and assurances related to a model’s worst-case risks are not within the capabilities of established evaluation methods. 22
最坏情况风险估计：通常，AI 安全评估中发现的最恶劣行为，只能作为模型在最坏情况下可能造成的危害的下界。这使得大多数 AI 风险评估存在保守偏差，倾向于低估最坏情况风险，有时会造成严重后果。例如，据报道，OpenAI 在 2025 年 4 月的评估未能发现 GPT-4o 过度迎合的问题，导致该模型对一些用户的不健康行为表示认同，其中包括自我伤害行为 (OpenAI, 2025)。一些研究尝试通过评估不含拒绝式安全防护机制的“仅提供帮助”（“helpful-only”）模型版本 (Ko et al., 2025)、允许模型使用工具，或在对模型内部权重和激活值进行轻微修改的条件下开展评估 (Che et al., 2025)，来减轻评估的保守偏差。尽管如此，现有成熟评估方法仍无法对模型的最坏情况风险作出高置信度的估计并提供相关保证。22
> 1 R ISK ASSESSMENT
> 1 风险评估
1.2 System safety assessment1.2 系统安全评估
Updates: Ongoing efforts to improve organizational and system safety, coupled with trial and error, are contributing to an emerging understanding of how AI systems and organizations can be run to effectively manage risks.
进展：持续改进组织安全与系统安全的努力，加上试错实践，正逐步增进人们对如何运行 AI 系统和管理组织以有效管控风险的理解。
Advantages: Most safety-related failures are not merely due to system design flaws, but also stem from problems with risk management frameworks for identifying and addressing risks.
优势：大多数与安全相关的失效并非仅由系统设计缺陷造成，也源于用于识别和应对风险的风险管理框架存在问题。
Challenges: Not all AI-deploying organizations have effective systems safety measures in place, and organizational safety audits are not common. AI safety is not just about individual systems, but also their interaction with users, tools, and the rest of the world. For example, when an AI company discovers concerning behaviour from their system, the resulting risks depend, in large part, on what pro-cesses are in place to identify and respond to the risks. For example, a company might quickly escalate and work to mitigate the risk. Or they might downplay it in order to focus on other priorities. System safety considers both AI systems and the broader context that they are deployed in. The study of system safety focuses on the inter-actions between different technical components as well as processes and incentives in an organisation (IAISR, Hendrycks, 2024; Alaga et al., 2024; Schuett et al., 2024; Żywiołek et al., 2025). For example, some AI companies have recently revised their safety frame-works when prior risk thresholds have been crossed or when regulations take effect. The practice of system safety engineering has a long history in areas such as aircraft flight control and nuclear reactor control (Dekker, 2019; Rismani et al., 2023). System safety assess-ments evaluate if a critical system continues to function as intended even under human error, insider threats, or the failure of individual technical components. In AI sys-tems safety assessments, this includes analysing how AI deployments might interact with existing social, economic, and political structures to create emergent down-stream risks that individual system evaluations might miss (Weidinger et al., 2023), as well as analysing risks that emerge from multiple AI systems and humans interacting with each other. Currently, system- and organizational-safety audits are not common in the AI ecosystem.
挑战：并非所有部署 AI 的组织都已落实有效的系统安全措施，而且组织安全审计并不普遍。AI 安全不仅涉及单个系统，还涉及系统与用户、工具及外部世界的交互。例如，当一家 AI 公司发现其系统存在令人担忧的行为时，由此产生的风险在很大程度上取决于该公司建立了哪些识别和应对风险的流程。例如，公司可能会迅速将问题上报，并采取措施缓解风险。或者，公司可能会淡化这一问题，以便专注于其他优先事项。系统安全既考虑 AI 系统本身，也考虑其部署所处的更广泛背景。系统安全研究关注不同技术组件之间的相互作用，以及组织内部的流程和激励机制 (IAISR, Hendrycks, 2024; Alaga et al., 2024; Schuett et al., 2024; Żywiołek et al., 2025)。例如，一些 AI 公司近期在先前设定的风险阈值被突破或法规生效时，修订了其安全框架。系统安全工程实践在飞机飞行控制和核反应堆控制等领域有着悠久的历史 (Dekker, 2019; Rismani et al., 2023)。系统安全评估考察关键系统即使在出现人为错误、内部人员威胁或个别技术组件失效的情况下，是否仍能按预期运行。在 AI 系统安全评估中，这包括分析 AI 的部署可能如何与现有社会、经济和政治结构相互作用，产生单个系统评估可能遗漏的涌现性下游风险 (Weidinger et al., 2023)，以及分析多个 AI 系统与人类彼此互动所产生的风险。目前，系统安全审计和组织安全审计在 AI 生态系统中尚不普遍。
1.3 downstream impact assessment, risk analysis and prediction1.3 下游影响评估、风险分析与预测
Updates: The interdisciplinary science of evaluating AI’s impacts on society is emerging. This science is facilitated, in part, by empirical academic research, AI company reporting on usage, and analysis of incidents.
最新进展：评估 AI 对社会影响的跨学科科学正在兴起。学术界的实证研究、AI 公司发布的使用情况报告以及事件分析，在一定程度上推动了这门科学的发展。
Advantages: Downstream impact assessments offer the most direct ways to study emerging AI impacts and risks.
优势：下游影响评估提供了研究新出现的 AI 影响和风险的最直接方法。
Challenges: Downstream consequences are diffuse and systemic, making it fundamentally challenging to characterize AI impacts and risks with high confidence. 23
挑战：下游后果具有弥散性和系统性，因此，要以高度置信度刻画 AI 的影响和风险，存在根本性的困难。23
> 1 R ISK ASSESSMENT
> 1 风险评估
AI is poised to have enormous impacts on human economic, social, and political life. Assessing and forecasting the many societal impacts of AI systems is one of the central goals of risk assessments. However, it is also very challenging and greatly under-emphasized in current research due to its inherent prospective and sys-temic nature (Weidinger et al., 2023 Solaiman et al., 2023). Studying global, societal impacts of AI also requires nuanced analysis, inclusion, and consideration of under-repre-sented people, including the estimated 6.8 billion humans who do not use generative AI (GAAN, 2026) and the estimated 3.7 billion humans who do not use the internet (UN, 2026). Research on forecasting involves taxonomising risk (Slattery et al., 2024), studying usage data, analysing trends, risk modelling, predicting progress in AI capabilities, develop-ing models of AI’s future impacts, and updating forecasts in response to findings from field tests and usage data. For example, recent research has shown that analysis of posts on the r/ChatGPT subreddit showed sharp increases in discussions of attachment and emo-tional dependence nearly six months before mainstream media journalists broke news on the phenomenon (Dai et al., 2026). This research also plays an important role in inform-ing which evaluations and audits are needed for valid assessments of likely and severe risk scenarios. Because of the complexities involved in the study of downstream soci-etal impacts, continued work to thoroughly monitor and study them will require nuanced analysis, interdisciplinarity, and inclusion (Wallach et al., 2024).
AI 即将对人类的经济、社会和政治生活产生巨大影响。评估和预测 AI 系统对社会的诸多影响，是风险评估的核心目标之一。然而，由于这项工作本身具有前瞻性和系统性，它也极具挑战性，并且在当前研究中远未得到足够重视 (Weidinger et al., 2023 Solaiman et al., 2023)。研究 AI 在全球范围内的社会影响，还需要细致入微的分析，并将代表性不足的人群纳入研究、考虑他们的处境，其中包括估计有 6.8 十亿不使用生成式 AI 的人 (GAAN, 2026)，以及估计有 3.7 十亿不使用互联网的人 (UN, 2026)。预测研究涉及对风险进行分类 (Slattery et al., 2024)、研究使用数据、分析趋势、建立风险模型、预测 AI 能力的进展、构建 AI 未来影响的模型，以及根据实地测试的发现和使用数据更新预测。例如，近期研究发现，对 r/ChatGPT 子版块帖子的分析显示，有关依恋和情感依赖的讨论急剧增加，比主流媒体记者首次报道这一现象早了近六个月 (Dai et al., 2026)。这类研究还发挥着重要作用，有助于确定需要开展哪些评估和审计，才能有效评估可能发生及后果严重的风险情景。由于下游社会影响研究涉及诸多复杂因素，持续、全面地监测和研究这些影响，需要细致入微的分析、跨学科合作与包容性参与 (Wallach et al., 2024)。
Field tests: Field tests and human participant studies aim to assess the real-world impacts of AI systems. They include analysing current impacts on topics such as deepfakes, labour, inequality, market concentration, misinformation, polarisa-tion, privacy, mental health, and education. For example, recent work has estimated that photorealistic AI deepfake videos appearing to depict child sexual abuse material rose over 26,000% between 2024 and 2025 (IWF, 2026) and that 1.2 million children across 11 countries disclosed having their images manipulated into sexually explicit deepfakes in the past year (Unicef, 2026). Concurrently, researchers at Anthropic have recently published details and analysis related to how AI systems may be impacting labor markets (Anthropic, 2026). Developers also sometimes “beta test” models or start “bug bounty programs” (e.g. Anthropic, 2024) to incentivise users to find and report vulnerabilities so that they can be fixed. One kind of field test that is particularly relevant to malicious use risks is “uplift studies” (Bateman et al., 2024). Uplift studies aim to assess how much an AI system can help users with a task (e.g. performing cyberattacks) relative to users without access to that system. For example, some AI labs have tested if using LLMs uplifts humans’ abilities to plan biological attacks (Anthropic, 2026; Zhou Hong et al. 2026). Field tests, com-bined with other usage data can also study questions such as how an AI system affects the mental health of users. As in the field of clinical drug trials, field tests may start with limited, controlled tests, and then gradually expand to real-world contexts to uncover new risks and side effects.
实地测试：实地测试和涉及人类参与者的研究旨在评估 AI 系统在现实世界中的影响。这些研究包括分析 AI 目前对深度伪造、劳动、不平等、市场集中度、错误信息、两极分化、隐私、心理健康和教育等方面的影响。例如，近期研究估计，2024 至 2025 年间，看似呈现儿童性虐待内容的照片级逼真 AI 深度伪造视频增加了超过 26,000% (IWF, 2026)，而在 11 个国家，共有 1.2 百万名儿童透露，他们的图像在过去一年中被篡改为露骨色情的深度伪造内容 (Unicef, 2026)。与此同时，Anthropic 的研究人员近期发表了有关 AI 系统可能如何影响劳动力市场的详细资料和分析 (Anthropic, 2026)。开发者有时也会对模型进行“贝塔测试”，或启动“漏洞赏金计划”（例如 Anthropic, 2024），以激励用户发现并报告漏洞，从而加以修复。与恶意使用风险尤为相关的一类实地测试是“能力提升研究”（uplift studies）(Bateman et al., 2024)。能力提升研究（uplift studies）旨在评估：与无法使用某一 AI 系统的用户相比，该系统能在多大程度上帮助用户完成任务（例如实施网络攻击）。例如，一些 AI 实验室已测试使用 LLMs 是否会提升人类策划生物攻击的能力（Anthropic, 2026; Zhou Hong et al. 2026）。实地测试结合其他使用数据，还可以研究 AI 系统如何影响用户心理健康等问题。与临床药物试验领域一样，实地测试可以从规模有限、条件受控的测试开始，然后逐步扩展到现实情境，以发现新的风险和副作用。
Prospective risk analysis and structured analytical techniques: The International AI Safety Report (IAISR) highlights an ‘evidence dilemma’ for emerging AI risks. On the one hand, early mitigations for emerging risks can turn out to be unnecessary or ineffec-tive. On the other hand, waiting for clear evidence of a risk before mitigating it can lead 24
前瞻性风险分析与结构化分析技术：《国际 AI 安全报告》（IAISR）强调了新兴 AI 风险所面临的“证据困境”。一方面，针对新兴风险采取的早期缓解措施最终可能被证明没有必要或没有效果。另一方面，等到有明确证据表明风险存在后才采取缓解措施，可能会导致 24
> 1 R ISK ASSESSMENT
> 1 风险评估
to systematic neglect of certain risks (Casper et al., 2025). To navigate this dilemma, trans-parency infrastructure and early risk assessment are key. When assessing risks that have not yet occurred, or risks that may take a variety of forms (e.g. cyber attacks), it is often necessary to use prospective risk analysis and structured analytical techniques. These techniques are often used outside the field of AI, e.g. in nuclear safety, cybersecu-rity, or aircraft flight control. They have also been crucial in historical debates, e.g. over the health impacts of ozone depletion and smoking. Nonetheless, they are not yet widely used in AI risk assessment (IAISR, Murray, 2025). Structured risk assessments are also needed to combine evidence in order to construct a safety case , used by an AI developer to model risks, state assumptions, and convincingly argue that their system is safe (Clymer, 2024; Buhl et al., 2024; Habli et al., 2025; Hilton et al., 2025). This requires assessing the full life cycle and the full stack of safety techniques used, as well as an assessment of the systemic interaction between components and the outside world (see 1.6).
某些风险被系统性地忽视（Casper et al., 2025）。要应对这一困境，透明度基础设施和早期风险评估至关重要。在评估尚未发生的风险或可能以多种形式出现的风险（例如网络攻击）时，通常有必要使用前瞻性风险分析与结构化分析技术。这些技术经常用于 AI 以外的领域，例如核安全、网络安全或飞机飞行控制。它们在历史上的一些争论中也发挥了关键作用，例如关于臭氧层损耗和吸烟对健康影响的争论。然而，它们尚未在 AI 风险评估中得到广泛应用（IAISR, Murray, 2025）。还需要通过结构化风险评估来整合证据，以构建安全论证（safety case），供 AI 开发者用于风险建模、陈述假设，并有说服力地论证其系统是安全的（Clymer, 2024; Buhl et al., 2024; Habli et al., 2025; Hilton et al., 2025）。这需要评估整个生命周期和所采用的全套安全技术，并评估各组件之间以及组件与外部世界之间的系统性交互（见 1.6）。
1.4 Loss-of-control risk assessment1.4 失控风险评估
> Updates: Emerging research has characterized control-undermining behaviours in AI systems, agents with high levels of cyber autonomy, and systemic loss of control risks.
> 最新进展：新近研究已对 AI 系统中破坏控制的行为、具有高度网络自主性的智能体，以及系统性失控风险进行了刻画。
> Advantages: Loss-of-control risks are increasingly able to be studied in terms of model propensities, model capabilities, and empirical trends.
> 优势：越来越能够从模型倾向、模型能力和实证趋势的角度研究失控风险。
> Challenges: Despite increasing concreteness, loss-of-control risk assessment is much more of a predictive pursuit than an empirical science. Loss-of-control scenarios are diverse and can range from being driven by a single to many AI systems and from being accidental to intentional.
> 挑战：尽管失控风险评估日益具体化，但它在很大程度上仍是一项预测性工作，而非一门实证科学。失控情景多种多样，既可能由单个 AI 系统驱动，也可能由多个 AI 系统驱动；既可能出于意外，也可能出于故意。
Loss of control refers to scenarios where advanced AI systems come to operate outside of human control, with no clear path to regaining control. This includes both scenarios that involve passively ceding control and scenarios that involve AI systems actively under-mining control measures in pursuit of their own goals. Assessing this risk depends partly on assessing and forecasting AI’s control-un-dermining capabilities. These include AI agency (autonomous action and planning), oversight evasion, persuasion, autonomously earning or seizing financial and com-puting resources, conducting cyber attacks, as well as AI research and development (IAISR). Assessments of control loss risk also focus on understanding propensities –how often and why AI systems use control-undermining capabilities. Evidence for all of the above control-undermining capabilities is growing but current capabilities remain insufficient to allow a loss of control (IAISR). However, there is evidence of today’s AI systems using their limited control undermining capabilities in certain scenarios, e.g. to avoid being replaced (IAISR; Greenblatt et al., 2024; Baker et al., 2025; Lynch et al., 2025). Recent empirical work suggests that loss-of-control risk assessment can be made more concrete by tracking precursor capabilities by examining autonomous replication, shutdown resistance, self-proliferation dynamics, and scaffold self-improvement in security-relevant settings (Black et al., 2025; Pan et al., 2024; Pan et al., 2025; Air et al., 2026 a; Schlatter et al., 2026 b; Hong et al., 2026; Fan et al., 2026). These results offer early 25
失控是指先进 AI 系统开始在人类控制之外运行，且没有明确途径重新获得控制的情景。这既包括人类被动让出控制权的情景，也包括 AI 系统为追求自身目标而主动破坏控制措施的情景。评估这一风险，部分取决于对 AI 破坏控制的能力进行评估和预测。这些能力包括 AI 的能动性（自主行动和规划）、规避监督、说服、自主赚取或夺取资金和计算资源、实施网络攻击，以及 AI 研究与开发（IAISR）。失控风险评估还着重于理解行为倾向——AI 系统多频繁地使用破坏控制的能力，以及为何使用这些能力。支持上述所有破坏控制能力的证据正在增多，但当前能力仍不足以导致失控（IAISR）。然而，有证据表明，当今的 AI 系统会在某些情景中使用其有限的破坏控制能力，例如为了避免被替换（IAISR; Greenblatt et al., 2024; Baker et al., 2025; Lynch et al., 2025）。近期实证研究表明，可以通过考察安全相关情境中的自主复制、抗拒关闭、自我扩散动态和支架自我改进，追踪失控的前兆能力，从而使失控风险评估更加具体（Black et al., 2025; Pan et al., 2024; Pan et al., 2025; Air et al., 2026 a; Schlatter et al., 2026 b; Hong et al., 2026; Fan et al., 2026）。这些结果提供了早期的 25
> 1 R ISK ASSESSMENT
> 1 风险评估
evidence about specific component capabilities, rather than evidence that current systems can cause full loss of control. These demonstrations show that assessments can move beyond purely speculative scenario analysis by measuring how specific control-un-dermining capabilities emerge under particular model, scaffold, environment, task, and budget conditions. Despite the dangers posed by control-undermining behavior from AI systems, loss of control could also occur via deliberate decisions by humans to release highly agentic AI sys-tems. Some early signs suggest that highly autonomous, general-purpose, goal-directed AI systems are an imminent concern. For example, projects such as Conway and Moltbook demonstrate that existing agents can achieve a high degree of goal-directed autonomy, even earning money via cryptocurrency transfers to keep themselves running. As a result, there are now credible fears in the scientific community that some AI systems may soon become goal-oriented invasive species in cyberspace. This makes it a pressing research priority to prepare guardrails and security mitigations for increasingly powerful and numerous AI agents in cyberspace. For example, systems with frontier-lev-els of cyber offense capabilities, such as Claude Mythos (Anthropic, 2026) should be continuously studied and monitored for risks that they could power fully cyber-autono-mous agents. Loss of control events could further come about via gradual, systemic changes in which AI systems gradually come to accrue more economic, cultural, and governmental influence (Kulveit et al., 2025). This suggests value in understanding gradual disempower-ment dynamics and having informed conversations in the science and governance spheres about how to maintain meaningful human control over institutions.
关于具体组成能力的证据，而非表明当前系统能够导致完全失控的证据。这些演示表明，通过衡量特定的破坏控制能力如何在特定模型、支架、环境、任务和预算条件下涌现，评估可以超越纯粹推测性的情景分析。尽管 AI 系统破坏控制的行为会带来危险，但人类有意决定发布具有高度能动性的 AI 系统，也可能导致失控。一些早期迹象表明，高度自主、通用且以目标为导向的 AI 系统正成为迫在眉睫的隐忧。例如，Conway 和 Moltbook 等项目表明，现有智能体能够实现高度的目标导向自主性，甚至通过加密货币转账获得收入，以维持自身运行。因此，科学界如今有充分理由担忧，某些 AI 系统可能很快就会成为网络空间中以目标为导向的入侵物种。这使得为网络空间中能力日益强大、数量不断增加的 AI 智能体建立防护机制和安全缓解措施，成为一项紧迫的研究重点。例如，应持续研究和监测具有前沿水平网络攻击能力的系统，如 Claude Mythos (Anthropic, 2026)，以评估它们可能为具备完全网络自主性的智能体提供能力支持的风险。失控事件还可能通过渐进的系统性变化而发生，在此过程中，AI 系统逐步积累更大的经济、文化和政府事务影响力 (Kulveit et al., 2025)。这表明，理解人类逐步丧失权能的动态过程，并在科学和治理领域围绕如何维持人类对各类机构的实质性控制开展知情讨论，具有重要价值。
1.5 Secure evaluation infrastructure1.5 安全评估基础设施
> Updates: There is a solidified academic consensus that deeper forms of model and organizational access allow for greater levels of third-party scrutiny and that these forms of access can be facilitated by secure evaluation infrastructure and procedures.
> 最新进展：学术界已形成稳固共识，即更深入的模型访问和组织访问能够支持更高程度的第三方审查，而安全的评估基础设施和程序可以促进此类访问。
> Advantages: Secure evaluation infrastructure and protocols can allow for more meaningful forms of external scrutiny with limited tradeoffs to security.
> 优势：安全的评估基础设施和规程能够支持更具实质意义的外部审查，同时仅需在安全性方面作出有限让步。
> Challenges: Some tradeoffs exist between scrutiny, security, and efficiency. There is also an implementation gap between what types of secure auditing infrastructure is possible and what is implemented.
> 挑战：审查、安全性和效率之间存在一定的权衡。在技术上可行的安全审计基础设施类型与实际部署的类型之间，也存在实施差距。
Independent third-party AI testing and assurance providers play an important role in translating AI safety research into repeatable evaluation methods, objective testing reports, certification schemes, and practical risk management support for industry. As AI systems become more agentic and high-impact, third-party evaluation can help vali-date developer claims, support procurement decisions, and increase public trust. External auditors and oversight bodies need infrastructure and protocols that enable thorough evaluation and verification of system properties while protecting sen-sitive intellectual property. Ideally, it could be possible for evaluation infrastructure to 26
独立第三方 AI 测试与保障服务提供方在将 AI 安全研究转化为可重复的评估方法、客观的测试报告、认证方案以及面向行业的实用风险管理支持方面，发挥着重要作用。随着 AI 系统的智能体特性日益增强、影响日益扩大，第三方评估有助于核实开发者的声明、支持采购决策，并增强公众信任。外部审计人员和监督机构需要能够在保护敏感知识产权的同时，对系统属性进行全面评估和验证的基础设施与规程。理想情况下，评估基础设施或许能够 26

enable double-blindness: the evaluator’s inability to directly access the system’s parameters and developers’ inability to know what exact evaluations are run (Reuel et al., 2024; Buck-nall et al., 2025; Casper et al., 2024; Charnock et al., 2026). Meanwhile, the importance of mutual security will continue to grow as system capabilities and risks increase. Methods for developing secure infrastructure for auditing and oversight are known to be possible. However, open challenges include determining what level of access is appropriate for which evaluations and conducting the engineering work of designing, building, and integrating efficient infrastructure (Brundage et al., 2026). Further research should also explore how audit results can be effectively and reliably incorporated into risk management and decision-making frameworks. 27
实现双盲：评估者无法直接访问系统参数，开发者也无法知道具体开展了哪些评估 (Reuel et al., 2024; Buck-nall et al., 2025; Casper et al., 2024; Charnock et al., 2026)。与此同时，随着系统能力和风险的增加，保障双方安全的重要性将持续上升。目前已知，开发用于审计和监督的安全基础设施在方法上是可行的。然而，尚待解决的挑战包括确定各类评估适合采用何种访问级别，以及开展高效基础设施的设计、构建和集成等工程工作 (Brundage et al., 2026)。进一步的研究还应探索如何将审计结果有效、可靠地纳入风险管理和决策框架。27

Key information and updates关键信息与最新进展
Since ISE 2025, several new developments are important to address. This summary lists research priorities that are particularly important given these new developments.
自 ISE 2025 以来，出现了几项需要重点关注的新进展。本摘要列出了鉴于这些新进展而尤为重要的优先研究事项。
Some recent incidents have likely been preventable with existing methods. Some new AI incidents since SCAI 2025 (see Risk assessment) likely could have been prevented with established safety methods based on prompting, fine-tuning, red-teaming, and monitoring. Research priorities: Understanding the internal organizational processes that lead to absent or insufficient application of safeguards. Understanding how to develop better processes and systems incentives. Avenues include developing internal systems safety measures, rapid model auditing under time pressure, and transparency infrastructure. 28
近期的一些事件很可能本可通过现有方法加以预防。自 SCAI 2025 以来发生的一些新 AI 事件（参见“风险评估”），很可能本可通过基于提示、微调、红队测试和监测的成熟安全方法加以预防。研究重点：理解导致防护措施未被采用或采用不足的组织内部流程。理解如何制定更完善的流程和系统层面的激励机制。可探索的途径包括制定内部系统安全措施、在时间压力下开展快速模型审计，以及建设透明度基础设施。28

> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
Open-weight models are now trailing closely behind the capabilities of frontier closed models—enabling research but lacking effective misuse safeguards. According to common benchmarks, open weight models are estimated to be 3 to 12 months behind the frontier (varying across capabilities). Developers of frontier closed models have recently designated these closed models as ‘High’ risk for cyber and biological misuse, and added safeguards against misuse. Similarly, most closed models from reputable developers refuse nudification and CSAM user requests. However, open-weight models cannot be effectively safeguarded in these ways.
开放权重模型的能力如今已紧随前沿闭源模型之后，既为研究提供了支持，也缺乏有效的滥用防护措施。根据常用基准测试，估计开放权重模型落后于前沿水平 3 至 12 个月（因能力类别而异）。前沿闭源模型的开发者近期已将这些闭源模型的网络和生物领域滥用风险评定为“高”，并增设了防范滥用的措施。同样，信誉良好的开发者所推出的大多数闭源模型都会拒绝用户提出的生成裸体化图像及儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）的请求。然而，无法通过这些方式有效地保障开放权重模型的安全。
Research priorities: Advancing model-level protections against open model misuse (see Section 2.2.5). Advancing societal resilience against attempted biological, chemical, and cyber attacks as well as CSAM creation (see Section 4).
研究重点：推进模型层面的防护措施，以防止开放模型被滥用（见第 2.2.5 节）。增强社会应对生物、化学和网络攻击企图以及儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）制作的韧性（见第 4 节）。
Autonomous AI Agents are increasingly capable and widely deployed, but their increased risks deter adoption. Users can now easily access AI agents that can control their browsers and whole computers, taking actions on the web, often with broad access to private storage or email accounts. Agents can be unreliable, take harmful actions, and can be “hijacked” by fraudsters. Ensuring agent safety and security has become a key bottleneck to AI adoption. Therefore, this ISE 2026 has an additional focus on risk management practices for agents covered in the Companion Report.
自主 AI 智能体的能力日益增强，部署日益广泛，但其不断增加的风险阻碍了人们采用它们。如今，用户可以轻松使用能够控制其浏览器乃至整台计算机的 AI 智能体，在网络上执行操作，而这些智能体通常拥有对私人存储空间或电子邮件账户的广泛访问权限。智能体可能不可靠、采取有害行动，还可能被诈骗者“劫持”。确保智能体的安全性与安保已成为 AI 应用推广的关键瓶颈。因此，本次 ISE 2026 还特别关注配套报告所涵盖的智能体风险管理实践。
Research priorities: Improving real-time agent monitoring and control methods (see Section 3) and safe agent design methodology (see Section 2.2.6). Research also needs to determine shared best practices for agent risk management. Emerging risk management practices for agents are therefore covered in detail in the Companion Report on Agentic Risk Management. Frontier AI systems are persistently vulnerable to “jailbreaks” and other methods to make them automate harmful tasks. Recent findings have shown that efforts from developers to safeguard their systems against user-attempts to elicit harmful behavior or information are useful. However, frontier models are still persistently able to perform harmful tasks, such as providing instructions for performing crimes or automating the production of hate speech, when prompted in certain ways by users. One study from 2025 crowd-sourced over 60,000 successful exploits against 19 production models.
研究重点：改进智能体的实时监测与控制方法（见第 3 节），以及安全智能体的设计方法（见第 2.2.6 节）。研究还需要确定智能体风险管理的共同最佳实践。因此，《智能体风险管理配套报告》详细介绍了新兴的智能体风险管理实践。前沿 AI 系统始终容易受到“越狱”及其他使其自动执行有害任务的方法的影响。近期研究结果表明，开发者为防范用户试图诱导系统产生有害行为或提供有害信息而采取的防护措施是有用的。然而，当用户以某些方式提供提示时，前沿模型仍然能够执行有害任务，例如提供实施犯罪的指导，或自动生成仇恨言论。2025 年的一项研究通过众包收集了针对 19 个已投入生产使用的模型的超过 60,000 个成功漏洞利用案例。
Research priorities: Improving and scaling algorithms for adversarial safety (see Section 2.2.3-2.2.4) and stress testing (see Section 1.1). Designing and integrating effective monitoring and control methods (see Section 3).
研究重点：改进并扩大用于对抗性安全的算法（见第 2.2.3-2.2.4 节）和压力测试（见第 1.1 节）的规模。设计并集成有效的监测与控制方法（见第 3 节）。
The research areas in this category involve developing technical methods for creating safer and more trustworthy systems. This section focuses on the system develop-ment phase, whereas Section 3 “Control: Monitoring and Intervention,” focuses on techniques used during and after deployment. It has been argued that “society will reject autonomous agents unless we have some credible means of making them safe” (Weld and Etzioni, 1994). Moti-vated by this concern, the following subsections explore methods for developing safe and trustworthy systems. We follow a classic safety engineering framework by examining: 29
这一类别的研究领域涉及开发技术方法，以构建更安全、更值得信赖的系统。本节侧重于系统开发阶段，而第 3 节“控制：监测与干预”则侧重于部署期间及部署后使用的技术。有人曾提出，“除非我们有某种可信的手段来确保自主智能体的安全，否则社会将拒绝接受它们”（Weld and Etzioni, 1994）。出于这一担忧，以下各小节将探讨开发安全且值得信赖的系统的方法。我们遵循经典的安全工程框架，考察以下问题：29
