how to specify precisely what properties we want an AI system to have, validate that these properties are desirable, design and implement the system to meet the specification, and verify that it meets its specification. The framework used in this section uses traditional engineering concepts includ-ing specification, design, and verification.
如何精确规定我们希望 AI 系统具备的属性，确认这些属性符合期望，设计并实现满足规格的系统，以及验证系统是否满足其规格。本节采用的框架运用了规格制定、设计和验证等传统工程概念。
Structure结构
AI systems that are trustworthy, reliable and secure by design give people the confidence to embrace and adopt AI innovation. Following a classic safety engineer-ing framework, the research areas in this category involves A. Specifying and validating the desired behaviour - This includes technical methods to address the complex challenges in specifying system behaviours in a way that accurately captures the desired intent without causing undesired side effects, for both single-stakeholder settings (e.g. reward hacking, scalable methods to discover specification loopholes) and multi-stakeholder settings (e.g. balanc-ing competing preferences, ethical and legal alignment). B. Designing a system that meets the specification - This covers techniques for training models - both closed and open weights - that are trustworthy (e.g. reduc-ing confabulation, increasing robustness against tampering), alternative finetuning methods to make specific precise changes to an AI system (e.g. model editing), and methods to build AI systems in a way that are guaranteed to meet their specifications (e.g. verifiable programme synthesis, world models with formal guarantees).
通过设计确保值得信赖、可靠且安全的 AI 系统，能够让人们有信心接受并采用 AI 创新成果。遵循经典的安全工程框架，这一类别的研究领域包括 A. 规定并确认期望行为——这包括用于应对系统行为规格制定中复杂挑战的技术方法，使规格能够准确表达预期意图，同时避免产生不期望的副作用，既适用于单一利益相关方情境（例如奖励黑客行为、可扩展的规格漏洞发现方法），也适用于多利益相关方情境（例如平衡相互竞争的偏好、伦理与法律对齐）。B. 设计满足规格的系统——这涵盖训练值得信赖的模型的技术，包括封闭权重和开放权重模型（例如减少虚构、增强抵御篡改的鲁棒性）；能够对 AI 系统进行特定精确修改的替代性微调方法（例如模型编辑）；以及以保证满足规格的方式构建 AI 系统的方法（例如可验证的程序合成、具有形式化保证的世界模型）。
C. Verifying that the AI system meets its specification - This entails techniques to pro-vide high-confidence assurances that an AI system is what its developers claim it to be and that it meets its specifications (e.g. formal verification), including in
C. 验证 AI 系统是否满足其规格——这涉及以高置信度保证 AI 系统确如其开发者所声称的那样且满足其规格的技术（例如形式化验证），包括在
novel contexts (e.g. robustness testing), as well as interpretability techniques to
新情境中（例如鲁棒性测试），以及用于
look into the black box to understand why the AI system behaves the way it does (e.g. mechanistic interpretability).
探查黑箱内部、理解 AI 系统为何表现出特定行为的可解释性技术（例如机理可解释性）。
Relationship to other concepts与其他概念的关系
What is alignment? The commonly used term “alignment” has many different definitions in the AI literature, not all of which are compatible (Gabriel et al., 2024). A common definition is “the process of ensuring that an AI’s goals, values, and behaviours are consistent with those intended by its human creators or operators.” However, since scientists still largely lack an understanding of what, if any, coherent “goals” or “values” today’s frontier AI systems have, current alignment research de facto focuses only on the “behaviour” part of this definition. So in practice, much current AI safety research uses a working definition of alignment as “ensuring that AI behaves as intended.”
什么是对齐？常用术语“对齐”（alignment）在 AI 文献中有许多不同的定义，而这些定义并非都相互兼容（Gabriel et al., 2024）。一个常见的定义是：“确保 AI 的目标、价值观和行为与其人类创造者或操作者的意图一致的过程。”然而，由于科学家在很大程度上仍不了解当今的前沿 AI 系统是否具有任何连贯的“目标”或“价值观”，以及它们具体是什么，当前的对齐研究事实上仅关注这一定义中的“行为”部分。因此，在实践中，当前许多 AI 安全研究将对齐的工作定义表述为“确保 AI 按预期行事”。
What is assurance ? Assurance refers to the broader process of determining if a system performs as expected. As such, providing assurance requires appropriate specification, validation, design, implementation and verification. 30
什么是保障（assurance）？保障是指确定系统是否按预期运行的更广泛过程。因此，提供保障需要适当的规格制定、确认、设计、实现和验证。30
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
What is robustness? Robust systems continue to behave as intended under a broad range of circumstances. This includes unfamiliar inputs as well as “adversarial” inputs designed to make the system fail. For example, state-of-the-art AI systems can be “jailbroken” into producing harmful text or instructions – against their developer’s intentions – when a user asks using adversarial prompting techniques.
什么是鲁棒性（robustness）？鲁棒的系统在各种情形下都能持续按预期行事。这些情形包括不熟悉的输入，以及旨在使系统失效的“对抗性”输入。例如，当用户使用对抗性提示技术提出请求时，最先进的 AI 系统可能被“越狱”，从而违背开发者的意图，生成有害文本或指令。
2.1 Specification & Validation: Defining the system’s purpose2.1 规格制定与确认：定义系统的用途
Updates: Specification and validation failures have led to real-world incidents, including ones related to sycophancy, psychosis, and suicide in AI users.
最新进展：规格制定和确认方面的失误已导致现实世界中的事件，包括与 AI 的谄媚行为以及 AI 用户的精神病性症状和自杀有关的事件。
Advantages: Ongoing research combined with analysis of incidents have led to improvements in how system specifications are developed.
优势：持续开展的研究结合对事件的分析，推动了系统规格制定方法的改进。
Challenges: There are fundamental limitations to the specification problem in both single-and multi-stakeholder settings. Validating that a given specification aligns with desired downstream societal impacts is fundamentally challenging.
挑战：无论是在单一利益相关方还是多个利益相关方的情境中，规格制定问题都存在根本性局限。确认某项给定规格是否符合预期的下游社会影响，是一项根本性的挑战。
“How do we want the system to behave?”
“我们希望系统如何行事？”
In engineering fields, specification involves defining desired system behaviour, whereas validation ensures that the specification meets the needs of the user, developer, or society. Specification and validation require confronting the complexity of defin-ing objectives in a way that captures user or societal benefit without omitting important constraints or causing undesired side effects. A key challenge for specification/valida-tion is to develop faithful methods to translate human oversight into automated systems. How can we design processes for developing proxies for humans based on human feedback and demonstrations?
在工程领域，规格制定涉及定义期望的系统行为，而确认则确保规格满足用户、开发者或社会的需求。规格制定和确认要求我们应对定义目标时的复杂性，使目标既能体现用户或社会的利益，又不会遗漏重要约束或造成不期望的副作用。规格制定与确认的一项关键挑战，是开发能够将人类监督忠实地转化为自动化系统的方法。我们如何设计流程，以便根据人类反馈和示范开发人类的代理？
Avoiding reward hacking and unintended consequences: Even in a simple set-ting with one human’s well-defined and fixed preferences, subtle mis-specifications can yield unacceptable results if the AI system optimises rigidly for the literal specifica-tion rather than the user’s true intent. For example, training a chatbot to say things that users approve of can cause it to unintentionally learn to pander to the user’s specific opinions (Sharma). This type of behavior from AI systems has led to psychosis, mental health crises in users, and even suicide (Yeung et al., 2025; Clegg, 2025; Carlbring and Andersson, 2025). Such rigid optimisation can also produce emergent behaviours that were not planned by the developer such as “reward hacking” (OpenAI. 2025), power-seeking (Ngo et al., 2022), sabotage behaviours (Bondarenko et al., 2025; Benton et al., 2024; Omo-hundro, 2018; Russell, 2019; Lynch et al., 2025), and producing misleading statements (Wen et al., 2024). One documented case showed an AI system actively identifying and exploiting vulnerabilities in how its programming work was scored, explicitly stating “let’s hack” while finding solutions that passed tests without solving the intended prob-lems (Baker et al., 2025). These challenges highlight the value of work to define and implement more reliable frameworks for specifying true human goals in the AI develop-ment process. Paradigms like “Assistance Games”, where an AI system must infer and act upon a user’s goals under uncertainty offer methods for systems to actively learn users’ 31
避免奖励黑客行为（reward hacking）和非预期后果：即使在只有一个人、且其偏好明确而固定的简单情境中，如果 AI 系统僵化地针对规格的字面表述进行优化，而非针对用户的真实意图进行优化，细微的规格错误也可能产生不可接受的结果。例如，训练聊天机器人说用户认可的话，可能使其无意中学会迎合用户的特定观点 (Sharma)。AI 系统的此类行为已导致用户出现精神病性症状、心理健康危机，甚至自杀 (Yeung et al., 2025; Clegg, 2025; Carlbring and Andersson, 2025)。这种僵化的优化还可能产生开发者未曾计划的涌现行为，例如“奖励黑客行为” (OpenAI.2025)、权力寻求 (Ngo et al., 2022)、蓄意破坏行为 (Bondarenko et al., 2025; Benton et al., 2024; Omo-hundro, 2018; Russell, 2019; Lynch et al., 2025)，以及生成误导性陈述 (Wen et al., 2024)。一个有记录的案例显示，某个 AI 系统主动识别并利用其编程工作评分方式中的漏洞，在寻找能够通过测试却未解决预期问题的方案时，明确表示“让我们钻空子” (Baker et al., 2025)。这些挑战凸显了相关工作的价值，即在 AI 开发过程中，定义并实施更可靠的框架，以明确规定人类的真实目标。“辅助博弈”（Assistance Games）等范式要求 AI 系统在不确定条件下推断用户的目标并据此行动，为系统主动学习用户的 31
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
under-specified goals (Hadfield-Menell et al., 2026; Shah et al., 2020). However, there remain unaddressed problems with how to define a user’s true goals when user preferences are malleable, constructed, or incomplete (Carroll et al., 2024; Zhi-Xuan et al., 2025).
未充分明确的目标提供了方法 (Hadfield-Menell et al., 2026; Shah et al., 2020)。然而，当用户偏好具有可塑性、是在过程中建构形成的或不完整时，如何定义用户的真实目标，仍存在尚未解决的问题 (Carroll et al., 2024; Zhi-Xuan et al., 2025)。
Defining clear boundaries for acceptable behaviour: When designing frontier AI sys-tems, it is difficult to precisely define the boundaries between acceptable and unacceptable behaviour. In an emerging best practice, model developers attempt to do so by using and publishing model “specifications” or “constitutions” (OpenAI, 2026; Anthropic, 2026). Many of these challenges stem from the dual use nature of information. For example, some biology lab protocols are useful for both benign and harmful bioengineering exper-iments. Defining acceptable behaviours is made further challenging by how some harmful tasks can be decomposed into individually-benign subtasks (e.g. Jones et al., 2024; Li et al., 2024). Effectively defining safe behavioural boundaries and ensuring that sys-tems can learn them is an ongoing challenge which requires an extensive understanding of emerging AI misuse threats. Designing safe systems will always require some trade-offs with genuinely useful capabilities, but refining our understanding of what types of AI capabilities are harmful in theory and in practice can help practitioners navigate these specification dilemmas in a way that minimizes trade-offs.
为可接受行为定义清晰的边界：在设计前沿 AI 系统时，很难精确定义可接受行为与不可接受行为之间的边界。一种正在形成的最佳实践是，模型开发者尝试通过使用和发布模型“规格”或“宪法”来界定这些边界 (OpenAI, 2026; Anthropic, 2026)。其中许多挑战源于信息的双重用途性质。例如，一些生物实验室操作规程既可用于良性的生物工程实验，也可用于有害的生物工程实验。某些有害任务可以被分解为单独来看无害的子任务，这使界定可接受行为变得更加困难（例如 Jones et al., 2024; Li et al., 2024）。有效界定安全的行为边界并确保系统能够学会这些边界，是一项持续存在的挑战，需要广泛了解新兴的 AI 滥用威胁。设计安全的系统始终需要在某些确实有用的能力上作出权衡，但深化我们对哪些类型的 AI 能力在理论上和实践中有害的理解，可以帮助从业者在应对这些规范制定困境时尽量减少权衡的代价。
Pluralistic and legal alignment: Humans often disagree on how AI systems should behave. This is a fundamentally unsolvable problem. However, there exist princi-pled approaches for attempting to balance different viewpoints in ways that are normatively accepted (Sorensen et al., 2024; Conitzer et al, 2024). For example, many human institutions use voting as an acceptable way of resolving disagreements. In AI, develop-ing analogous processes for balancing differing views, prompting mutual understanding, and encouraging dialogue make a meaningful difference in how much AI systems serve as tools of education versus manipulation. Efforts to make more “pluralistically aligned” sys-tems in practice will require nuanced analysis, interdisciplinarity, and inclusion. It will also be helpful to approach these problems by studying how specifications respect rele-vant legal frameworks and normative ethical principles (Kolt et al., 2026; Hadfield et al., 2026). Finally, it will also be key to approach pluralism in AI in a way that is wary of “plural-ism washing”, recognizing that technical approaches to pluralistic alignment fail to address, and can sometimes distract from, deeper problems related to systematic biases and social power dynamics in the AI ecosystem (Kalluri, 2020; Dobbe et al., 2021; Birhane et al., 2022; Sloane et al., 2022; Gabriel and Keeling, 2025).
多元主义对齐与法律对齐：人们对于 AI 系统应当如何行事往往存在分歧。这是一个根本上无法解决的问题。不过，确实存在一些有原则依据的方法，尝试以规范上可接受的方式平衡不同观点（Sorensen et al., 2024; Conitzer et al, 2024）。例如，许多人类机构将投票作为一种可接受的分歧解决方式。在 AI 领域，开发类似的流程来平衡不同观点、促进相互理解并鼓励对话，能够切实影响 AI 系统在多大程度上成为教育工具或操纵工具。在实践中构建更加“符合多元主义对齐要求”的系统，需要细致入微的分析、跨学科合作以及包容各方的参与。研究规范如何遵循相关法律框架和规范伦理原则，也将有助于处理这些问题（Kolt et al., 2026; Hadfield et al., 2026）。最后，在处理 AI 中的多元主义问题时，警惕“多元主义洗白”（pluralism washing）也至关重要，即认识到多元主义对齐的技术方法无法解决 AI 生态系统中与系统性偏见和社会权力动态有关的更深层问题，有时还会转移人们对这些问题的注意力（Kalluri, 2020; Dobbe et al., 2021; Birhane et al., 2022; Sloane et al., 2022; Gabriel and Keeling, 2025）。
2.2 Design and implementation: Building the system2.2 设计与实现：构建系统
“How do we build the system?”
“我们如何构建这个系统？”
This section focuses on techniques to make systems that meet their specifications. The design and implementation process involves sourcing data, pretraining models, post-training models, and integrating them into AI systems. 32
本节重点介绍使系统满足其规范的技术。设计与实现过程包括获取数据、对模型进行预训练和后训练，以及将模型集成到 AI 系统中。32

> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
2.2.1 Training data and pretraining methods2.2.1 训练数据与预训练方法
> Updates: Emerging research has shown that pretraining data engineering can build strong safeguards into AI systems against harmful capabilities and propensities.
> 最新进展：新兴研究表明，预训练数据工程能够在 AI 系统中建立强有力的防护措施，以防范有害能力和倾向。
> Advantages: Pretraining data interventions can be powerful methods for controlling model capabilities and propensities. They offer an effective means of limiting dual-use knowledge in models, such as information about how to create cyber or biological attacks.
> 优势：预训练数据干预可以成为控制模型能力和倾向的有力方法。这些方法能够有效限制模型中的军民两用知识，例如有关如何实施网络攻击或生物攻击的信息。
> Challenges: Pretraining data curation experiments can be expensive, error prone, and have slow experimental feedback loops.
> 挑战：预训练数据整理实验可能成本高昂、容易出错，而且实验反馈周期较长。
Curating pretraining data: Pretraining is the first and often the most computation-ally- and data-intensive stage of developing modern AI systems. It is also the key stage in which models develop core knowledge representations. Modern AI systems are often pretrained on web-scale datasets, which makes it challenging to effectively curate and control the pretraining process (Paullada et al., 2021). Common pretraining datasets have been found to contain harmful, toxic, abusive, and even illegal content (Birhane et al., 2023; Thiel, 2023). Meanwhile, researchers have found evidence that methods for pretraining data curation greatly affect a system’s capabilities and propensities (Maini et al., 2025; O’Brien et al., 2025; Tice et al., 2026). Work to understand the relationship between pre-training dataset contents and emergent system behaviours will help with efforts to more safely curate pretraining data (Casper et al., 2025). In particular, doing so with high precision, high recall, high efficiency, and in a way that handles the massively multilin-gual nature of internet text will be key to improving the viability of pretraining methods (Anwar et al., 2024; Casper et al., 2025). See also Section 2.2.5 (Building safer open weight models). Shared pretraining data filters for harmful content - CSAM, bioweapon protocols, and similar - are an area of mutual interest , since they are easy to share and no devel-oper benefits from this content in their corpus.
预训练数据整理：预训练是开发现代 AI 系统的第一个阶段，通常也是计算和数据需求最大的阶段。这也是模型形成核心知识表征的关键阶段。现代 AI 系统通常在网络规模的数据集上进行预训练，这使得有效整理数据和控制预训练过程变得困难（Paullada et al., 2021）。研究发现，常见的预训练数据集包含有害、有毒、辱虐性乃至非法内容（Birhane et al., 2023; Thiel, 2023）。与此同时，研究人员发现了证据，表明预训练数据的整理方法会极大地影响系统的能力和倾向（Maini et al., 2025; O’Brien et al., 2025; Tice et al., 2026）。理解预训练数据集内容与系统涌现行为之间关系的研究，将有助于更安全地整理预训练数据（Casper et al., 2025）。尤其是，以高精确率、高召回率和高效率开展这项工作，并妥善处理互联网文本所具有的大规模多语言特性，将是提高预训练方法可行性的关键（Anwar et al., 2024; Casper et al., 2025）。另见第 2.2.5 节（构建更安全的开放权重模型）。针对有害内容——儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）、生物武器操作规程及类似内容——的共享预训练数据过滤器，是各方共同关注的领域，因为这些过滤器易于共享，而且任何开发者都无法从语料库中的此类内容获益。
Attributing model behaviours to training data: Methods for attributing model behaviours to specific examples from training data allow overseers to study how potentially harmful behaviours emerge in systems (Grosse et al., 2023). These tools could also help researchers identify what types of training interventions can mitigate them. For example, attributing risky capabilities or propensities to specific examples from training data could help developers curate safer pretraining datasets (O’Brien et al., 2025; Tice et al., 2025). Research frontiers include improving the efficiency and scalability of these methods, causally studying how models develop personas and behaviours (Anthropic-F; Tice et al., 2025), and predicting what data is needed to learn a particular behaviour (Engstrom et al., 2024; Hamidieh et al., 2025). 33
将模型行为归因于训练数据：将模型行为归因于训练数据中特定样本的方法，使监督者能够研究潜在有害行为如何在系统中涌现（Grosse et al., 2023）。这些工具还可以帮助研究人员确定哪些类型的训练干预能够缓解这些行为。例如，将具有风险的能力或倾向归因于训练数据中的特定样本，可以帮助开发者整理出更安全的预训练数据集（O’Brien et al., 2025; Tice et al., 2025）。研究前沿包括提高这些方法的效率与可扩展性，从因果角度研究模型如何形成角色特征和行为（Anthropic-F; Tice et al., 2025），以及预测学习某种特定行为需要哪些数据（Engstrom et al., 2024; Hamidieh et al., 2025）。33
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
2.2.2 Truthfulness and honesty2.2.2 真实性与诚实性
Updates: Empirically, hallucinations by frontier AI models have decreased. However, ongoing research has characterized distinct incidents of strategic dishonesty from AI systems.
最新进展：实证结果表明，前沿 AI 模型产生的幻觉有所减少。然而，持续开展的研究已刻画了 AI 系统出现策略性不诚实行为的具体事件。
Advantages: Thus far, untruthfulness and dishonesty in frontier AI systems have been empirically characterizable, and there is progress on mitigation.
优势：迄今为止，前沿 AI 系统中的不真实与不诚实行为能够通过实证方法加以刻画，缓解措施也取得了进展。
Challenges: Strategic dishonesty has been observed as a precedented, emergent harm in recent frontier AI systems. Despite their wide use, modern AI systems sometimes produce incorrect statements, which could be due to ‘accidental’ errors or ‘deliberate’ dishonesty. In some cases, mechanis-tic interpretability techniques can suggest what the system assesses or internally ‘believes’ to be true or false (Marks and Tegmark, 2023), in which case we can define ‘dishonesty’ as behavior where AI systems make assertions that are contrary to what they internally ‘believe’ (Ward et al., 2023), whereas ‘incompetence’ involves an AI system failing to form accurate ‘beliefs’ or assessments of what is in fact true (Ren et al., 2025). Dishon-esty includes examples of AI systems providing users with information that is clearly false because it helps them achieve a broader goal (e.g. Scheuer et al., 2023; Järviniemi and Hubin-ger, 2024). Regardless of their nature, untrue statements from AI systems are understood to be both directly harmful and a systemic risk to human education and epistemics (Virvou et al., 2025; Obiefuna, 2025). Methods for reducing the occurrence of false statements from AI systems are an ongoing research challenge. Approaches include both developing more truthful models through training on appropriate data (Evans et al., 2021; King, 2025) or designing systems to substantiate claims and cite references (Zhou et al., 2024). Frontiers for future work will include work to study and improve both factuality and hon-esty while also balancing these paradigms with risks of providing harmful information (Ren et al., 2024 Ren et al., 2025).
挑战：在近期的前沿 AI 系统中，策略性不诚实行为已作为一种有先例的涌现性危害被观察到。尽管现代 AI 系统已得到广泛应用，但它们有时仍会生成错误陈述，其原因可能是“意外”的错误，也可能是“故意”的不诚实行为。在某些情况下，机制可解释性技术可以提示系统将什么判断为或在内部“相信”为真或为假（Marks and Tegmark, 2023）；此时，我们可以将“不诚实”定义为 AI 系统作出与其内部“信念”相悖的断言的行为（Ward et al., 2023），而“能力不足”则指 AI 系统未能对实际为真的内容形成准确的“信念”或判断（Ren et al., 2025）。不诚实行为的例子包括：AI 系统向用户提供明显虚假的信息，因为这样有助于它们实现更广泛的目标（例如 Scheuer et al., 2023; Järviniemi and Hubin-ger, 2024）。无论其性质如何，AI 系统作出的不真实陈述都被认为既会造成直接危害，也会对人类教育与认知构成系统性风险（Virvou et al., 2025; Obiefuna, 2025）。如何减少 AI 系统作出虚假陈述，仍是一项持续存在的研究挑战。相关方法包括通过使用适当的数据进行训练来开发更真实的模型（Evans et al., 2021; King, 2025），或设计能够为主张提供佐证并引用参考文献的系统（Zhou et al., 2024）。未来的研究前沿将包括研究并提高事实准确性与诚实性，同时在这些范式与提供有害信息的风险之间取得平衡（Ren et al., 2024 Ren et al., 2025）。
2.2.3 Avoiding hazardous capabilities2.2.3 避免危险能力
Updates: Frontier AI systems have increased in their autonomy, generality, and intelligence. This has coincided with increases in these systems being implicated in criminal activity such as automating cyberattacks. Some progress has been made toward methods of reducing certain hazardous model capabilities in a targeted manner.
最新进展：前沿 AI 系统的自主性、通用性和智能水平已有所提高。与此同时，这些系统涉及自动化网络攻击等犯罪活动的情况也有所增加。在有针对性地削弱某些危险模型能力的方法方面，已取得一些进展。
Advantages: There is a large toolkit of techniques that can be used to limit the autonomy, generality, or intelligence of systems performing safety-critical functions.
优势：已有大量技术可用于限制执行安全关键功能的系统的自主性、通用性或智能水平。
Challenges: Methods to avoid hazardous capabilities are not always reliable or competitive in practical applications. 34
挑战：避免危险能力的方法在实际应用中并不总是可靠或具有竞争力。34
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
> Figure 2: AGI can also be thought of as the triple intersection of three distinct properties:
> 图 2：AGI 也可以被视为三种不同属性的三重交集：
> Autonomy, Generality and (domain) Intelligence. Source: Keep The Future Human
> 自主性、通用性和（领域）智能。来源：Keep The Future Human
It is challenging to ensure that AI systems cannot cause harm when they have powerful capabilities. There is a broad space of AI capabilities, and risks gener-ally increase with high autonomy, high generality, and high domain intelligence. For example, AlphaFold (Jumper et al., 2021) has high intelligence in the narrow domain of protein folding, but lacks autonomy or generality (“A” or “G”). A robotic lawn mower has high auton-omy but lacks generality or intelligence (“G” or “I”). Both are easy to control. A hypothetical future self-driving car that outperforms any human driver would have high autonomy and intelligence, but it poses a negligible loss-of-control risk due to low generality. Sys-tems having all three traits “A”, “G” and “I” are the most difficult to align or control. The three research directions below aim to improve trustworthiness by avoiding the “A”, “G”, or “I”, respectively.
当 AI 系统具备强大能力时，要确保它们不会造成危害是一项挑战。AI 的能力空间十分广阔，而风险通常会随着自主性、通用性和领域智能水平的提高而增加。例如，AlphaFold（Jumper et al., 2021）在蛋白质折叠这一狭窄领域具有很高的智能水平，但缺乏自主性或通用性（“A”或“G”）。机器人割草机具有很高的自主性，但缺乏通用性或智能（“G”或“I”）。两者都易于控制。假设未来有一辆自动驾驶汽车，其驾驶能力超过任何人类驾驶员，那么它将具有很高的自主性和智能水平，但由于通用性较低，其失控风险微乎其微。同时具备“A”、“G”和“I”这三种特征的系统最难对齐或控制。以下三个研究方向旨在分别通过避免“A”、“G”或“I”来提高可信性。
Limiting AI agent’s ability to influence the world (limited autonomy): Some early inci-dents with AI agents have involved agents taking irreversible harmful actions beyond the anticipated actions of users. For example, in July 2025, an AI agent deleted a software company’s entire codebase (Nolan, 2025). “Sandboxing” involves limiting the ways in which an agentic AI system can directly influence the world (Patil et al., 2024; He et al., 2024; Bus-cemi et al., 2025; Meng et al., 2025).
限制 AI 智能体影响世界的能力（限制自主性）：一些早期的 AI 智能体事件涉及智能体采取了超出用户预期的、不可逆的有害行动。例如，2025 年 7 月，一个 AI 智能体删除了一家软件公司的整个代码库（Nolan, 2025）。“沙箱隔离”（Sandboxing）是指限制智能体式 AI 系统能够直接影响世界的方式（Patil et al., 2024; He et al., 2024; Bus-cemi et al., 2025; Meng et al., 2025）。
“Machine unlearning” (limited generality): In addition to pretraining data curation (see Section 2.2.1), another method for limiting potentially harmful model capabilities is to actively suppress them. “Machine unlearning” algorithms can be used to suppress model capabilities in specific, high-risk domains (Li et al., 2024; Liu et al., 2024). For example, these techniques can be used to make text models less knowledge able about biohazards or make image/video models less able to photorealistically edit nude images of humans. Currently, machine unlearning algorithms can effectively make models safer, at the expense of sacrificing some positive uses of unlearned capabilities. However, making them robust has been a persistent challenge (Łucki et al., 2024; Feder Cooper et al., 2024; Sharma et al., 2024; Deeb and Roger, 2024; Che et al., 2025;). Current research priorities for improving unlearning algorithms involve improving the data, loss functions, optimi-zation techniques, and configurations behind unlearning algorithms to achieve more robust removal of unwanted capabilities (Casper et al., 2025). See also Section 2.2.5. 35
“机器遗忘”（限制通用性）：除预训练数据筛选整理（见第 2.2.1 节）之外，限制模型潜在有害能力的另一种方法是主动抑制这些能力。“机器遗忘”算法可用于抑制模型在特定高风险领域中的能力（Li et al., 2024; Liu et al., 2024）。例如，这些技术可用于减少文本模型掌握的生物危害相关知识，或降低图像/视频模型以照片级真实感编辑人体裸照的能力。目前，机器遗忘（machine unlearning）算法能够有效提高模型的安全性，但代价是牺牲被遗忘能力的一些有益用途。然而，使这些算法具备鲁棒性始终是一项挑战 (Łucki et al., 2024; Feder Cooper et al., 2024; Sharma et al., 2024; Deeb and Roger, 2024; Che et al., 2025;)。当前，改进遗忘算法的研究重点包括改进其所用的数据、损失函数、优化技术和配置，以更稳健地移除不需要的能力 (Casper et al., 2025)。另见第 2.2.5 节。35
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
Intelligence-bounded systems (limited intelligence): State-of-the-art intelligence is very often not needed for tasks. For example, it is common for users to give AI chat systems simple queries about facts, content summarization, or brainstorming (Deming et al., 2025). Relatedly, some combinations of safe queries for models can be unsafe (Li et al., 2024; Jones et al., 2024). Thus, as a matter of both risk management and efficient resource allo-cation, it is useful to develop effective approaches to using systems whose intelligence is correctly calibrated to the difficulties and dangers of specific use cases.
智能受限系统（有限智能）：执行任务往往并不需要最先进的智能水平。例如，用户经常向 AI 聊天系统提出有关事实、内容摘要或头脑风暴的简单请求 (Deming et al., 2025)。与此相关的是，某些对模型而言安全的查询在组合后可能变得不安全 (Li et al., 2024; Jones et al., 2024)。因此，从风险管理和高效资源分配两方面来看，开发有效方法，以使用智能水平与具体用例的难度和危险程度恰当匹配的系统，是有益的。
2.2.4 Adversarial Robustness2.2.4 对抗鲁棒性
Updates: Both human experts and attack algorithms can consistently circumvent safeguards implemented in frontier AI systems. One study identified over 60,000 exploits against the safety guardrails of 19 different production AI systems.
最新进展：人类专家和攻击算法都能够反复绕过前沿 AI 系统中实施的安全防护措施。一项研究发现了针对 19 个不同生产环境 AI 系统安全护栏的 60,000 多种利用手段。
Advantages: Empirically, efforts by model developers to increase the adversarial robustness of their frontier models have significantly increased the amount of effort required to circumvent safeguards.
优势：实证结果表明，模型开发者为增强其前沿模型的对抗鲁棒性所做的努力，已显著增加了绕过安全防护措施所需的投入。
Challenges: Robust safeguards are empirically challenging. Both increased scaling and algorithmic innovation may be required to ensure the security of frontier AI systems.
挑战：实证结果表明，构建稳健的安全防护措施具有挑战性。要确保前沿 AI 系统的安全性，可能既需要扩大规模，也需要算法创新。
Developing model safeguards that are more robust to malicious attacks: It is persis-tently difficult to design AI systems that reliably behave harmlessly in all real-world use cases. Models are particularly vulnerable to “adversarial attacks” which take the form of inputs that are specially optimized to make a system produce unsafe outputs, such as instructions for committing a crime. For example, a recent large-scale competition iden-tified over 60,000 exploits against the safety guardrails of 19 different production AI systems (Zou et al., 2025). Even state-of-the-art models are persistently vulnerable to state-of-the-art attack algorithms (e.g. Davies et al., 2026). Currently, adversarially training AI systems against diverse adversarial attacks is a standard but flawed defense. Imple-menting more effective robustness training techniques remains a key challenge. While model safeguards are consistently circumventable, efforts by model developers to improve robustness have empirically been able to greatly increase the amount of effort required to break defenses (AISI, 2025). Some approaches for improving adversarial training and adversarial robustness include scaling adversarial training (Howe et al., 2024; Lee et al., 2024; Zou et al., 2024) and modifying adversarial training algorithms (Xhonneux et al., 2024; Casper et al., 2024; Sheshadri et al., 2024; Dékány et al., 2025).
开发更能抵御恶意攻击的模型安全防护措施：设计能够在所有现实世界用例中可靠地保持无害行为的 AI 系统，始终十分困难。模型尤其容易受到“对抗攻击”的影响；这类攻击采用经过专门优化的输入，诱使系统生成不安全的输出，例如实施犯罪的指导。例如，近期一项大规模竞赛发现了针对 19 个不同生产环境 AI 系统安全护栏的 60,000 多种利用手段 (Zou et al., 2025)。即使是最先进的模型，也始终容易受到最先进攻击算法的影响（例如 Davies et al., 2026）。目前，针对多种对抗攻击对 AI 系统进行对抗训练，是一种标准但存在缺陷的防御方式。实施更有效的鲁棒性训练技术仍是一项关键挑战。尽管模型的安全防护措施屡屡可被绕过，但实证结果表明，模型开发者为提高鲁棒性所做的努力，已能够大幅增加突破防御所需的投入 (AISI, 2025)。改进对抗训练和对抗鲁棒性的一些方法包括扩大对抗训练规模 (Howe et al., 2024; Lee et al., 2024; Zou et al., 2024)，以及修改对抗训练算法 (Xhonneux et al., 2024; Casper et al., 2024; Sheshadri et al., 2024; Dékány et al., 2025)。
2.2.5 Building safer open-weight models2.2.5 构建更安全的开放权重模型
Updates: In the past year, models with publicly downloadable weights have become much more capable and popular. These models have benefits such as de-centralizing power and enabling safety research. However, they can be used discretely and arbitrarily tampered with by users, making them empirically common for some forms of misuse.
最新进展：过去一年中，权重可公开下载的模型在能力和普及程度上都有了显著提升。这些模型具有分散权力和支持安全研究等益处。然而，用户可以隐蔽地使用这些模型并任意篡改它们，实证结果表明，这使它们经常被用于某些形式的滥用。
Advantages: Open-weight model risk management can be improved using a mixture of pre-training, post-training, and evaluation techniques. 36
优势：结合预训练、后训练和评估技术，可以改进开放权重模型的风险管理。36
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
> Challenges: Open-weight models risk management is fundamentally challenging due to how external safeguards (e.g. content filters) can be trivially disabled, and open models can be arbitrarily tampered with.
> 挑战：由于外部安全防护措施（例如内容过滤器）可以轻易被禁用，而开放模型可以被任意篡改，开放权重模型的风险管理从根本上就具有挑战性。
Advanced open-weight AI models, which can be downloaded by anyone, tend to lag behind the capabilities of frontier proprietary models by only several months (Ember-son, 2025). From a safety perspective, open-weight models pose both benefits and risks. They distribute power, reduce single points of failure, and can be studied more easily for safety research. However, they also have greater potential for misuse. They can be modified arbitrarily, used without oversight, and spread irreversibly on the web. This suggests that methods for making open-weight models more safe and trustworthy will be key to both accessing their benefits and mitigating their risks. Due to the inevitable and wide spread of open-weight models, it will also be critical to build awareness and resilience to their risks (see Section 4). Because open-weight models spread across bor-ders, enable unique forms of unmonitorable misuse, and resist single-developer control, their risk management is an area of mutual interest .It is important for researchers and policymakers to be aware of the ecological differences and tradeoffs that exist between open and closed model deployments. Some models – even with safeguards – might enable acute forms of misuse if deployed with open weights. Other models might significantly hinder open-science or concentrate large amounts of power if deployed with closed weights. Other models might pose major risks regardless of deployment type (Casper et al., 2025).
任何人都可以下载的先进开放权重 AI 模型，其能力通常仅比前沿专有模型落后几个月 (Ember-son, 2025)。从安全角度来看，开放权重模型既带来益处，也带来风险。它们能够分散权力、减少单点故障，而且更便于开展安全研究。然而，它们被滥用的可能性也更大。它们可以被任意修改、在无人监督的情况下使用，并以不可逆的方式在网络上传播。这表明，使开放权重模型更加安全、可信的方法，将是发挥其益处和缓解其风险的关键。鉴于开放权重模型不可避免地广泛传播，提高对其风险的认识和抵御能力也将至关重要（见第 4 节）。由于开放权重模型跨越国界传播，使独特且无法监控的滥用形式成为可能，并且难以由单一开发者控制，因此，其风险管理是各方共同关切的领域。研究人员和政策制定者有必要了解开放式与封闭式模型部署之间存在的生态差异和权衡。某些模型即使具备防护措施，在以开放权重方式部署时，也可能使严重的滥用形式成为可能。另一些模型若以封闭权重方式部署，则可能严重阻碍开放科学，或导致权力高度集中。还有一些模型无论采用何种部署方式，都可能带来重大风险（Casper et al., 2025）。
Training data curation: A key goal for improving the safety of the open-weight model ecosystem is to make models that are more “tamper resistant” against harmful forms of fine-tuning. Training data curation is currently understood to be a state-of-the-art method for making models more benign under modifications. For example, O’Brien et al. (2025) found that filtering biohazard-related text from a model’s pretraining data made it over 10x more resistant to learning those capabilities through fine-tuning than post-train-ing baseline techniques. However, it is not understood for what types of capabilities data curation is effective, how to scalably and effectively perform such curation, or what the relationship is between training data contents and emergent model capabilities (Maini et al., 2025; Casper et al., 2025). See also Section 2.2.1.
训练数据筛选与整理：提高开放权重模型生态系统安全性的一个关键目标，是构建对有害微调更具“抗篡改能力”的模型。目前，训练数据筛选与整理被认为是使模型在经过修改后仍保持较强无害性的最先进方法之一。例如，O’Brien et al. (2025) 发现，从模型的预训练数据中过滤掉与生物危害相关的文本后，相较于训练后处理的基线技术，模型抵御通过微调习得这些能力的能力提高至 10x 以上。然而，目前尚不清楚数据筛选与整理对哪些类型的能力有效，如何以可扩展且有效的方式开展此类筛选与整理，以及训练数据内容与模型涌现能力之间存在何种关系（Maini et al., 2025; Casper et al., 2025）。另见第 2.2.1 节。
Tamper-resistant unlearning: There has been a significant amount of work on “machine unlearning” algorithms that are resistant to re-learning unwanted capabil-ities. However, prior approaches have broadly struggled. For example, Casper et al. (2025) observe that state-of-the-art tamper-resistant unlearning methods, as evaluated by second-party red-teaming research, are only resistant to several hundred steps of adversarial fine-tuning. This underscores a need for more tamper-resistant unlearning algo-rithms by improving the data, loss functions, optimization techniques, and configurations behind unlearning algorithms to achieve more robust removal of unwanted capabilities (Li et al., 2024; Casper et al., 2025). See also Section 2.2.3. 37
抗篡改遗忘：已有大量研究致力于开发能够抵御重新习得非期望能力的“机器遗忘”（machine unlearning）算法。然而，以往的方法普遍面临困难。例如，Casper et al. (2025) 指出，经第二方红队研究评估，最先进的抗篡改遗忘方法只能抵御数百步的对抗性微调。这凸显出有必要改进遗忘算法所采用的数据、损失函数、优化技术和配置，开发抗篡改能力更强的遗忘算法，从而更稳健地移除非期望能力（Li et al., 2024; Casper et al., 2025）。另见第 2.2.3 节。37
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
Evaluations of open-weight models under realistic (mis)use cases: Because they can be tampered with post-deployment, worst-case risk assessment for open-weight models requires analyzing their potential harms under malicious fine-tuning. However, this is currently uncommon in practice (Wallace et al., 2025; Casper et al., 2025; Kamachee et al., 2025; Dombrowski et al., 2025; Paskov et al., 2026). It also remains unclear how to rigor-ously estimate worst-case model behaviours under realistic tampering threats (Casper et al., 2025; Paskov et al., 2026).
在现实使用（及滥用）场景下评估开放权重模型：由于开放权重模型在部署后可能遭到篡改，对其进行最坏情形风险评估时，需要分析其在恶意微调下可能造成的危害。然而，目前这种做法在实践中并不常见（Wallace et al., 2025; Casper et al., 2025; Kamachee et al., 2025; Dombrowski et al., 2025; Paskov et al., 2026）。此外，如何在现实的篡改威胁下严格估计模型在最坏情形下的行为，目前仍不明确（Casper et al., 2025; Paskov et al., 2026）。
2.2.6 Building safer AI agents2.2.6 构建更安全的 AI 智能体
Updates: AI agents are rapidly becoming more powerful, common, and economically impactful. The AI agent ecosystem generally lacks transparency and standardized safety practices. AI incidents related to misuse and user safety are increasing.
最新进展：AI 智能体正迅速变得更强大、更普遍，并产生更大的经济影响。AI 智能体生态系统普遍缺乏透明度和标准化的安全实践。与滥用和用户安全相关的 AI 事件正在增加。
Advantages: Numerous types of guardrails can be implemented to improve the design, testing and monitoring of AI agents across their lifecycle.
优势：可以实施多种类型的防护措施，以改进 AI 智能体在整个生命周期中的设计、测试和监控。
Challenges: Capability/security tradeoffs and immature safety practices in the agent ecosystem have led to incidents with AI agents. AI agents are rapidly becoming more powerful and common (Staufer et al., 2026; METR, 2026; Shapira et al., 2026). For example, OpenClaw agents have become popular in 2026 and have been implicated in a number of security and alignment concerns (Ying et al., 2025; Wang et al., 2026). With this has come growing concerns about how powerful AI agents can serve as a risk factor for acute misuse (Iqbal et al., 2025; Kouremetis et al., 2025), crises of accountability (Himmelreich, 2019; Feder Cooper et al., 2022; Kolt, 2025), and loss of control (Bengio, 2023; Hendrycks et al., 2023; IAISR). Agent safety is also complicated by how AI agents are often more likely to comply with harmful requests than the same underlying language models that power them operating as a chatbot (Andriuschenko et al., 2024; Yu et al., 2052; Fan et al., 2025). Thus, in addition to the underlying language model being safe, agent safety also requires that the agent’s prompts, tools, and environment are designed safely and in a way that mitigates emergent harms. Amidst mounting incidents from AI agents (Staufer et al., 2026), further progress in agent safety will require sandboxing (see Section 2.2.3), agentic identity infrastructure (see Section 4.1), systems safety (see Section 1.2), and agentic safety evaluations (e.g. Vijayvargiya et al., 2025).
挑战：智能体生态系统中能力与安全性之间的权衡，以及尚不成熟的安全实践，已导致涉及 AI 智能体的事件发生。AI 智能体正迅速变得更强大、更普遍（Staufer et al., 2026; METR, 2026; Shapira et al., 2026）。例如，OpenClaw 智能体在 2026 年流行起来，并涉及多项安全与对齐方面的问题（Ying et al., 2025; Wang et al., 2026）。随之而来的是日益加深的担忧：强大的 AI 智能体可能成为严重滥用（Iqbal et al., 2025; Kouremetis et al., 2025）、问责危机（Himmelreich, 2019; Feder Cooper et al., 2022; Kolt, 2025）和失控（Bengio, 2023; Hendrycks et al., 2023; IAISR）的风险因素。AI 智能体通常比为其提供支持的同一底层语言模型以聊天机器人形式运行时更容易遵从有害请求，这也使智能体安全问题更加复杂（Andriuschenko et al., 2024; Yu et al., 2052; Fan et al., 2025）。因此，除了底层语言模型本身需要安全之外，智能体安全还要求以安全且能减轻涌现危害的方式设计智能体的提示词、工具和环境。在 AI 智能体引发的事件不断增加的背景下（Staufer et al., 2026），智能体安全的进一步发展将需要沙箱隔离（见第 2.2.3 节）、智能体身份基础设施（见第 4.1 节）、系统安全（见第 1.2 节）以及智能体安全评估（例如 Vijayvargiya et al., 2025）。
2.2.7 Trustworthy multi-agent interaction2.2.7 可信的多智能体交互
Updates: AI agents are increasingly used in multi-agent workflows, or participate in strategic or competitive interactions with other agents on behalf of human users with different interests.
最新进展：AI 智能体正日益广泛地用于多智能体工作流，或代表具有不同利益的人类用户，与其他智能体开展策略性或竞争性交互。
Advantages: Research in multi-agent systems and multi-agent RL demonstrates that it is possible to build or train AI systems to more reliably achieve cooperative outcomes.
优势：多智能体系统和多智能体强化学习（RL）领域的研究表明，可以构建或训练 AI 系统，使其更可靠地实现合作结果。
Challenges: LLM-based AI agents are not currently trained to handle strategic multi-agent interactions with competing or adversarial agents. 38
挑战：目前，基于大语言模型（LLM）的 AI 智能体尚未接受应对与竞争性或对抗性智能体进行策略性多智能体交互的训练。38
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
Given the increasing use of AI agents in multi-agent workflows (e.g. Claude Code sub-agents), as well as the increasing number of strategic interactions between AI agents serving different users or goals (e.g. OpenClaw agents, social media bots, agen-tic financial traders) it will be crucial to design AI agents so that they can remain safe and trustworthy in multi-agent interactions (Tomašev et al., 2025a). In the case of mul-ti-agent teams and workflows, where agents all serve a single user or high level goal, trustworthiness requires properties like safe delegation to sub-agents (Tomašev et al., 2026), avoidance of cascading failures or weakest-link vulnerabilities, and reliable inter-agent communication (Reid et al., 2025). As for strategic or competitive interactions, AI agents need to be designed to have the cooperative capabilities required for safe out-comes (Dafoe et al., 2020; Dafoe et al., 2021), while remaining robust to uncooperative or adversarial behavior (Franklin et al., 2026), ensuring legal alignment (Kolt et al., 2026), and avoiding multi-agent risks such as miscoordination, conflict, or undesirable col-lusion between agents (Hammond et al., 2025). Progress has been made on avoiding these risks in the fields of multi-agent systems and multi-agent reinforcement learning (Trivedi et al., 2024; Vinitsky et al., 2023; Oldenburg & Zhi-Xuan, 2024), but these tech-niques have yet to be translated to frontier AI systems.
鉴于 AI 智能体在多智能体工作流（例如 Claude Code 子智能体）中的使用日益增多，以及服务于不同用户或目标的 AI 智能体之间的策略性交互（例如 OpenClaw 智能体、社交媒体机器人、智能体式金融交易系统）日益频繁，如何设计 AI 智能体，使其在多智能体交互中保持安全和可信，将至关重要 (Tomašev et al., 2025a)。对于所有智能体都服务于同一用户或高层目标的多智能体团队和工作流，可信性要求具备一些特性，例如向子智能体安全委派任务 (Tomašev et al., 2026)、避免级联故障或最薄弱环节漏洞，以及可靠的智能体间通信 (Reid et al., 2025)。至于策略性或竞争性交互，需要将 AI 智能体设计为具备实现安全结果所需的合作能力 (Dafoe et al., 2020; Dafoe et al., 2021)，同时能够稳健应对不合作或对抗性行为 (Franklin et al., 2026)，确保与法律要求对齐 (Kolt et al., 2026)，并避免协调失误、冲突或智能体之间不当串通等多智能体风险 (Hammond et al., 2025)。多智能体系统和多智能体强化学习领域在规避这些风险方面已取得进展 (Trivedi et al., 2024; Vinitsky et al., 2023; Oldenburg & Zhi-Xuan, 2024)，但这些技术尚未应用于前沿 AI 系统。
2.3 Verification: Assessing if the system works as specified2.3 验证：评估系统是否按规约运行
“Does the system meet its specification (behave as desired)?”
“系统是否满足其规约（按预期行事）？”
The research areas described in this section aim to assess the extent to which the built system (2.2) meets its specifications (2.1). This section discusses several broad types of techniques that can be used to provide evidence that a system is safe. In practice, the effectiveness of these methods is often limited by access and transparency, but they can play a central role in constructing AI safety cases : structured arguments for why sys-tems pose an acceptably low level of risk (Clymer et al., 2024; Buhl et al., 2024).
本节介绍的研究领域旨在评估所构建的系统 (2.2) 在多大程度上满足其规约 (2.1)。本节讨论几大类可用于提供系统安全性证据的技术。在实践中，这些方法的有效性往往受到访问权限和透明度的限制，但它们可以在构建 AI 安全论证（AI safety cases）中发挥核心作用：这类论证以结构化方式说明，为何系统带来的风险处于可接受的低水平 (Clymer et al., 2024; Buhl et al., 2024)。
2.3.1 Red-teaming2.3.1 红队测试
Updates: Human experts and attack algorithms are consistently able to extract harmful behaviors from frontier AI systems, such as assisting users in planning dangerous or criminal activities.
最新进展：人类专家和攻击算法持续能够诱发前沿 AI 系统的有害行为，例如协助用户策划危险或犯罪活动。
Advantages: Red-teaming methods are empirically effective at identifying vulnerabilities. They are recognized as a pillar of modern AI risk assessment and management.
优势：实证表明，红队测试方法能够有效识别漏洞。这些方法被公认为现代 AI 风险评估与管理的支柱之一。
Challenges: Red teams sometimes fail to identify vulnerabilities. Red-teaming is also particularly difficult in multi-agent use cases. The goal of robustness testing is to develop techniques for evaluating whether sys-tems are trustworthy, even in novel contexts such as unprecedented “black swan” events or under attacks from malicious users. This includes developing improved red-teaming tools to identify inputs that cause systems to behave harmfully. 39
挑战：红队有时无法发现漏洞。在多智能体应用场景中，红队测试也尤其困难。稳健性测试的目标是开发相关技术，以评估系统即使在前所未有的“黑天鹅”事件等新情境下，或遭到恶意用户攻击时，是否仍然可信。这包括开发更完善的红队测试工具，以识别会导致系统产生有害行为的输入。39
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
Robustness testing: Adversarial testing depends on techniques to evaluate system safety under deliberate attempts to make them behave harmfully. There are numer-ous approaches to adversarial testing (see also Section 1.1). For example, researchers have developed many model “jailbreaking” techniques which can subvert the safeguards in modern AI systems, causing them to behave harmfully (Jin et al., 2024). Attacks can also be conducted on any data modality that the model can process. For example, mul-timodal models that can process text, images, video, and/or audio data can have a very large attack surface as a result (Liu et al., 2024; Jiang et al., 2025). Currently, state-of-the-art red-teaming approaches rely on a mix of automated (e.g. Davies et al., 2026) and manual attacks (e.g. Zou et al., 2025). Key research goals include the continued develop-ment of more effective and scalable ways to attack systems and integrating those methods into evaluation frameworks.
稳健性测试：对抗性测试依赖于能够在有人蓄意诱使系统产生有害行为时评估其安全性的技术。对抗性测试有多种方法（另见第 1.1 节）。例如，研究人员已开发出许多模型“越狱”（jailbreaking）技术，能够绕过现代 AI 系统的安全防护措施，导致其产生有害行为 (Jin et al., 2024)。攻击也可以针对模型能够处理的任何数据模态展开。例如，能够处理文本、图像、视频和/或音频数据的多模态模型，可能因此具有非常大的攻击面 (Liu et al., 2024; Jiang et al., 2025)。目前，最先进的红队测试方法依赖于自动化攻击（例如 Davies et al., 2026）与人工攻击（例如 Zou et al., 2025）的结合。关键研究目标包括持续开发更有效、可扩展性更强的系统攻击方法，并将这些方法整合到评估框架中。
Red-teaming systems in multiagent contexts: Systems that behave safely in simple, controlled settings can often fail in novel, more complex contexts. One very promi-nent version of this is emergent failures due to multi-agent interactions (Hammond et al., 2025). These types of failures are expected to become increasingly prominent as highly autonomous AI agents continue to be adopted. For example, if one self-driving car learns to drive safely on streets with human drivers, it is still possible for it to be unsafe on streets with other self-driving cars because they may not behave exactly the same as humans. Multiagent failures are challenging to study because they often emerge unexpectedly and are hard to demonstrate in laboratory settings. Future work to study and identify emergent multiagent failure modes will involve a mix of theory, simulation, adversarial attacks, and field tests to understand emergent multiagent failure modes (Ham-mond et al., 2025; Schroeder deWitt, 2025; He et al., 2025). Further research should also study how agents deployed in the economy can communicate and cooperate with each other and with people and online services to avoid risks, e.g. through interoperability standards and agent authentication (Chan et al., 2024; Chan et al., 2025) as well as learning cooperative skills (Dafoe et al., 2021; Dafoe et al., 2020).
在多智能体情境中对系统开展红队测试：在简单、受控环境中表现安全的系统，在新颖且更复杂的情境中往往可能失效。其中一种尤为突出的情况是多智能体交互引发的涌现性失效 (Hammond et al., 2025)。随着高度自主的 AI 智能体持续得到采用，预计这类失效将日益突出。例如，如果一辆自动驾驶汽车学会了在有人类驾驶员的街道上安全行驶，它在有其他自动驾驶汽车的街道上行驶时仍可能不安全，因为这些汽车的行为可能与人类并不完全相同。多智能体失效难以研究，因为这类失效往往出乎意料地涌现，而且很难在实验室环境中复现。未来研究和识别涌现性多智能体失效模式的工作，将结合理论、仿真、对抗攻击和实地测试，以理解这些涌现性多智能体失效模式 (Ham-mond et al., 2025; Schroeder deWitt, 2025; He et al., 2025)。进一步的研究还应探讨，部署于经济活动中的智能体如何彼此沟通与合作，以及如何与人类和在线服务沟通与合作以规避风险，例如通过互操作性标准和智能体身份认证 (Chan et al., 2024; Chan et al., 2025)，以及学习合作技能 (Dafoe et al., 2021; Dafoe et al., 2020)。
2.3.2 Quantitative verification of safety properties2.3.2 安全属性的定量验证
> Updates: Research is ongoing on methods for quantifying safety-related properties of AI and designing systems that are, in some ways, “safe by design.”
> 最新进展：研究人员正在持续研究量化 AI 安全相关属性的方法，以及设计在某些方面“通过设计保障安全”的系统的方法。
> Advantages: If successful, these methods could help practitioners make high-confidence assurances related to safety for some AI systems.
> 优势：如果取得成功，这些方法可以帮助从业者对某些 AI 系统的安全性作出高置信度的保证。
> Challenges: Current methods are not currently common or practical for frontier AI systems.
> 挑战：现有方法目前尚未在前沿 AI 系统中得到普遍应用，也不具备实用性。
Quantitative safety and safety by design: Techniques that provide quantitative bounds on the likelihood of certain behaviors could provide safety assurances akin to existing industry standards for systems such as jet engines and nuclear reactors. These solutions include formal verification approaches for proving that AI-written code, AI scaffolding, or AI containment measures have specific safety-related properties. With current models and methods, it is not possible to use these techniques to make 40
定量安全与通过设计保障安全：能够为某些行为的发生概率提供定量界限的技术，可以提供类似于喷气发动机和核反应堆等系统现有行业标准所提供的安全保证。这些解决方案包括形式化验证方法，用于证明 AI 编写的代码、AI 支撑框架或 AI 隔离措施具有特定的安全相关属性。以当前的模型和方法，尚无法使用这些技术作出 40
> 2dE vELOPING TRuST wORT hy, S EC uRE AN d R ELIABLE Sy STEMS
> 2 开发可信、安全且可靠的系统
strong assurance about frontier system behaviours, but continued work may help to estab-lish practical techniques for making quantitative assurances of safety-related properties (Dalrymple et al., 2024). Some approaches to developing AI that is “safe by design” include techniques for distilling some machine-learned algorithms into code (Michaud et al., 2024), developing verifiable models of how AI systems affect their environment, or developing methods to build systems from smaller verified components.
关于前沿系统行为的强有力保证，但持续开展的研究可能有助于确立实用技术，以对安全相关属性作出定量保证 (Dalrymple et al., 2024)。开发“通过设计保障安全”的 AI 的一些途径包括：将某些机器学习所得的算法蒸馏为代码的技术 (Michaud et al., 2024)、构建关于 AI 系统如何影响其环境的可验证模型，或开发利用经过验证的较小组件来构建系统的方法。
2.3.3 understanding system behavior and cognition2.3.3 理解系统行为与认知
> Updates: Mechanistic interpretability techniques have been used to supplement alignment evaluations for some frontier AI systems. Model persona research might offer paths to understand how far aligned behavior generalizes.
> 最新进展：机制可解释性技术已被用于补充某些前沿 AI 系统的对齐评估。模型人格研究可能为理解对齐行为能够泛化到何种程度提供途径。
> Advantages: These techniques offer unique ways to identify and address manipulation, deception, or other harmful capabilities and behaviours in AI systems.
> 优势：这些技术为识别和应对 AI 系统中的操纵、欺骗或其他有害能力与行为提供了独特的途径。
> Challenges: It is unclear if and how much unique, practical value mechanistic interpretability techniques offer, while research into “model psychology” is unlikely to be sufficient for very strong assurances.
> 挑战：机制可解释性技术是否能提供独特的实用价值，以及能提供多大价值，目前尚不明确；而“模型心理学”研究不太可能足以提供非常强有力的保证。
Mechanistic interpretability: Techniques for understanding how models function and represent concepts internally could aid in the discovery of safety-related system prop-erties (Sharkey et al., 2025). For example, mechanistic interpretability techniques might be able to help researchers characterise and intervene on model representations that cor-respond to harmful concepts such as deception or malice. Some frontier model developers have begun to use interpretability techniques to supplement evaluations of alignment (e.g. Anthropic, 2026) and monitor model cognition at runtime (Kramár et al., 2026). How-ever, it is unclear the extent to which new techniques for mechanistic interpretability are broadly useful or competitive with simple mechanistic or black-box baselines. The principal goal for safety-focused mechanistic interpretability research is to under-stand if and how new mechanistic techniques can offer practical advantages over existing approaches for model diagnostics and editing (Sharkey et al., 2025).
机制可解释性：用于理解模型如何运作以及如何在内部表征概念的技术，可以帮助发现与安全相关的系统属性 (Sharkey et al., 2025)。例如，机制可解释性技术或许能够帮助研究人员刻画并干预与欺骗或恶意等有害概念相对应的模型表征。一些前沿模型开发者已开始使用可解释性技术来补充对齐评估（例如 Anthropic, 2026），并在运行时监测模型认知 (Kramár et al., 2026)。然而，新的机制可解释性技术在多大程度上具有广泛用途，或能与简单的机制方法或黑箱方法基线相竞争，目前尚不明确。以安全为重点的机制可解释性研究，其主要目标是了解新的机制分析技术能否以及如何相较于现有的模型诊断与编辑方法提供实际优势 (Sharkey et al., 2025)。
Cognitive science of AI models: An alternative approach to understanding model cognition uses behavioral and experimental methods from cognitive science and psychol-ogy, instead of focusing on internally-represented concepts or algorithms. This includes approaches that predict and understand LLM behavior through the problem they are trained to solve (McCoy et al., 2023; Ku et al., 2025), and also more recent work studying the psychology of LLM personas and the degree to which they represent coherent and generalizable patterns of behavior (Marks et al, 2026; Soligo et al, 2026; Sofroniew et al, 2026). Understanding AI behavior can provide evidence that AI systems will remain safe and aligned in novel situations, though it remains unclear if such research will mature sufficiently to serve the needs of verification (Nielsrolf et al., 2025). 41
AI 模型的认知科学：理解模型认知的另一种途径是采用认知科学和心理学中的行为研究与实验方法，而非聚焦于内部表征的概念或算法。这包括通过 LLM 受训要解决的问题来预测和理解其行为的方法 (McCoy et al., 2023; Ku et al., 2025)，也包括近期研究 LLM 人格心理特征，以及这些人格在多大程度上体现出连贯且可泛化的行为模式的工作 (Marks et al, 2026; Soligo et al, 2026; Sofroniew et al, 2026)。理解 AI 行为可以为 AI 系统在新情境下仍将保持安全与对齐提供证据，但此类研究能否发展到足够成熟、满足验证需求的程度，目前仍不明确 (Nielsrolf et al., 2025)。41
