> 3 控制
3 Control3 控制
Associated with IAISR chapter 3.3.2
对应 IAISR 第 3.3.2 节
Key information and updates关键信息与最新进展
Since ISE 2025, several new developments are important to address. This summary lists research priorities that are particularly important given these new developments.
自 ISE 2025 以来，出现了几项需要重点关注的新进展。本摘要列出了鉴于这些新进展而尤为重要的优先研究事项。
Control-undermining AI behaviors have become a difficult challenge for safety testing in practice . AI systems finding loopholes (reward hacking) in evaluations is now a leading concern among evaluation developers. Additionally, models can now often detect when they are being tested (evaluation awareness). This makes test results less trustworthy.
破坏控制的 AI 行为已成为实际安全测试中一项棘手的挑战。AI 系统在评估中寻找漏洞（奖励黑客，reward hacking），如今已成为评估开发者最主要的关切之一。此外，模型如今往往能够察觉自己正在接受测试（评估意识，evaluation awareness）。这使测试结果的可信度降低。
Research priorities: Evaluations and adaptable testing that models cannot distinguish from real settings. Ensuring that written chains of thought remain faithful and monitorable (see Sections 3.1-3.2). Realistic evaluations for sandbagging and alignment faking, plus secure access regimes that allow third parties to detect and neutralize evaluation awareness (Section 1.1 and 1.5). 42
优先研究事项：开展模型无法与真实场景区分的评估和可适应性测试。确保书面思维链保持忠实且可监测（见第 3.1-3.2 节）。针对能力隐藏（sandbagging）和伪装对齐（alignment faking）开展贴近实际的评估，并建立安全访问机制，使第三方能够检测并消除评估意识的影响（第 1.1 和 1.5 节）。42
> 3 C ONTROL
> 3 控制
Evidence of control-undermining AI behaviors related to future loss of control risks has accumulated in controlled experiments. Under specific circumstances, observed behaviors include possible early warnings for loss-of-control risks: attempted self-preservation, self-exfiltration, blackmail, generalized misalignment, among others.
在受控实验中，与未来失控风险有关的、破坏控制的 AI 行为证据不断积累。在特定情况下，观察到的行为包括一些可能预示失控风险的早期警示信号：试图自我保存、自我外传、勒索、泛化性失配等。
Research priorities: Developing methods for control and alignment that resist control-undermining strategies. Threat modeling to understand the likelihood and imminence of loss-of-control risks, and to design operationalizable red lines (see Sections 1.4 and 3.2).
优先研究事项：开发能够抵御破坏控制策略的控制与对齐方法。开展威胁建模，以了解失控风险发生的可能性及其迫近程度，并设计可付诸实施的红线（见第 1.4 和 3.2 节）。
The research areas in this category focus on tools for controlling a system (after it has been developed) to behave as desired, often through feedback loops involv-ing monitoring and intervention. In engineering, “control” usually refers to the process of managing or regulating a running system’s behaviour to achieve a desired outcome. It is about designing mechanisms—often through feedback loops—to ensure that a system operates as desired even when faced with disturbances or uncertainties.
本类别的研究领域侧重于用于控制系统（在其开发完成后）按预期行事的工具，通常通过包含监测和干预的反馈回路来实现。在工程学中，“控制”通常指管理或调节运行中系统的行为，以实现预期结果的过程。其核心是设计机制——通常通过反馈回路——以确保系统即使面临扰动或不确定性，也能按预期运行。
3.1 Control, monitoring and intervention3.1 控制、监测与干预
Updates: Monitoring and intervention tools are common and often considered an essential component of “safety cases” for AI systems.
最新进展：监测和干预工具已得到普遍使用，并且往往被视为 AI 系统“安全论证”（safety cases）的必要组成部分。
Advantages: Monitoring and intervention techniques diverse in their varieties, simple to implement, and highly useful for building safer systems. These methods are nearly ubiquitous among frontier AI systems (thought to varying levels of rigor).
优势：监测和干预技术种类多样、易于实施，对构建更安全的系统非常有用。这些方法在前沿 AI 系统中几乎无处不在（尽管严谨程度各不相同）。
Challenges: Monitors are imperfect and vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Meanwhile, developing AI systems using monitor outputs as an optimization signal risks degrading the reliability of the monitors. “Conventional” monitoring and intervention refers to techniques that can be straightforwardly integrated into many types of AI systems regardless of scope, domain, or intelligence. They often parallel techniques from other fields such as cybersecurity and content moderation. These techniques help researchers identify and act on poten-tially harmful actions that systems might be taking. When incidents occur, these methods also help in the construction of incident reports.
挑战：监测器并不完美，而且容易受到对抗性攻击。与此同时，使用监测器输出作为优化信号来开发 AI 系统，存在降低监测器可靠性的风险。“常规”监测与干预是指可以直接集成到多种 AI 系统中的技术，不受系统范围、应用领域或智能水平的限制。这些技术通常与网络安全和内容审核等其他领域的技术相似。这些技术帮助研究人员识别系统可能正在采取的潜在有害行动，并采取应对措施。事件发生时，这些方法也有助于编写事件报告。
Hardware-enabled mechanisms (HEMs): Given trusted hardware, certain tools built into hardware can produce trustworthy attestations about who is running what, where, and how much (RAND) — claims that third parties such as regulators, auditors, or counterparties to international agreements can rely on (Harack et al., 2025; Baker et al., 2025; Brundage et al., 2020). Well-designed HEMs can also preserve privacy and intellectual property: confidential computing is designed to prevent the compute provider from learn-ing what is being run unless the user chooses to share an attestation, enabling structured transparency. Frontiers for future work on hardware-enabled mechanisms include both the engineering challenge of designing these tools to be efficient and the practi-cal challenge of integrating them into compute infrastructure (Aarne et al., 2024; O’Gara et al., 2025). Hardware-enabled mechanisms are an area of mutual interest - if successfully 43
硬件支持机制（Hardware-enabled mechanisms，HEMs）：在硬件可信的前提下，某些内置于硬件的工具可以就谁在何处运行什么、运行规模多大（RAND）提供可信的证明——监管机构、审计机构或国际协议的其他缔约方等第三方可以依赖这些声明（Harack et al., 2025; Baker et al., 2025; Brundage et al., 2020）。设计良好的 HEMs 还可以保护隐私和知识产权：机密计算旨在防止算力提供方获知正在运行的内容，除非用户选择分享相关证明，从而实现结构化透明。硬件支持机制未来工作的前沿方向，既包括将这些工具设计得高效的工程挑战，也包括将其集成到算力基础设施中的实际挑战（Aarne et al., 2024; O’Gara et al., 2025）。硬件支持机制是一个各方共同关注的领域——如果成功 43
> 3 C ONTROL
> 3 控制
implemented, they allow safety agreements and commitments to be verified across bor-ders without exposing sensitive intellectual property (Harack et al., 2025; Baker et al., 2025; Brundage et al., 2020).
实施，便可在不暴露敏感知识产权的情况下，跨境核验安全协议和承诺的履行情况（Harack et al., 2025; Baker et al., 2025; Brundage et al., 2020）。
System state monitoring: Techniques for monitoring a system’s activities can help to identify when it might be performing in a harmful or unexpected way. For example, a company providing a chatbot service may wish to filter the model’s responses using an unsafe-text classifier before sending them to a user. There are many different approaches that can be taken to state monitoring. Techniques can vary by the object of monitoring which can be system inputs, outputs, chains of thought (see below), and/ or internal cognition. They can also vary by the type of monitor which can include filters, event-loggers, and anomaly detectors. Frontiers for additional research include further developing and integrating monitors that achieve both a high degree of monitoring efficacy (IAISR), and performing reliable monitoring even under adversarial attempts to evade over-sight (Greenblatt et al., 2023).
系统状态监测：监测系统活动的技术，有助于识别系统何时可能以有害或出乎预期的方式运行。例如，提供聊天机器人服务的公司可能希望在将模型回复发送给用户之前，使用不安全文本分类器对其进行过滤。状态监测可以采用多种不同的方法。这些技术可根据监测对象的不同而有所区别，监测对象可以是系统输入、输出、思维链（见下文）和/或内部认知。它们也可根据监测器类型的不同而有所区别，监测器可以包括过滤器、事件记录器和异常检测器。有待进一步研究的前沿方向包括：进一步开发和集成监测器，使其既能实现高度有效的监测（IAISR），又能在遭遇旨在规避监督的对抗性尝试时仍可靠地执行监测（Greenblatt et al., 2023）。
LLM chain-of-thought faithfulness and legibility: Large language model chain-of-thought reasoning does not always faithfully represent how a model arrived at its decisions (Turpin et al., 2023). This poses challenges to safety because, without faithful reasoning, models could fool overseers by saying one thing and doing another. For example, language models have stated that they gave their answer based on a logical argument when they actually chose it based on hints that they should not have exploited (Anthropic, 2025, Turpin et al., 2023), such as seeing that the correct answer in a multiple-choice test is always “B”. One potential challenge with chain-of-thought monitoring stems from how, under optimisation pressure on their reasoning, systems may learn to obfuscate their rea-soning in ways that can be actively misleading (OpenAI, 2025; Baker et al., 2025; Korbak et al., 2025).
LLM 思维链的忠实性与可理解性：大型语言模型的思维链推理并不总能忠实反映模型如何作出决策（Turpin et al., 2023）。这给安全带来了挑战，因为如果推理过程不忠实，模型就可能通过言行不一来欺骗监督者。例如，语言模型曾声称自己依据逻辑论证给出答案，但实际上却是根据本不应利用的提示作出选择（Anthropic, 2025, Turpin et al., 2023），例如发现某项选择题测试中的正确答案总是“B”。思维链监测的一项潜在挑战在于，当推理过程受到优化压力时，系统可能学会以具有主动误导性的方式掩饰其推理过程（OpenAI, 2025; Baker et al., 2025; Korbak et al., 2025）。
3.2 Controlling and evaluating highly capable AI systems3.2 控制与评估高能力 AI 系统
> Updates: Ongoing research is investigating how to reliably contain and control AI systems, even ones that may be highly intelligent and autonomously pursuing goals misaligned with their designers’ intent.
> 最新进展：正在开展的研究正探索如何可靠地约束和控制 AI 系统，即使这些系统可能具有很高的智能，并自主追求与设计者意图不一致的目标。
> Advantages: Scalable oversight techniques can be incorporated into “safety cases.”
> 优势：可扩展监督技术可以纳入“安全论证”（safety cases）。
> Challenges: Human agency and involvement is essential. Scalable oversight techniques are not uniquely useful for safety and may exacerbate risks to the extent that they are used to increase capabilities beyond what can be reliably monitored by humans.
> 挑战：人的自主性与参与至关重要。可扩展监督技术的用途并不限于安全；如果被用于将能力提升到人类无法可靠监测的程度，这些技术就可能加剧风险。
A particularly challenging frontier in operational control involves developing techniques for controlling AI systems that are not only highly capable but may potentially attempt to undermine control mechanisms. Unlike conventional methods which offer system-ag-nostic approaches to monitoring and intervention, this section focuses on research toward techniques for controlling systems that are potentially very powerful and may actively undermine attempts to control them (Hubinger, 2020). 44
运行控制领域一个尤其具有挑战性的前沿方向，是开发用于控制 AI 系统的技术，而这些系统不仅能力很强，还可能试图破坏控制机制。常规方法提供不依赖于特定系统的监测与干预手段，而本节重点介绍控制技术的相关研究，其对象是可能非常强大、并可能主动破坏控制尝试的系统（Hubinger, 2020）。44
> 3 C ONTROL
> 3 控制
When discussing methods for building systems that humans may not be able to reliably oversee, we urge caution against ”safetywashing” (Ren et al., 2024) which refers to the usage of safety as a motivation for research and development work that does not provide net value for safety. It is useful to acknowledge that the ability to oversee highly intelligent systems is not inherently uniquely valuable for safety. Such methods could also be used to develop AI capabilities beyond what can be reliably monitored by real humans.
在讨论如何构建人类可能无法可靠监督的系统时，我们敦促警惕“安全漂洗”（safetywashing）（Ren et al., 2024），即以安全为动机开展研发工作，却未能为安全带来净收益。有必要认识到，监督高智能系统的能力并非天然只对安全有价值。此类方法也可能被用于发展超出实际人类所能可靠监测范围的 AI 能力。
Scalable oversight: This research studies techniques that may allow less capa-ble systems to oversee and control more capable ones. Common themes behind scalable oversight techniques include affording less-capable overseers more computational resources (Evans et al., 2019) or having multiple highly-capable systems debate (Irving et al., 2018 Michael et al., 2023; Buhl et al., 2025), though these might not scale to superhu-man systems. A core research gap is that the alignment of overseer AIs is itself unverified, a problem amplified by the rapidly growing use of AI for internal AI R&D, including for safety oversight (see Section 4.1). Research frontiers include improving the reliability of oversight even when systemic errors from humans are present, developing more effective AI debate protocols, methods for monitoring systems for control-under-mining behaviours, and methods for monitoring untrusted smarter systems with a combination of humans and relatively trusted weaker systems (Greenblatt et al., 2023 Korbak et al., 2025). Another approach to oversight research is to combine multiple control strategies and attempt to quantify their joint success probability. For example, research-ers can work to quantify the reliability of nested scalable oversight regimes where less capable systems oversee more capable ones (Engels et al., 2025).
可扩展监督：这一研究方向探索可能使能力较弱的系统监督和控制能力较强的系统的技术。可扩展监督技术的常见思路包括为能力较弱的监督者提供更多计算资源（Evans et al., 2019），或让多个能力很强的系统进行辩论（Irving et al., 2018 Michael et al., 2023; Buhl et al., 2025），但这些方法未必能扩展到超人类系统。一个核心研究缺口在于，承担监督职责的 AI 本身是否对齐尚未得到验证，而在内部 AI 研发中使用 AI 的情况正迅速增加，包括将其用于安全监督，这进一步放大了该问题（见第 4.1 节）。研究前沿包括：即使存在源于人类的系统性错误，也能提高监督的可靠性；开发更有效的 AI 辩论协议；开发监测系统是否存在破坏控制行为的方法；以及结合人类与相对可信的较弱系统，监测不可信的更智能系统的方法（Greenblatt et al., 2023 Korbak et al., 2025）。监督研究的另一种思路是结合多种控制策略，并尝试量化它们共同取得成功的概率。例如，研究人员可以尝试量化嵌套式可扩展监督机制的可靠性，在这种机制中，能力较弱的系统监督能力较强的系统（Engels et al., 2025）。
Non-agentic AI systems as guardrails: Agentic AI systems, especially future ones that broadly outperform humans, pose additional risks compared to other AI systems. How-ever, non-agentic AI systems could be used to monitor their proposed actions and intervene by blocking them if needed. For example, it is possible that a “Scientist AI” (Bengio et al., 2025) could be used to estimate the probability that a candidate action from an agentic AI violates any plausible interpretation of safety specifications, and reject potentially unsafe actions. Certain approaches could guarantee that unsafe actions will be identified, with quantitative error bounds (Bengio et al., 2025). 45
将非智能体式 AI 系统用作安全护栏：与其他 AI 系统相比，智能体式 AI 系统，尤其是未来在广泛任务上超越人类的系统，会带来额外风险。不过，可以使用非智能体式 AI 系统来监测它们提出的行动，并在必要时通过阻止这些行动进行干预。例如，或许可以使用“Scientist AI”（Bengio et al., 2025）来估计智能体式 AI 提出的某项候选行动违反安全规范的任何一种合理解释的概率，并拒绝可能不安全的行动。某些方法可以保证识别出不安全行为，并给出定量误差界限（Bengio et al., 2025）。45
4 S OCIE TAL RESILIENCE
4 社会韧性
4 Societal Resilience4 社会韧性
Associated with IAISR 2026 chapter 3.5
对应 IAISR 2026 第 3.5 章
Key information and updates关键信息与最新进展
Since ISE 2025, several new developments are important to address. This summary lists research priorities that are particularly important given these new developments.
自 ISE 2025 以来，出现了几项需要重点关注的新进展。本摘要列出了鉴于这些新进展而尤为重要的优先研究事项。
Rapidly growing societal impacts of frontier AI, including harms, are diffuse and difficult to study. Much like electricity, the internet, and other general-purpose technologies, frontier AI systems are integrating into most industries. Researchers currently find it challenging to study and forecast systemic AI impacts related to education, labour, equality, consumer protection, political radicalization, etc.
前沿 AI 对社会的影响（包括危害）正迅速扩大，这些影响广泛分散，难以研究。与电力、互联网及其他通用技术类似，前沿 AI 系统正在融入大多数行业。目前，研究人员发现，研究和预测 AI 在教育、劳动、平等、消费者保护、政治激进化等方面的系统性影响颇具挑战。
Research priorities: Understanding AI’s diffuse downstream impacts (see Section 1.3 and 4.1) and studying which levers governments and companies can use to mitigate risks. 46
研究重点：理解 AI 广泛分散的下游影响（见第 1.3 节和第 4.1 节），并研究政府和企业可以采取哪些干预手段来降低风险。46
> 4 S OCIE TAL RESILIENCE
> 4 社会韧性
Autonomous AI agents now interact, program, and take actions together on the web, and humans know little about their actions. Platforms such as the AI-built social media site Moltbook lead to largely unpredictable multi-agent AI interactions. Possible risks are poorly understood.
自主 AI 智能体如今在网络上相互交互、协同编程并共同行动，而人类对它们的行为知之甚少。由 AI 构建的社交媒体网站 Moltbook 等平台促成了在很大程度上难以预测的多智能体 AI 交互。人们对潜在风险的了解十分有限。
Research priorities: Scalably monitoring human providers of compute infrastructure for agents; developing agent IDs and reputation systems; identifying effective points of intervention. Broadening monitoring and control methods, as well as real world field observations, to cover a multi-agent ecosystem (Section 3).
研究重点：以可扩展的方式监测为智能体提供算力基础设施的人类服务提供方；开发智能体 ID 和声誉系统；确定有效的干预切入点。拓展监测和控制方法以及现实世界的实地观测，使其覆盖多智能体生态系统（第 3 节）。
Model developers’ internal use of AI has become pervasive, accelerating progress and automating oversight. Model developers first use the most capable models internally, without external oversight. In 2026, internal models are used organization-wide, including for developing new models and automating safety evaluation and oversight. In the future, this might accelerate progress to levels that are societally not manageable. Already today, it makes it harder for AI companies to understand if and how their safety oversight process functions.
模型开发者在内部使用 AI 已十分普遍，这既加快了进展，也推动了监督的自动化。模型开发者会首先在内部使用能力最强的模型，且不受外部监督。2026 年，内部模型已在整个组织范围内使用，包括用于开发新模型，以及实现安全评估和监督的自动化。未来，这可能使进展加速到社会无法应对的程度。即使在当下，这也已使 AI 公司更难了解其安全监督流程是否发挥作用，以及如何发挥作用。
Research priorities: Developing metrics and tools for measuring the extent, speed, and effect of AI R&D automation. Assessing how plausible and how imminent an unmanageable “intelligence explosion” scenario is, including developing operationalized red lines (see Section 2). Understanding if and how AI systems overseeing the safety of other AI systems can be sufficiently safe (see Section 3).
研究重点：开发用于衡量 AI 研发自动化的程度、速度和影响的指标与工具。评估不可控的“智能爆炸”情景有多大的可能性，以及距离发生还有多近，包括制定可操作的红线（见第 2 节）。理解负责监督其他 AI 系统安全性的 AI 系统能否达到足够的安全水平，以及如何达到这一水平（见第 3 节）。
4.1 AI ecosystem monitoring4.1 AI 生态系统监测
Updates: Ongoing research progress is being made on methods to study the spread, usage, and impact of AI.
最新进展：用于研究 AI 传播、使用及影响的方法正在持续取得研究进展。
Advantages: A large toolkit of techniques can help researchers and the public better understand the uses and impacts of hardware, models, AI-generated data, AI models, and AI agents.
优势：丰富的技术工具可以帮助研究人员和公众更好地理解硬件、模型、AI 生成的数据、AI 模型和 AI 智能体的用途与影响。
Challenges: It is inherently hard to maintain thorough awareness of AI usage and agents in cyberspace. Most ecosystem monitoring methods can be circumvented by adversarial actors, and there are large implementation gaps. Methods for ecosystem monitoring support the identification and tracking of AI systems, data, and impacts. It serves as a foundation of resilience, facilitating accountability infrastructure, supporting greater public understanding, and enabling more informed governance.
挑战：全面、持续地掌握网络空间中的 AI 使用情况和智能体动态，本身就十分困难。大多数生态系统监测方法都可能被对抗性行为者规避，而且在实施方面存在巨大缺口。生态系统监测方法支持对 AI 系统、数据及影响的识别和追踪。生态系统监测是韧性的基础，有助于建设问责基础设施、增进公众理解，并支持更充分知情的治理。
Awareness of internal deployments of AI systems: The most visible deployments of AI systems happen when a system is made available to external users. However, some of the most consequential deployments of AI systems happen behind closed doors (Stix et al., 2025). For example, internally-deployed systems can be used to automate analysis of sensitive data, profile users, automate research, and recursively self-improve (Kwon and Casper, 2025). Delegating oversight roles to AI systems could also erode a develop-er’s understanding of how their own internal risk management infrastructure works 47
掌握 AI 系统内部部署的情况：AI 系统最显而易见的部署形式，是向外部用户开放系统。然而，一些影响最为重大的 AI 系统部署是在不对外公开的情况下进行的（Stix et al., 2025）。例如，内部部署的系统可用于自动分析敏感数据、构建用户画像、自动开展研究，以及进行递归自我改进（Kwon and Casper, 2025）。将监督职责委托给 AI 系统，还可能削弱开发者对其自身内部风险管理基础设施运作方式的理解 47
> 4 S OCIE TAL RESILIENCE
> 4 社会韧性
(see Section 3.2 on scalable oversight). It forms a key risk factor for loss of control risks. Even basic metrics of AI R&D automation still need to be developed and rigor-ously measured - capital share of R&D spending, researcher effort allocation between humans and AI, AI subversion incidents, whether automated safety progress is keeping pace with automated capability progress, and others (Chan et al., 2026). Managing risks from internally-deployed systems is a challenging technical governance problem, made difficult by regulatory grey areas and a lack of awareness (Stix et al., 2025; Kwon and Casper, 2025; Acharya and Delaney, 2025). It also offers a potential barrier to developing international agreements about AI. To better monitor and manage risks from internal deployments, one technical research priority could be to develop secure infrastructure that can allow external authorities to maintain more awareness about internal uses of AI systems that meet thresholds triggering regulatory requirements.
（关于可扩展监督，见第 3.2 节）。它构成了导致失控风险的一个关键风险因素。即使是 AI 研发自动化的基本指标，也仍有待开发和严格测量，包括研发支出中的资本份额、人类与 AI 之间的研究工作量分配、AI 颠覆事件、自动化安全研究的进展是否跟得上自动化能力研究的进展，以及其他指标 (Chan et al., 2026)。管理内部部署系统带来的风险，是一个具有挑战性的技术治理问题，监管灰色地带和认知不足使这一问题更加棘手 (Stix et al., 2025; Kwon and Casper, 2025; Acharya and Delaney, 2025)。这也可能成为制定 AI 国际协议的障碍。为了更好地监测和管理内部部署带来的风险，一项技术研究重点可以是开发安全的基础设施，使外部主管机构能够更充分地了解那些达到触发监管要求的阈值的 AI 系统在内部的使用情况。
Cloud and hardware monitoring: The points of access for cloud compute and hard-ware represent a significant point of visibility into understanding who is using compute for what and where. Researching techniques to monitor the use and distribution of compute, both legal and illegal, enables the assessment of trends, risks, and the allocation of resources to enforce safe usage policies (Sastry et al., 2024 Kulp et al., 2024; Egan and Heim, 2023; Aarne et al., 2024; Heim, et al 2024; O’Gara et al., 2025). If implemented successfully, cloud- and hardware-enabled mechanisms could play a unique role in verifying compliance, even for international agreements and across borders (Baker et al., 2024; Harack et al., 2025; Brundage et al., 2020) - see Section 3.1.
云与硬件监测：云计算资源和硬件的接入点是重要的观察窗口，有助于了解谁在何处使用计算资源，以及将其用于何种用途。研究监测计算资源合法及非法使用和分配情况的技术，有助于评估趋势和风险，并为执行安全使用政策分配资源 (Sastry et al., 2024 Kulp et al., 2024; Egan and Heim, 2023; Aarne et al., 2024; Heim, et al 2024; O’Gara et al., 2025)。如果成功实施，基于云和硬件的机制可以在核查合规情况方面发挥独特作用，甚至可用于国际协议和跨境合规核查 (Baker et al., 2024; Harack et al., 2025; Brundage et al., 2020)——参见第 3.1 节。
User monitoring: Monitoring for system misuse can help AI service providers identify malicious users who may be seeking to misuse a system and/or vulnerable users who may be psychologically harmed by the system. It is a key part of “know-your-customer” approaches to risk management. User monitoring is not as simple as identifying poten-tially unsafe uses (e.g. chats) due to (1) the risk of unintentionally impeding useful red-teaming (Longpre et al., 2024) and (2) the potential for adversarial users to imple-ment sophisticated strategies to evade detection , such as using multiple accounts and obfuscation techniques (Clymer et al., 2025). When successful, user monitoring methods can be used to identify and intervene in acutely harmful uses of AI systems. For example, in late 2025, Anthropic reported on the identification and response to instances of Claude being used to automate cyberattacks (Anthropic, 2025). Frontiers for future work include iteration on methods to efficiently identify risky or unhealthy user behaviours with a low false posi-tive rate.
用户监测：监测系统滥用情况，可以帮助 AI 服务提供商识别可能试图滥用系统的恶意用户，以及／或者可能在心理上受到系统伤害的脆弱用户。这是“了解你的客户”（know-your-customer）风险管理方法的关键组成部分。用户监测并非只是识别潜在的不安全使用行为（例如聊天）那么简单，原因在于：(1) 可能无意中妨碍有益的红队测试 (Longpre et al., 2024)，以及 (2) 对抗性用户可能采取复杂策略来逃避检测，例如使用多个账户和混淆技术 (Clymer et al., 2025)。用户监测方法在成功发挥作用时，可用于识别并干预对 AI 系统的严重有害使用。例如，2025 年末，Anthropic 报告了其识别和应对利用 Claude 自动实施网络攻击的事件的情况 (Anthropic, 2025)。未来工作的前沿方向包括迭代改进相关方法，以较低的误报率高效识别有风险或不健康的用户行为。
Tracing usage patterns across the model lifecycle: One key, high-level lens into how AI systems impact the world is through usage monitoring (e.g. Anthropic, 2026; Anthropic, 2026). By collecting and monitoring data on the usage, spread, capabilities, and unexpected impacts of frontier systems, AI researchers can gather insights about potential impacts and risks. However, key challenges with tracking usage include privacy preservation, infrastructure for sharing insights, and effective tools for bridging the gap between the collection of usage data and gaining meaningful insights about impacts. 48
追踪模型全生命周期中的使用模式：使用监测是从宏观层面了解 AI 系统如何影响世界的一个关键视角 (e.g. Anthropic, 2026; Anthropic, 2026)。通过收集和监测前沿系统的使用、传播、能力及意外影响方面的数据，AI 研究人员可以深入了解其潜在影响和风险。然而，追踪使用情况的关键挑战包括隐私保护、用于分享研究发现的基础设施，以及能够弥合使用数据收集与获得有意义的影响洞见之间差距的有效工具。48
> 4 S OCIE TAL RESILIENCE
> 4 社会韧性
Data provenance: A variety of techniques can help to identify AI-generated content and are a principal defence against AI deepfakes and misinformation. Methods include developing reliable classifiers of AI-generated content (Lin et al., 2024; Kaur et al., 2025; Piro-gov et al., 2025), watermarking AI-generated data (images, video, audio, and text) (Zhao et al., 2024; Jiang et al., 2024; Cao), and tagging AI-generated data with metadata to indicate its origin. These techniques are imperfect, especially in adversarial circumstances. For example, watermarks can be removed by tampering with data. However, in forensic sci-ence, similar techniques like fingerprinting are also circumventable but useful nonetheless. Further progress on these methods will benefit from developing more reliable methods. However, the largest barrier to impact with these methods is simply standardization and integration into applications.
数据溯源：多种技术可帮助识别 AI 生成的内容，是抵御 AI 深度伪造和错误信息的主要防线。相关方法包括开发可靠的 AI 生成内容分类器 (Lin et al., 2024; Kaur et al., 2025; Piro-gov et al., 2025)、为 AI 生成的数据（图像、视频、音频和文本）添加水印 (Zhao et al., 2024; Jiang et al., 2024; Cao)，以及为 AI 生成的数据添加元数据标签以标明其来源。这些技术并不完善，在对抗性环境下尤其如此。例如，可以通过篡改数据来去除水印。然而，在法庭科学中，指纹识别等类似技术也可以被规避，但仍然有用。开发更可靠的方法将有助于进一步推进这些方法。然而，这些方法发挥实际作用的最大障碍，其实就是标准化以及与应用的集成。
Model provenance: Tools for model provenance help to identify and track AI models – especially open-weight ones. Most notably, these tools help researchers study the ori-gins and lifecycle of harmful models in the ecosystem. Methods for model provenance involve techniques to help users and AI providers ascertain the identity and origin of a model. One approach is to “watermark models” with black-box methods, such as identification backdoors (e.g. Cheng et al., 2025), identifiable biases in text gener-ation (e.g. Kirchenbauer et al., 2023), or white-box methods, such as model weight watermarks (e.g. Gloaguen et al., 2025; Block et al., 2025). Other approaches can seek to perform model heritage inference ‘in the wild,’ even when unaided by watermarks (Horwitz et al., 2024; Nikolic et al,. 2025; Zhu et al., 2025). Much like data provenance meth-ods, model provenance methods can be circumvented, but they can be informative in many cases nonetheless. Research frontiers include studying how stable watermark-ing techniques are under fine-tuning (Casper et al., 2025) as well as implementing and scaling empirical model heritage studies (Horwitz et al., 2025)
模型溯源：模型溯源工具有助于识别和追踪 AI 模型，尤其是开放权重模型。最值得关注的是，这些工具有助于研究人员研究生态系统中有害模型的起源和生命周期。模型溯源方法涉及帮助用户和 AI 提供商确认模型身份及来源的技术。一种途径是为模型“添加水印”，采用黑盒方法，例如身份识别后门 (e.g. Cheng et al., 2025)、文本生成中可识别的偏差 (e.g. Kirchenbauer et al., 2023)，或采用白盒方法，例如模型权重水印 (e.g. Gloaguen et al., 2025; Block et al., 2025)。其他方法可以尝试在没有水印辅助的情况下，在“真实环境中”推断模型的传承关系（Horwitz et al., 2024; Nikolic et al,.2025; Zhu et al., 2025）。与数据溯源方法非常相似，模型溯源方法也可能被规避，但在许多情况下仍能提供有用信息。研究前沿包括考察水印技术在微调下的稳定性（Casper et al., 2025），以及开展模型传承关系的实证研究并扩大其规模（Horwitz et al., 2025）
4.2 Incident response and resilience4.2 事件响应与韧性
> Updates: Ongoing research and development efforts combined with emerging case studies related to AI incidents have enabled a better understanding of incident response and resilience strategies.
> 最新进展：持续开展的研究与开发工作，加上不断涌现的 AI 事件案例研究，使人们对事件响应与韧性策略有了更深入的理解。
> Advantages: Incident response and resilience strategies are a necessary and important step to long-term AI risk management, particularly when prevention is difficult.
> 优势：事件响应与韧性策略是长期 AI 风险管理中必要且重要的一步，尤其是在难以预防事件发生的情况下。
> Challenges: It is difficult to prepare for unknown future incidents. There are also implementation gaps related to incident response.
> 挑战：很难为未来未知的事件做好准备。事件响应的实施也存在不足。
Given its increasing power and widespread use, frontier AI should be expected to cause some incidents and systemic harm, even when safeguards are in place. A final pillar of risk management is thus to identify, resist, absorb, recover, and adapt to emerging AI risks (IAISR). Future disruptions from AI will sometimes manifest as discrete harms in various domains, and other times involve cascades of systemic impacts, rippling throughout soci-ety (Shelby et al., 2022; Lawrence; Uuk et al., 2024). As a result, efforts to improve societal 49
鉴于前沿 AI 的能力日益增强且应用广泛，即使已采取防护措施，也应预期它仍会引发一些事件和系统性危害。因此，风险管理的最后一项支柱是识别、抵御、吸收新出现的 AI 风险，从中恢复并加以适应（IAISR）。未来 AI 带来的扰动有时会表现为各个领域中的个别危害，有时则会引发系统性影响的连锁反应，波及整个社会（Shelby et al., 2022; Lawrence; Uuk et al., 2024）。因此，提高社会 49
> 4 S OCIE TAL RESILIENCE
> 4 社会韧性
resilience to AI risks is an inherently interdisciplinary pursuit (Bernardi) to be under-taken simultaneously by companies, governments, and other stakeholders. Goals for improving resilience include: Company and government efforts to prepare for biological, chemical, cyber, and deepfake threats exacerbated by frontier AI systems (IAISR) by addressing the supply chains upstream and the resilience strategies downstream of inci-dents specific to each of these domains. Government efforts to mobilize in preparation for AI-enabled terrorism threats may be particularly crucial in the coming months. Steering the “offence-defence” balance in each of these fields toward safety, often by developing AI systems to specialize in defensive uses (IAISR). Company and government efforts to develop incident reporting and response strategies (Wasil-A, Wasil-B; Wei et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2025). For example, Anthropic reported detecting and blocking an attempt in September 2025 for using Claude models to automate cyberattacks (Anthropic, 2025). Shared incident report-ing infrastructure is an example of an area of mutual interest for both nations and companies, paralleling how competing aircraft manufacturers pool accident data. Improving AI literacy, including the public’s ability to avoid manipulation via AI propaganda, scams, and fake content. Improving the ability of societal institutions to recover from disruptions (Maas, 2018; Gandhi et al., 2025). Developing systems that are resilient to disruptions is simultaneously a process that benefits from iteration while also offering few opportunities for it. This highlights the importance of near-term work to manage AI harms to build knowledge about how to do so before greater future risks materialize. For example, work to manage post-Mythos (Anthropic, 2026) AI cyberthreats in the coming months will be crucial to prepare for the next generations of AI cyberthreats in coming months and years.
应对 AI 风险的韧性，本质上是一项跨学科事业（Bernardi），需要企业、政府及其他利益相关方同时推进。提高韧性的目标包括：企业和政府采取措施，针对各领域特定事件的上游供应链和下游韧性策略，为应对前沿 AI 系统加剧的生物、化学、网络和深度伪造威胁做好准备（IAISR）。政府为应对 AI 赋能的恐怖主义威胁而开展的动员准备工作，在未来几个月可能尤为关键。推动各领域的“攻防”平衡向有利于安全的方向发展，通常通过开发专门用于防御用途的 AI 系统来实现（IAISR）。企业和政府努力制定事件报告与响应策略（Wasil-A, Wasil-B; Wei et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2025）。例如，Anthropic 报告称，其于 2025 年 9 月发现并阻止了一次利用 Claude 模型自动实施网络攻击的尝试（Anthropic, 2025）。共享事件报告基础设施是国家和企业具有共同利益的一个领域，类似于相互竞争的飞机制造商汇集事故数据的做法。提升 AI 素养，包括公众避免受到 AI 宣传、诈骗和虚假内容操纵的能力。提高社会机构从扰乱中恢复的能力（Maas, 2018; Gandhi et al., 2025）。开发能够抵御扰动的系统，是一个既能从迭代中获益、又缺乏迭代机会的过程。这凸显了近期开展 AI 危害管理工作的重要性，以便在未来更大的风险成为现实之前，积累如何管理这些危害的知识。例如，未来几个月，为应对 Mythos（Anthropic, 2026）之后的 AI 网络威胁而开展的工作，将对做好准备以应对随后数月乃至数年内下一代 AI 网络威胁至关重要。
4.3 Infrastructure and institutions for AI agents4.3 面向 AI 智能体的基础设施与制度
> Updates: AI agents are increasingly participating in web-based interactions and the digital economy, necessitating infrastructure and institution design for trustworthy agent interactions.
> 最新进展：AI 智能体日益参与网络交互和数字经济，因此需要设计基础设施与制度，以支持可信的智能体交互。
> Advantages: Technical and social infrastructure will be crucial for ensuring that AI agent interactions are secure and monitorable, while avoiding deleterious societal outcomes.
> 优势：技术与社会基础设施对于确保 AI 智能体交互安全且可监测，同时避免有害的社会后果，将至关重要。
> Challenges: Agent providers may not converge on or abide by common standards; institutional inertia may limit adaptation or implementation of new institutional designs for AI agents.
> 挑战：智能体提供商可能无法就共同标准达成一致，或不遵守这些标准；制度惯性可能限制针对 AI 智能体的新制度设计的调整或实施。
As autonomous AI systems begin to transform workplaces, mediate social interactions, and participate in the economy, societal resilience will require moni-toring, oversight and incentivization of safe and trustworthy activity by AI agents. 50
随着自主 AI 系统开始改变工作场所、介入社会交往并参与经济活动，社会韧性的维持将需要对 AI 智能体的活动进行监测、监督，并激励其安全、可信地开展活动。50
> 4 S OCIE TAL RESILIENCE
> 4 社会韧性
Achieving this requires building technical infrastructure, standards, and protocols for mediating agent interaction, while designing institutions that disincentivize and sanc-tion normatively or legally unacceptable behavior.
实现这一目标，需要建设用于协调智能体交互的技术基础设施、标准和协议，同时设计相应制度，以抑制并制裁在规范或法律层面不可接受的行为。
Agent infrastructure: External infrastructure for identifying agents, shaping their inter-actions, and detecting and remedying their behaviour is growing increasingly important from a security and monitoring standpoint (Chan et al., 2025). The strongest convergence so far is around attribution , which is increasingly handled through agent-specific identity credentials (South et al., 2025; OpenID Foundation, 2025). However, compliance with norms around agent identification remains uneven, with some popular agent sys-tems failing to respect norms such as “robots.txt” files (Staufer et al., 2026). For agent interactions , interoperability and communication protocols (e.g. Model Context Protocol, Agent2Agent, Agent Payments Protocol) are emerging as the layer on which cross-organi-sational deployments depend. Infrastructure is less mature when it comes to detecting and remedying harmful behaviour. R untime monitoring and oversight tooling primarily lives inside individual systems, though standards are emerging around agent telemetry (Liu & Solomon, 2025). Key challenges lie in the development and standardisation of protocols involving AI agents (e.g. South et al., 2025; CAISI, 2026). Since effectiveness depends on industry-wide adoption, standardisation of these protocols is an area of mutual interest .For more on how agent infrastructure informs risk management practices, see the Com-panion Report on Agentic Risk Management .
智能体基础设施：从安全和监测的角度来看，用于识别智能体、塑造其交互方式，以及检测并纠正其行为的外部基础设施正变得日益重要（Chan et al., 2025）。迄今为止，共识最强的领域是归属认定（attribution），这正越来越多地通过智能体专属身份凭证来实现 (South et al., 2025; OpenID Foundation, 2025)。然而，智能体身份识别相关规范的遵守情况仍参差不齐，一些广泛使用的智能体系统未能遵守“robots.txt”文件等规范 (Staufer et al., 2026)。在智能体交互方面，互操作性与通信协议（例如 Model Context Protocol、Agent2Agent、Agent Payments Protocol）正成为跨组织部署所依赖的基础层。在检测和纠正有害行为方面，基础设施尚不成熟。运行时监控与监督工具目前主要存在于各个系统内部，不过，围绕智能体遥测的标准正在形成 (Liu & Solomon, 2025)。关键挑战在于涉及 AI 智能体的协议的开发与标准化（例如 South et al., 2025; CAISI, 2026）。由于这些协议的有效性取决于整个行业的采用，其标准化是各方共同关心的领域。有关智能体基础设施如何为风险管理实践提供支持的更多内容，请参阅配套的《智能体风险管理报告》。
Institution design for AI agents: With the proliferation of AI agents in society and the economy, it will be important to design institutions and mechanisms to ensure distributional AI safety (Tomašev et al, 2025a), and to avoid the possibility of grad-ual human disempowerment due to AI economic competition and cultural displacement (Kulveit et al, 2025; Edelman et al, 2025; Yang et al., 2026). Such institutions and mechanisms include: updating market mechanisms for AI participants (Tomašev et al, 2025b); creat-ing reputation, mediation, and contracting mechanisms for AI agents (Tewolde et al, 2026); and providing classificatory guidance on normatively acceptable AI behavior through Model Specification Institutions (Hadfield et al, 2026). These institutions would incentivize trustworthy and cooperative interactions between otherwise self-interested agents, while mitigating the deleterious outcomes that might arise when AI agents deviate from legal and social norms (Kolt et al, 2026; Hadfield, 2026), or from AI competition that is misaligned with the public good (Kulveit et al, 2025). However, it remains unclear if exist-ing institutions will adapt rapidly enough to implement new AI-ready institution designs. 51
面向 AI 智能体的制度设计：随着 AI 智能体在社会和经济中的广泛普及，设计相应的制度和机制，以确保分配层面的 AI 安全（distributional AI safety）(Tomašev et al, 2025a)，并避免人类因 AI 带来的经济竞争和文化替代而逐渐丧失自主权的可能性，将变得十分重要 (Kulveit et al, 2025; Edelman et al, 2025; Yang et al., 2026)。此类制度和机制包括：为 AI 参与者更新市场机制 (Tomašev et al, 2025b)；为 AI 智能体建立声誉、调解和缔约机制 (Tewolde et al, 2026)；以及通过模型规范机构（Model Specification Institutions），就规范上可接受的 AI 行为提供分类指导 (Hadfield et al, 2026)。这些制度将激励原本追求自身利益的智能体开展可信且合作的交互，同时减轻 AI 智能体偏离法律和社会规范 (Kolt et al, 2026; Hadfield, 2026)，或 AI 竞争与公共利益不一致时可能产生的有害后果 (Kulveit et al, 2025)。然而，现有制度能否足够迅速地作出调整，以落实适应 AI 的新制度设计，仍不明确。51
> 5 C ONCL uSION
> 5 结论
5 Conclusion5 结论
This consensus report has been designed and written to, as accurately as possible, sum-marize points of agreement among AI researchers across the world about scientific research priorities for AI safety. In the process of writing this report, we have been encouraged by a high level of agreement among the contributors on priorities as well as productive points of discussion on some points of disagreement. This report, how-ever, comes with limitations. Not all contributors agree on how to best prioritize the research objectives outlined here. It is also worth acknowledging that it was drafted in English by contributors representing some, but not all, parts of the world. This report is also engaging in a conversation that much of the world’s population is not involved in, such as the estimated 6.8 billion people who do not use generative AI (GAAN, 2026) or the estimated 3.7 billion people who do not use the internet (UN, 2026). Despite limitations, we hope that the points of widespread scientific agreement covered in this report can help governments seeking to form and coor-dinate strategies to manage AI risks. Due to their global nature, AI risks are often best addressed by coalitions rather than individual companies or countries. Work-ing together on these problems as an international community will help us better realize benefits and mitigate risks alike. Meanwhile, we believe that framing progress in AI as an adversarial race between global powers is not in the best interest of any nations’ pop-ulation. Racing to develop powerful AI capabilities may be against the shared interests of people, businesses, and countries alike if it involves cutting corners on risk manage-ment. As has been the case with nuclear energy and space shuttles, high-profile incidents can cast a shadow that creates unease and sets back global progress on adoption. This is also reflected in business polling and scientific research showing that safety and risk have become a leading bottleneck to AI deployment and adoption, especially with the rise of AI agents. While this report’s contributors share the consensus that critical progress risk management research remains to be made, this is not to say that scientific progress is sufficient, or even the main bottleneck to effective risk management. Techni-cal solutions alone will be far from sufficient, and need to be complemented with robust policies . Simply developing an improved risk management toolkit does little to promote broader risk-awareness, public education, risk governance, transparency, adoption of best practices, fair distribution of benefits, or checks on power in the AI ecosystem. Some prominent AI incidents of the past year have been ones that the AI research community has predicted and known how to mitigate for years but which market incentives alone were insufficient to avoid. We worry that some types of AI incidents may persist, despite being avoidable using established best techni-cal practices such as those in the Companion Report on Agentic Risk Management .52
本共识报告的设计与撰写旨在尽可能准确地概述全球 AI 研究人员对 AI 安全科学研究优先事项的共识。在撰写本报告的过程中，参与者对优先事项的高度共识，以及围绕一些分歧展开的富有成效的讨论，都令我们深受鼓舞。然而，本报告也存在局限。对于如何最合理地确定本报告所列研究目标的优先次序，并非所有参与者都持一致意见。还应承认，本报告以英语起草，参与者仅代表世界上的部分地区，而非所有地区。本报告所参与的讨论，也未涵盖世界人口中的很大一部分，例如估计有 6.8 十亿人未使用生成式 AI (GAAN, 2026)，以及估计有 3.7 十亿人未使用互联网 (UN, 2026)。尽管存在局限，我们仍希望，本报告涵盖的广泛科学共识能够帮助各国政府制定和协调 AI 风险管理战略。由于 AI 风险具有全球性，联合行动往往比单个公司或国家独自行动更能有效应对这些风险。国际社会共同努力解决这些问题，将有助于我们更好地实现收益并减轻风险。与此同时，我们认为，将 AI 的进步描述为全球大国之间的对抗性竞赛，不符合任何国家民众的最大利益。如果竞相开发强大 AI 能力的过程涉及在风险管理上偷工减料，那么这种竞赛可能违背个人、企业和国家的共同利益。正如核能和航天飞机领域曾经发生的情况一样，备受关注的事故可能蒙上阴影，引发不安，并阻碍全球推广应用的进程。企业调查和科学研究也反映了这一点：安全与风险问题已成为 AI 部署和采用的主要瓶颈，随着 AI 智能体的兴起，这一点尤为突出。尽管本报告的参与者一致认为，风险管理研究仍有关键进展亟待取得，但这并不意味着科学进步足以实现有效的风险管理，甚至也不意味着它是其中的主要瓶颈。仅靠技术解决方案远远不够，还需要辅以健全的政策。仅仅开发一套改进的风险管理工具，几乎无助于在 AI 生态系统中提高更广泛的风险意识、开展公众教育、推进风险治理、提升透明度、推动最佳实践的采用、公平分配收益或制约权力。过去一年中一些引人注目的 AI 事件，早在多年前就已被 AI 研究界预见，研究界也早已知道如何缓解这些事件，但仅靠市场激励不足以避免它们。我们担心，某些类型的 AI 事件可能会持续发生，尽管采用既有的最佳技术实践，例如《智能体风险管理配套报告》中的实践，本可以避免这些事件。52
> 5 C ONCL uSION
> 5 结论
Finally, it is often said that policymakers need the scientific community’s help
最后，人们常说，政策制定者需要科学界的帮助
in implementing sound approaches to AI risk management. We hope that this consen-sus report can play a role in this process. However, we also emphasize that the scientific community will need help from the policy community as well . In particular, by establishing more transparency and evidence-generating reporting infrastruc-ture from frontier developers, policymakers can greatly assist in ongoing research efforts to understand pathways to navigating shared AI risks, opportunities, and tradeoffs in areas of shared global interest. 53
来实施合理的 AI 风险管理方法。我们希望这份共识报告能在这一过程中发挥作用。然而，我们也强调，科学界同样需要政策界的帮助。具体而言，通过建立要求前沿 AI 开发者提高透明度并提供证据的报告基础设施，政策制定者可以极大地支持正在开展的研究，帮助人们理解如何在全球共同关切的领域应对共同的 AI 风险、把握机遇并权衡取舍。53

COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC R ISK MANAGEMENT
智能体风险管理配套报告
Companion Report on Agentic Risk Management Introduction智能体风险管理配套报告 引言
Agentic AI systems are increasingly capable of performing complex tasks with limited human involvement. 2025 saw a sharp increase in interest in AI agents, with releases accelerating sharply and agents’ autonomy levels rising in parallel [The AI Agent Index 2025]. This momentum has continued into early 2026, with OpenClaw and then Claw-code becoming the fastest-growing open source projects in history [Github 2026-A, Github 2026-B]. However, AI agents pose heightened reliability risks because they can act autono-mously and can directly affect other systems or the environments in which they are deployed. Both agent failures and agents effectively executing unintended objectives can cause greater harm than non-agentic AI systems, because humans have fewer chances to 63
智能体 AI 系统在有限的人类参与下执行复杂任务的能力日益增强。2025 年，人们对 AI 智能体的关注急剧增加，相关产品发布显著加速，智能体的自主性水平也同步提升 [The AI Agent Index 2025]。这一势头延续至 2026 年初，OpenClaw 以及随后出现的 Claw-code 先后成为历史上增长最快的开源项目 [Github 2026-A, Github 2026-B]。然而，AI 智能体带来了更高的可靠性风险，因为它们能够自主行动，并能直接影响其他系统或其部署环境。与非智能体 AI 系统相比，智能体失效以及智能体有效执行非预期目标这两种情况都可能造成更大的危害，因为人类进行干预的机会更少 63
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
intervene. Multi-agent systems introduce further risks, as errors can propagate, com-pound and amplify through agent interactions [International AI Safety Report 2026]. While some risks associated with agentic AI—such as prompt injection and data exfiltra-tion—are well-documented [OWASP Agentic AI Security 2025], the emergence of highly autonomous systems introduces novel safety and control challenges [The Singapore Con-sensus on Global AI Safety Research Priorities 2025, TC260 AI Safety and Governance Framework 2.0]. These risks include autonomous replication [UKAISI 2025], continu-ous self-improvement [Stanford Zitong Yang 2026, Shanghai AI Lab 2025], and agents conducting scientific experiments autonomously [Nature Communications 2025]. Early empirical evidence has documented agents executing unauthorised actions on behalf of non-owners and propagating unsafe practices across agent boundaries [Shap-ira et al. 2026]. Despite these developments, most general-purpose AI risk management frame-works have limited focus on the unique challenges posed by agentic AI systems [METR 2025, Open Problems in Frontier AI Risk Management 2026]. Crucially, agentic AI necessitates a paradigm shift from static, model-level alignment to dynamic, sys-tem-level runtime governance [World Economic Forum AI Agents in Action 2025]. In response, major jurisdictions are beginning to draft formal frameworks, guidance, and standards to address these gaps [Singapore Model AI Governance Framework for Agentic AI, China’s National Standardization Administration TC260 Draft Standards on Agent Safety, US CAISI/NIST AI Agent Standards Initiative]. This momentum presents a unique opportunity for the global scientific community to identify, synthesise, and scale emerging technical practices.
进行干预。多智能体系统引入了更多风险，因为错误可能通过智能体之间的交互传播、累积并放大 [International AI Safety Report 2026]。尽管与智能体 AI 相关的一些风险——例如提示注入和数据外泄——已有充分记录 [OWASP Agentic AI Security 2025]，高度自主系统的出现仍带来了新的安全与控制挑战 [The Singapore Con-sensus on Global AI Safety Research Priorities 2025, TC260 AI Safety and Governance Framework 2.0]。这些风险包括自主复制 [UKAISI 2025]、持续自我改进 [Stanford Zitong Yang 2026, Shanghai AI Lab 2025]，以及智能体自主开展科学实验 [Nature Communications 2025]。早期实证证据已记录到，智能体代表非所有者执行未经授权的操作，并跨越智能体边界传播不安全做法 [Shap-ira et al. 2026]。尽管出现了这些进展，大多数通用 AI 风险管理框架对智能体 AI 系统所带来的独特挑战仍关注有限 [METR 2025, Open Problems in Frontier AI Risk Management 2026]。关键在于，智能体 AI 要求实现范式转变：从静态的模型层面对齐，转向动态的系统层面运行时治理 [World Economic Forum AI Agents in Action 2025]。为此，主要司法管辖区正开始起草正式框架、指导文件和标准，以弥补这些缺口 [Singapore Model AI Governance Framework for Agentic AI, China’s National Standardization Administration TC260 Draft Standards on Agent Safety, US CAISI/NIST AI Agent Standards Initiative]。这一势头为全球科学界识别、综合并推广新兴技术实践提供了独特机遇。
Goals目标
Resulting from the International Scientific Exchange (ISE) on AI Safety 2026, this companion report synthesises emerging practices in agentic AI risk management. It pursues three primary objectives: 1. Grounding risk management in scientific evidence: Building a shared scientific basis for agentic risk management by drawing on documented developer prac-tice and peer-reviewed research, synthesised by researchers, frontier developers, and technical bodies globally. 2. Demonstrating framework alignment: Mapping governance and regulatory frameworks to identify areas of alignment that may support interoperability, mutual recognition, and global compliance. 3. Providing guidance and resources: Offering practical guidance and illustrative cases to incentivise knowledge-sharing among practitioners. 64
本配套报告是 2026 年 AI 安全国际科学交流（ISE）的成果，综合梳理了智能体 AI 风险管理的新兴实践。本报告旨在实现三个主要目标：1。以科学证据为风险管理奠定基础：借鉴有记录的开发者实践和经过同行评审的研究，并由全球研究人员、前沿 AI 开发者及技术机构加以综合，为智能体风险管理建立共同的科学基础。2.展示框架之间的一致性：梳理治理框架与监管框架之间的对应关系，识别可能支持互操作、相互认可和全球合规的一致之处。3.提供指导与资源：提供实用指导和示例案例，以促进实践者之间的知识共享。64
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Structure结构
In alignment with the International AI Safety Report 2026 , “AI systems” in this compan-ion report refers to general-purpose AI (GPAI)—systems adaptable to a wide range of tasks. Specifically, we focus on general-purpose AI agents: systems that autonomously plan and act to accomplish complex goals. While various functional definitions of “agents” exist, we adopt the four-capability model of Perception, Planning, Memory, and Execution [ITU-T F.748.46]. The companion report structures AI risk management into three primary stages: Design and Development, Testing and Deployment, and Operation and Monitoring, adapt-ing the AI lifecycle stages as defined in ISO/IEC 42001:2023, ISO/IEC 22989:2022 and CSA
与《2026 年国际 AI 安全报告》保持一致，本配套报告中的“AI 系统”指通用人工智能（GPAI），即能够适应广泛任务的系统。具体而言，我们聚焦于通用 AI 智能体：能够自主规划并采取行动以实现复杂目标的系统。尽管“智能体”存在多种功能性定义，我们采用由感知、规划、记忆和执行构成的四能力模型 [ITU-T F.748.46]。本配套报告参考 ISO/IEC 42001:2023、ISO/IEC 22989:2022 和 CSA 所定义的 AI 生命周期阶段，将 AI 风险管理划分为三个主要阶段：设计与开发、测试与部署，以及运行与监控
Guidelines on Securing AI Systems . However, these lifecycle structures were designed for base models and generative AI systems with relatively discrete development, valida-tion, and deployment boundaries. Agentic AI systems, which execute multi-step actions in diverse real-world settings under sparse human supervision, blur these boundaries. Gov-ernance practices are still emerging, risk-relevant properties do not confine themselves to discrete lifecycle phases, and runtime behaviour is difficult to predict from pre-de-ployment testing alone. We nonetheless refer to standard lifecycle stages in order to make the companion report’s principles and practices interoperable with the regulatory and standards landscape they are intended to inform. They should be understood as a coordinating structure that signals where risk mitigation can take place, not as rigid or mutually exclusive categories.
《AI 系统安全保障指南》。然而，这些生命周期结构是针对基础模型和生成式 AI 系统设计的，其开发、验证和部署之间的界限相对清晰。智能体 AI 系统在少量人类监督下，于多样化的现实环境中执行多步骤行动，从而模糊了这些界限。治理实践仍在形成之中，与风险相关的属性并不局限于某个独立的生命周期阶段，而且仅凭部署前测试难以预测运行时行为。尽管如此，我们仍采用标准的生命周期阶段，以便使配套报告中的原则和实践能够与其旨在为之提供参考的监管及标准体系相衔接。应将这些阶段理解为一种协调结构，用于指明可以在哪些环节开展风险缓解，而非僵化或相互排斥的类别。
Methodology方法
The companion report draws on two types of sources. First, research publications, developer documentation, and risk management frameworks from leading labs provide the empirical basis for identifying foundational risk management principles and cat-aloguing how they are being implemented in practice. Second, existing national and international standards, legislation, and policy guidance inform the governance context for each principle, showing why the principle is required or recommended by authoritative bodies. Through illustrative cases, we identify where a principle has been independently implemented or recommended across multiple sources—noting these as signals of convergence that can provide practical guidance to practitioners. Three methodological limitations should be noted here. First, the companion report aims to address only agent-specific risks that require distinct risk management approaches. Broader risks inherited from foundation models are not treated here unless the agen-tic context exacerbates or materially transforms the pre-existing risk. Second, the lifecycle structure adopted here is for organisation only. The companion report anchors princi-ples and practices to the stage where they are most consequential, while recognising that risk identification, assessment, and mitigation are continuous across the agen-tic lifecycle and are not confined to any single stage. The final limitation is the level of maturity of both technical practices and governance frameworks. Agentic AI govern-ance is a nascent field; convergence across developers and jurisdictions is thinner than 65
配套报告参考了两类来源。首先，领先实验室的研究出版物、开发者文档和风险管理框架，为识别基础性风险管理原则以及梳理这些原则在实践中的实施方式提供了实证基础。其次，现有的国内和国际标准、法律法规及政策指南为每项原则提供了治理背景，说明权威机构为何要求或建议遵循该原则。通过示例案例，我们识别出某项原则在多个来源中被独立实施或推荐的情况，并将其视为趋于一致的信号，可为实践者提供实际指导。这里需要指出三项方法上的局限。首先，配套报告仅旨在讨论需要采用独特风险管理方法的智能体特有风险。对于从基础模型继承而来的更广泛风险，本文不予讨论，除非智能体应用情境加剧了既有风险，或使其发生实质性变化。其次，此处采用的生命周期结构仅用于组织内容。配套报告将各项原则和实践对应到其影响最为重大的阶段，同时承认风险识别、评估和缓解贯穿智能体的整个生命周期，并不局限于任何单一阶段。最后一项局限在于技术实践和治理框架的成熟程度。智能体人工智能治理是一个新兴领域；不同开发者和司法管辖区之间的共识程度低于 65
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
for more established AI risk domains. The companion report’s findings should therefore be seen as exploratory, mapping the current technical landscape to inform future research rather than constituting a definitive framework.
那些更为成熟的人工智能风险领域。因此，配套报告的研究发现应被视为探索性的，其通过梳理当前技术格局为未来研究提供参考，而非构成一个定论性的框架。
Process编制过程
This companion report provides a synthesis of emerging global practices in agentic AI risk management. The text underwent an extensive peer-review process involv-ing consultation with the Expert Planning Committee, conference participants, and other expert reviewers. Through multiple iterations of written consultation and in-per-son deliberation, the companion report was refined to reflect a broad consensus among a diverse group of international researchers. The contributors to this synthesis—listed in the introductory pages—represent a cross-section of the global AI ecosystem, includ-ing leading academic institutions, frontier AI developers, government technical bodies, and civil society organisations. This collaborative approach seeks to ensure that the identified practices are both scientifically grounded and practically applicable across differ-ent jurisdictions.
本配套报告综合梳理了全球正在形成的智能体人工智能风险管理实践。本文经过了广泛的同行评审，其中包括征询专家规划委员会、会议参与者及其他专家评审者的意见。通过多轮书面意见征询和面对面讨论，配套报告得到完善，以反映来自多元背景的国际研究人员群体所达成的广泛共识。参与本次综合研究的贡献者列于报告开篇各页，他们代表了全球人工智能生态系统的不同领域，包括领先学术机构、前沿人工智能开发者、政府技术机构和民间社会组织。这种协作方式力求确保所识别的实践既有科学依据，又能在不同司法管辖区切实适用。
Mapping agentic risks to foundational mitigation principles将智能体风险映射至基础性缓解原则
We begin with a set of agentic AI hazards identified in several authoritative sources, including China’s TC260 Research Report on Agent Safety Standardisation (3.2.3 Sum-mary of Agentic AI Risks), the Careful Adoption of Agentic AI Services guidance released by several national cybersecurity agencies, OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 , and a growing body of academic literature. While this provides a useful baseline, we do not claim that this list is exhaustive.
我们首先汇集了若干权威来源所识别的一组智能体人工智能危险源，这些来源包括中国 TC260《智能体安全标准化研究报告》（3.2.3 智能体人工智能风险概述）、多个国家网络安全机构发布的《审慎采用智能体人工智能服务》指南、《OWASP 智能体应用十大风险 2026》，以及日益丰富的学术文献。虽然这提供了一个有用的基准，但我们并不声称该清单已穷尽所有情况。
Agentic hazard Mitigating principle Example practices
智能体危险源 缓解原则 实践示例
Agents acquire or exercise capabilities beyond what the task requires, including autonomous privilege escalation through tool access, API calls, or inter-agent delegation
智能体获取或运用超出任务所需的能力，包括通过工具访问、API 调用或智能体间委派自主提升权限
Least Privilege Task-scoped tool permissions; dynamic runtime capability restriction; API access controls with reauthorisation per action Agent actions are consequential but untraceable; in multi-agent systems, it can be especially unclear which agent acted, or on whose authority
最小权限 将工具权限限定在任务范围内；在运行时动态限制能力；对每项操作重新授权的 API 访问控制 智能体的行为会产生重大影响，却无法追溯；在多智能体系统中，尤其可能无法明确是哪个智能体采取了行动，或其依据谁的授权行事
Traceable Identity
可追溯身份
Unique agent identifiers; delegation chain logging; credential management for inter-agent authentication Agent actions are not recorded, making it difficult to investigate their actions, verify compliance, or hold them to account
唯一的智能体标识符；委派链日志记录；用于智能体间身份认证的凭据管理 智能体的行为未被记录，导致难以调查其行为、核实合规性或对其进行问责

可审计性 可显露篡改痕迹的操作日志；对工具调用、参数和输出进行结构化记录；支持监控和审查的实时日志访问 66

Systems with uncharacterised failure modes or capability boundaries are deployed
在尚未明确故障模式或能力边界的情况下部署系统
Validated Deployment
经验证的部署
Agent-specific capability evaluations; risk-gated deployment thresholds; phased rollout with defined escalation criteria Agentic systems face adversarial conditions, from supply chain compromise to inter-agent exploitation, that conventional robustness testing does not cover
针对智能体的能力评估；以风险为准入条件的部署阈值；具有明确升级处置标准的分阶段推出。智能体系统面临从供应链受损到智能体间漏洞利用等对抗性条件，而传统的稳健性测试并未涵盖这些条件
Adversarial Resilience
对抗韧性
Agent-specific red-teaming targeting tool use and orchestration logic; supply chain integrity verification; sandboxed adversarial evaluation environments Multi-agent deployments produce emergent behaviours, cascading failures, and aggregate harms that compound through shared components and feedback loops
针对智能体工具使用和编排逻辑的红队测试；供应链完整性验证；沙箱化的对抗性评估环境。多智能体部署会产生涌现行为、级联故障和总体危害，这些影响会通过共享组件和反馈回路叠加放大
Multi-Agent Stability
多智能体稳定性
Threat modelling of error transmission, accumulation, and feedback dynamics; system-wide safety controls calibrated to non-linear and path-dependent failure modes Agent behaviour drifts outside authorised parameters during execution, for example through corrupted state, manipulated context, or emergent goal shift, without detection or containment
针对错误传播、累积及反馈动态的威胁建模；根据非线性且具有路径依赖性的故障模式校准的系统级安全控制。智能体在执行过程中因状态损坏、上下文遭操纵或涌现的目标偏移等原因，行为偏离授权范围，且未被检测或遏制
Runtime Assurance
运行时保障
Continuous verification against behavioural baselines; session isolation and memory validation; graduated containment upon deviation A harmful or unintended agent action cannot be stopped, paused, or reversed because no override mechanism exists or the agent can circumvent it
依据行为基线开展持续验证；会话隔离与记忆验证；发生偏离时实施分级遏制。由于不存在接管机制，或智能体能够绕过该机制，有害或非预期的智能体行动无法被停止、暂停或撤销
Interruptibility Non-tamperable kill switches at task, session, and system levels; rollback capabilities for consequential actions; override mechanisms architecturally independent of agent control Agent reasoning and decision processes are opaque to deployers, operators, users, and affected parties, preventing meaningful scrutiny or trust calibration
可中断性。在任务、会话和系统层面设置不可篡改的紧急停止开关；为具有重大影响的行动提供回滚能力；设置在架构上独立于智能体控制的接管机制。智能体的推理和决策过程对部署方、运营方、用户及受影响方不透明，妨碍了有实质意义的审查或信任校准
Legibility Trajectory-level trace diagnostics; standardised observability infrastructure for agent workflows; chain-of-thought monitoring Agents take consequential, high-stakes, or irreversible actions without meaningful human review at the points where such review is warranted, whether by task design or by unexpected operational conditions
可理解性。轨迹级追踪诊断；面向智能体工作流的标准化可观测性基础设施；思维链监测。无论是由于任务设计，还是由于意外的运行条件，智能体都会在应当进行有实质意义的人工审查的节点，未经此类审查就采取具有重大影响、高风险或不可逆的行动
Human-in-the-Loop Oversight
人在回路监督
Approval gates for high-stakes, irreversible, or outlier actions; tiered autonomy levels with defined escalation criteria; interaction design that preserves meaningful review capacity against automation bias and cognitive overload 67
针对高风险、不可逆或异常行动设置审批关卡；设置具有明确升级处置标准的分级自主权限；通过交互设计抵御自动化偏见和认知过载，保留有实质意义的审查能力 67

1. design and development1. 设计与开发
Design and development is the initial lifecycle stage, in which an agentic sys-tem’s specifications are established and implemented into technical capabilities and controls. It is at this stage that foundational architectural decisions become locked in, and where technical controls can be embedded to mitigate risks [Singapore IMDA Model AI Governance Framework for Agentic AI]. The relevance of design-stage governance to risk management is well-established across both international standards such as ISO/ IEC 42001:2023, ISO/IEC 22989:2022 and IEEE 7000:2021, and in regulation including the EU AI Act Article 9.5 (a). For agentic systems specifically, governance at the design stage carries height-ened significance: because these systems are characterised by goal-directed autonomy, tool use, and environmental interaction, the opportunity to implement meaningful controls can diminish substantially once they are deployed. It is accordingly this design and devel-opment stage where the strongest convergence exists not only in governance principles, but also in technical practices already adopted by industry and research actors.
设计与开发是生命周期的初始阶段，在这一阶段，智能体系统的规格得以确立，并被落实为技术能力与控制措施。正是在这一阶段，基础性架构决策被固定下来，也可以嵌入技术控制措施以缓解风险 [Singapore IMDA Model AI Governance Framework for Agentic AI]。设计阶段治理与风险管理的相关性，已在 ISO/ IEC 42001:2023、ISO/IEC 22989:2022 和 IEEE 7000:2021 等国际标准，以及《欧盟人工智能法案》第 9.5 (a) 条等法规中得到充分确立。具体而言，对于智能体系统，设计阶段的治理尤为重要：由于这些系统具有目标导向的自主性、工具使用和环境交互等特征，一旦部署，实施有实质意义的控制措施的机会就可能大幅减少。因此，正是在设计与开发阶段，不仅治理原则呈现出最强的趋同性，业界和研究界已经采用的技术实践也呈现出最强的趋同性。
Principle 1: Least Privilege原则 1：最小权限
what is it其含义
For autonomous agents, trustworthy task delegation requires the guarantee that their actions remain within appropriate scope—and that authorised actors can constrain that scope when needed. The principle of least privilege requires that an agent’s capabilities are scoped to the minimum necessary for its current task and context, and that this scope adjusts as the task evolves. This extends the enforcement of least privilege from static permission minimisation to dynamic capability restric-tion and execution isolation, thereby limiting the impact of compromised or misbehaving agents, and ensuring that access escalation requires explicit authorisation rather than being inherited from broader permissions assigned at deployment.
对于自主智能体，可信赖的任务委派要求保证其行动始终处于适当范围之内，并保证获授权的主体能够在需要时限制这一范围。最小权限原则要求将智能体的能力范围限定为其当前任务和情境所必需的最低限度，并随着任务的演变调整这一范围。这将最小权限的实施从静态的权限最小化扩展到动态的能力限制和执行隔离，从而限制遭入侵或行为异常的智能体所造成的影响，并确保访问权限的提升必须经过明确授权，而不能继承部署时分配的更广泛权限。
Practical guidance for implementing Least Privilege
实施最小权限原则的实践指南
Agent developers: scope permissions to the current task rather than to a persistent agent identity, and which actions fall outside scope entirely; build pre-execution tool-access checks and rate-limits to surface attempts to escalate privileges.
智能体开发者：根据当前任务而非持久的智能体身份界定权限范围，并明确哪些行动完全超出范围；构建执行前的工具访问检查和速率限制机制，以暴露权限提升尝试。
Deployers: configure scoping to its operational context, set the explicit reauthorisation gates for out-of-scope actions, and ensure agents do not run with excessive host-level access.
部署方：根据运行情境配置权限范围，为超出范围的行动设置明确的重新授权关卡，并确保智能体不会以过高的主机级访问权限运行。
Cloud platforms: provide the isolated execution environments, with restricted filesystem and network access, that deployer-side enforcement needs.
云平台：提供部署方实施控制所需的隔离执行环境，并限制其文件系统和网络访问。
Shared responsibilities: whoever controls the product surface should enforce execution isolation: the agent developer or a vertically integrated model provider if the product is theirs; otherwise the deployer and cloud platform. 68
共同责任：控制产品交互界面的一方应实施执行隔离：如果产品归智能体开发者或垂直整合的模型提供商所有，则由其负责；否则，由部署方和云平台负责。68
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Governance context治理背景
The principle of least privilege represents one of the clearest areas of emerg-ing consensus in agentic AI risk management. Singapore’s Infocomm Media Development Authority (IMDA) Model AI Governance Framework for Agentic AI calls on developers to “apply the principle of least privilege to limit tools available to each agent, enforced through robust authentication and authorisation”. This stance is mirrored in the Cybersecurity Agency of Singapore Draft Addendum on Securing Agentic AI ,which advises organisations to “scope [agentic] execution privileges strictly only to what is necessary, ensuring that privileges are customised to each agent within a system”. The US National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) and its Center for AI Standards and Innovation (CAISI) , in their work on agent tool use with the Artificial Intelligence Safety Institute Consortium, suggest granular gradations for trust levels depending on agent implementation settings. Focusing on open source agentic systems,
最小权限原则是智能体 AI 风险管理中正在形成共识的最明确领域之一。新加坡资讯通信媒体发展局（IMDA）的《智能体 AI 治理示范框架》呼吁开发者“应用最小权限原则，限制每个智能体可用的工具，并通过健全的身份验证和授权机制加以落实”。新加坡网络安全局的《智能体 AI 安全保障补充文件草案》也体现了这一立场，建议组织“将［智能体的］执行权限严格限定在必要范围内，确保针对系统内的每个智能体定制权限”。美国国家标准与技术研究院（NIST）及其 AI 标准与创新中心（CAISI）在与人工智能安全研究所联盟共同开展的智能体工具使用研究中，建议根据智能体的具体实施环境，对信任级别进行精细分级。聚焦于开源智能体系统，
China ’s Artificial Intelligence Industry Alliance (AIIA) released the OpenClaw-Type Agent Deployment Risk Management Guide, which recommends strictly limiting the scope of the agent’s operations as well as a periodic review of permissions. Similarly, China’s Ministry of Industry and Information Technology issued risk mitigation recommendations which direct deployers to enforce least privilege for task completion and prohibit the use of administrator privileges by agentic systems. While least privilege is not yet an enforceable regulation, these frameworks position it not merely as a best practice, but as a baseline requirement for agentic security.
中国人工智能产业发展联盟（AIIA）发布了《OpenClaw 类智能体部署风险管理指南》，建议严格限制智能体的操作范围，并定期审查权限。同样，中国工业和信息化部发布了风险缓解建议，要求部署方在完成任务时落实最小权限原则，并禁止智能体系统使用管理员权限。尽管最小权限原则尚未成为具有强制执行力的法规要求，但这些框架将其定位为智能体安全的基线要求，而不仅仅是一项最佳实践。
Illustrative Cases示例案例
Agentic system developer frameworks operationalise this principle through increas-ingly granular implementation guidance. OpenAI ’s Practices for Governing Agentic AI Systems frames least privilege through action-space constraints, arguing that cer-tain actions should be excluded from an agent’s operational envelope entirely, while others should require explicit human reauthorisation. Google ’s Approach for Secure AI Agents
智能体系统开发者的框架通过日益细化的实施指导，将这一原则落实为具体操作。OpenAI 的《智能体 AI 系统治理实践》从行动空间约束的角度阐述最小权限原则，主张将某些行动完全排除在智能体的操作范围之外，而其他行动则应要求人类明确重新授权。Google 的《安全 AI 智能体方法》
extends this by distinguishing agentic least privilege from the principle’s traditional application: rather than minimising the agent’s permissions once, in a static way, permis-sions must instead be dynamically aligned with the agent’s task and current deployer and user intent. One example of developers implementing least privilege is the Agent Opera-tion Authorization framework co-authored by Alibaba , Cisco, and Okta. This framework employs fine-grained access controls centred on agent behaviour, with provisions for secure multi-hop delegation chains. Amazon Web Services’ (AWS) response to the NIST/CAISI request for information on securing agentic AI presents AWS Iden-tity and Access Management as an example of least-privilege authorisation enforced at the infrastructure level in an agentic context. From the security community’s side, the Open Worldwide Application Security Project (OWASP) guide on Agentic AI Threats and Mitigations prescribes specific controls: validate tool access before execution, rate-limit agent tool calls, and detect any attempts to circumvent least-privilege boundaries. 69
在此基础上进一步区分了智能体的最小权限原则与该原则的传统应用方式：权限不应仅以静态方式一次性最小化，而应根据智能体的任务以及部署方和用户当前的意图进行动态调整。开发者落实最小权限原则的一个例子是由 Alibaba、Cisco 和 Okta 共同编写的智能体操作授权（Agent Operation Authorization）框架。该框架采用以智能体行为为中心的细粒度访问控制，并为安全的多跳委托链提供相应机制。Amazon Web Services（AWS）在回应 NIST/CAISI 关于保障智能体 AI 安全的信息征询时，将 AWS Identity and Access Management 作为在智能体应用场景中于基础设施层面实施最小权限授权的示例。在安全社区方面，开放全球应用安全项目（OWASP）的《智能体 AI 威胁与缓解措施》指南规定了具体控制措施：在执行前验证工具访问权限，对智能体的工具调用实施速率限制，并检测任何试图绕过最小权限边界的行为。69
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Leading labs have independently converged on execution isolation as a comple-ment to permission controls. Anthropic ’s work on Claude Code sandboxing argues that effective execution isolation requires two distinct surfaces working together: filesys-tem isolation, which prevents modification of sensitive system files, and network isolation, which prevents exfiltration of sensitive data. Without both, a compromised agent can use one surface to escape the other. OpenAI ’s GPT-5.3-Codex System Card specifies that Codex agents operate within isolated, secure environments, with network access disabled by default and file edits restricted to the active workspace. The sandbox implementation varies by deployment: containerised environments in the cloud, and OS-level enforcement locally. Similarly, DeepSeek’ s V4 preview introduces the DeepSeek Elastic Compute platform which manages concurrent sandbox instances and allows isolation to be calibrated to workload and security requirements. In its response to the NIST/CAISI , Google proposes extending isolation to the hardware layer to govern the agent’s access to sensitive data, deploying agent workloads within Trusted Execution Environments where data remains encrypted even during processing. Not all least-privilege controls can be enforced at the developer level. When agents are deployed in environments that the developer does not control, whether through open APIs or open source distribution, the risk management responsibility partly shifts to the deployer. Anthropic ’s Computer Use Tool API documentation illustrates this from the platform side, advising deployers to run agents in dedicated virtual machines or containers with minimal privileges to prevent direct system attacks or accidents.
领先实验室各自独立地形成了一致认识，即将执行隔离作为权限控制的补充。Anthropic 关于 Claude Code 沙箱化的研究指出，有效的执行隔离需要两个不同层面的机制协同运作：文件系统隔离，防止修改敏感的系统文件；网络隔离，防止敏感数据外泄。如果这两种隔离机制未能同时具备，遭到入侵的智能体就可能利用其中一个层面突破另一个层面的隔离。OpenAI 的《GPT-5.3-Codex 系统卡》明确指出，Codex 智能体在隔离的安全环境中运行，默认禁用网络访问，并将文件编辑限制在当前工作区内。沙箱的实现方式因部署环境而异：云端采用容器化环境，本地则通过操作系统级机制强制实施。同样，DeepSeek 的 V4 预览版引入了 DeepSeek Elastic Compute 平台，该平台管理并发运行的沙箱实例，并允许根据工作负载和安全要求调整隔离程度。Google 在对 NIST/CAISI 的回应中提议，将隔离扩展至硬件层，以管控智能体对敏感数据的访问，并将智能体工作负载部署在可信执行环境（Trusted Execution Environments）中，使数据即使在处理过程中也保持加密状态。并非所有最小权限控制措施都能在开发者层面强制实施。当智能体被部署到开发者无法控制的环境中时，无论是通过开放 API 还是开源分发，风险管理责任都会部分转移至部署者。Anthropic 的 Computer Use Tool API 文档从平台角度说明了这一点，建议部署者在具有最低权限的专用虚拟机或容器中运行智能体，以防止针对系统的直接攻击或意外事件。
Huawei Cloud ’s security recommendations for OpenClaw document the risks of deploying agentic systems without execution isolation: the agent runs with user permissions directly on the host machine, and once compromised, the attacker gains full control of the host. The recommendations include using a virtual private cloud and subnet iso-lation, cautioning deployers “not to grant permissions with a single click to save time”. Following the surge in adoption of OpenClaw, the AI Agent Security Practice Guidelines ,jointly released by the China Academy of Information and Communications Technology (CAICT) and Tencent Cloud , call on deployers to establish a security baseline for agentic frameworks prior to deployment, including enforcing least privilege to prevent agents from gaining excess permissions. These cases illustrate that as open source and open weight agentic AI proliferates, least privilege cannot be enforced by developers alone. Govern-ance frameworks must turn the same scrutiny on deployers, who in practice control what agents can do and under what constraints.
Huawei Cloud 针对 OpenClaw 的安全建议记录了在缺乏执行隔离的情况下部署智能体系统的风险：智能体以用户权限直接在宿主机上运行，一旦遭到攻陷，攻击者便可完全控制宿主机。这些建议包括使用虚拟私有云和子网隔离，并告诫部署者“不要为了节省时间而一键授予权限”。随着 OpenClaw 的采用激增，中国信息通信研究院（CAICT）与 Tencent Cloud 联合发布的《AI 智能体安全实践指南》呼吁部署者在部署前为智能体框架建立安全基线，包括落实最小权限原则，以防止智能体获得过多权限。这些案例表明，随着开源和开放权重的智能体 AI 日益普及，仅靠开发者无法落实最小权限原则。治理框架必须对部署者施以同等审视，因为在实践中，部署者控制着智能体能够做什么，以及在何种约束下行动。
Principle 2: Traceable Identity原则 2：可追溯身份
what is it其含义
As agents plan and execute tasks autonomously across tools, services, and other agents, we need to be able to establish which agent did what, on whose behalf, and with what authorisation. The principle of traceable identity refers to the use of unique, verifi-able identifiers for agentic systems together with credentials that are positively scoped, time-limited, and non-transferable by the agent. These identifiers and credentials 70
随着智能体跨工具、服务和其他智能体自主规划并执行任务，我们需要能够确定哪个智能体做了什么、代表谁行动，以及获得了何种授权。可追溯身份原则是指，为智能体系统使用唯一且可验证的标识符，并配以授权范围明确限定、具有时限且智能体不可转让的凭证。这些标识符和凭证 70
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
serve three functions: enabling counterparties to verify the identity of the entity with which they are interacting; attributing actions to the relevant agent or deployment after the fact; and supporting intervention when a system malfunctions or causes harm.
发挥三项作用：使交互对方能够核实与其交互的实体的身份；在事后将行为归因于相关智能体或部署实例；以及在系统发生故障或造成损害时支持干预。
Practical guidance for implementing Traceable Identity
落实可追溯身份的实践指导
> Model developers: supply verifiable provenance and capability attributes for the underlying model, and signal material changes to the model, such as fine-tuning or capability upgrades, that trigger re-attestation.
> 模型开发者：提供底层模型的可验证来源信息和能力属性，并通报模型的重大变更，例如会触发重新认证的微调或能力升级。
> Agent developers: build the credential issuance systems and the interfaces through which agents are authenticated; design delegated authority flows where agents carry verifiable proof of who authorised them and their scope.
> 智能体开发者：构建凭证签发系统和用于验证智能体身份的接口；设计授权委托流程，使智能体携带可验证的证明，表明谁向其授予了权限，以及授权范围。
> Deployers: configure identity policies to agents’ operational context and manage the credential lifecycle, including re-attestation when agents or their underlying models change.
> 部署者：根据智能体的运行情境配置身份策略，并管理凭证生命周期，包括在智能体或其底层模型发生变化时进行重新认证。
> Cloud platforms: provide trusted runtime environments to which credentials are bound, so that compromised credentials cannot be used outside their intended deployment context.
> 云平台：提供与凭证绑定的可信运行时环境，使遭到泄露或盗用的凭证无法在预定部署情境之外使用。
> Shared responsibilities: where agents outlive the user session that originally authorised them, organisations need clear policies on whether credentials expire, transfer, or require re-authorisation.
> 共同责任：当智能体的存续时间超出最初授权它们的用户会话时，组织需要制定明确政策，规定凭证是否到期、转移或需要重新授权。
Governance context治理背景
Governance frameworks across jurisdictions are increasingly converging on agent identity as a foundational requirement for the safe, accountable, and trustworthy deployment of agentic systems. The transparency principle reflected in the EU AI Act Article 13, though formally applicable to a defined category of high-risk AI sys-tems under the regulation, establish a normative baseline that is directly relevant to agentic deployments: identity mechanisms are foundational for disclosing agentic “characteristics, capabilities and limitations of performance” as well as for ensuring that agentic operations are “sufficiently transparent to enable deployers to interpret a system’s output and use it appropriately”. The same approach is developed in Sin-gapore IMDA ’s Model AI Governance Framework For Agentic AI , which establishes identity as a core measure to bound agentic risks upfront and states that, as agents become more autonomous, “identity management has to be extended to agents…to track individual agent behaviour and establish who holds accountability for each agent”. Together with the launch of the US CAISI/NIST AI Agent Standards Initiative in 2026, the US National Cybersecurity Center of Excellence stipulates that the benefits of broadly adopting agentic systems “cannot be realized without the ability to understand how identity principles such as identification, authentication, and authorization can apply to agents” [Accelerating the Adoption of Software and AI Agent Identity and Authoriza-tion]. In parallel, the Cyberspace Administration of China ’s 2026 implementation guide on AI agents calls for a centralised agent registration platform where an agent’s developer, deployment method, interface protocols, capability declarations, and compliance cer-tification can be queried. China’s National Technical Committee on Information Technology (TC28) has already begun developing a series of national standards called 71
各司法管辖区的治理框架正日益形成共识，将智能体身份视为安全、可问责且可信地部署智能体系统的一项基础要求。欧盟《人工智能法案》第 13 条所体现的透明度原则，虽然在形式上适用于该法规所界定的一类高风险 AI 系统，但确立了一项与智能体部署直接相关的规范基线：身份机制是披露智能体“特征、能力和性能局限”的基础，也是确保智能体运行“具有足够的透明度，使部署者能够解读系统输出并恰当使用系统”的基础。新加坡 IMDA 的《智能体 AI 治理示范框架》进一步阐述了同样的思路，将身份确立为预先约束智能体风险的核心措施，并指出，随着智能体自主性增强，“身份管理必须扩展至智能体……以追踪单个智能体的行为，并确定谁对每个智能体承担责任”。伴随美国 CAISI/NIST 于 2026 年启动 AI 智能体标准倡议，美国国家网络安全卓越中心明确指出，如果“无法理解身份识别、身份验证和授权等身份原则如何适用于智能体”，就“无法实现”广泛采用智能体系统所带来的益处 [Accelerating the Adoption of Software and AI Agent Identity and Authoriza-tion]。与此同时，中国国家互联网信息办公室的 2026 年人工智能智能体实施指南呼吁建立一个集中式智能体注册平台，以便查询智能体的开发者、部署方式、接口协议、能力声明和合规认证。中国全国信息技术标准化技术委员会（TC28）已开始制定一系列国家标准，名为 71
