Agent Interconnection. Its projects, Agent Identity Code and Agent Identity Management ,aim to establish unified protocols for agent package and instance identifiers, registration, credential issuance, and authentication.
智能体互联。其《智能体身份编码》和《智能体身份管理》项目旨在为智能体软件包及实例的标识符、注册、凭证签发和身份验证建立统一协议。
Illustrative Cases示例案例
Beyond governance frameworks, major developers, including cloud platforms, have independently implemented agent identity management as a core product capa-bility. As demonstrated in Microsoft ’s Taxonomy of Failure Modes in Agentic AI Systems ,identity is critical to mitigating failure modes around impersonation, transparency, and permissions. Google Cloud ’s Vertex AI Agent Engine operationalises this through per-agent identity principals which are secured through binding them to trusted runtime environments, meaning that agents cannot reuse stolen credentials. Similarly, Alib-aba Cloud ’s agent identity service provides end-to-end identity management from the prototype stage to agent deployment, protecting access credentials and provid-ing secure agent access to Alibaba Cloud and third-party services. This convergence suggests that agent identity management is maturing from an application-level concern into shared cloud platform infrastructure. Reinforcing this trend, agent governance frameworks have begun articulating the specific threat models that agent identity must address. Cloud Security Alli-ance ’s framework on Agentic AI Identity and Access Management established the need for identity credentials that include verifiable provenance, reputation, purpose, and capability of an AI agent, responding to risks such as identity spoofing, reus-ing credentials to escalate privileges, and agents chaining delegations across one another to obtain unauthorised access. In many contexts, specific agents only exist temporarily, making it challenging to assign them a distinct identity: orchestration pat-terns increasingly spawn short-lived task-specific agents that can be created, cloned, and destroyed rapidly, making persistent credentials operationally infeasible and requiring ephemeral authentication mechanisms instead [A Novel Zero-Trust Identity Framework for Agentic AI]. Collectively, these frameworks build upon platform-spe-cific implementations toward formalised risk taxonomies for agent identity governance.
除治理框架之外，包括云平台在内的主要开发者也已独立将智能体身份管理实现为核心产品能力。正如 Microsoft 的《智能体人工智能系统失效模式分类》所展示的，身份对于缓解冒充、透明度和权限方面的失效模式至关重要。Google Cloud 的 Vertex AI Agent Engine 通过为每个智能体设置身份主体，将这一理念付诸实施；这些身份主体通过与可信运行时环境绑定来获得安全保障，这意味着智能体无法重复使用被盗凭证。同样，Alibaba Cloud 的智能体身份服务提供从原型阶段到智能体部署的端到端身份管理，保护访问凭证，并为智能体安全访问 Alibaba Cloud 和第三方服务提供支持。这种趋同表明，智能体身份管理正从应用层面的关注事项逐步发展为共享的云平台基础设施。智能体治理框架已开始阐明智能体身份必须应对的具体威胁模型，进一步强化了这一趋势。Cloud Security Alliance 的《智能体人工智能身份与访问管理》框架明确指出，身份凭证需要包含人工智能智能体可验证的来源、声誉、目的和能力，以应对身份欺骗、重复使用凭证来提升权限，以及智能体之间通过链式委托获取未授权访问等风险。在许多情境下，特定智能体仅临时存在，因此难以为其分配独立身份：编排模式越来越多地生成生命周期短、面向特定任务的智能体，这些智能体可以被快速创建、克隆和销毁，导致持久凭证在实际运行中不可行，因而需要采用临时身份验证机制 [A Novel Zero-Trust Identity Framework for Agentic AI]。总体而言，这些框架以各平台的具体实现为基础，推动形成用于智能体身份治理的形式化风险分类体系。
Shanghai AI Lab and Concordia AI ’s Frontier AI Risk Management Framework 1.5
上海人工智能实验室与 Concordia AI 的《前沿人工智能风险管理框架 1.5》
emphasises that agent identification can be used for monitoring, ensuring that agents’ behaviours are transparent, traceable, and controllable throughout their lifecycle. Finally, recent research has converged on delegated authority as the central ele-ment of agent identity. The OpenID Foundation ’s framework for Identity Management for Agentic AI argues that agents should no longer act as if they are the user. Instead, agents should use explicit “on-behalf-of” delegation—proving both who they are and what they are permitted to do, while remaining identifiable as agents, not as the user. Researchers have proposed extensions to OAuth 2.0 and OpenID Connect 1 that introduce agent-specific credentials and translate natural language permissions into verifiable access 1 OAuth 2.0 and OpenID Connect are industry standards for delegated authorisation and authentication, widely used for ‘Log in with Google’ type flows. 72
强调，智能体身份识别可用于监测，确保智能体在整个生命周期内的行为透明、可追溯且可控。最后，近期研究已一致将委托权限视为智能体身份的核心要素。OpenID Foundation 的《智能体人工智能身份管理》框架认为，智能体不应再以仿佛自己就是用户的方式行事。相反，智能体应使用明确的“代表用户”（on-behalf-of）委托机制，既证明自身身份，也证明其获准执行的操作，同时始终能够被识别为智能体，而非用户。研究人员提出了对 OAuth 2.0 和 OpenID Connect 1 的扩展，引入智能体专用凭证，并将自然语言权限转换为可验证的访问 1 OAuth 2.0 和 OpenID Connect 是用于委托授权和身份验证的行业标准，广泛用于“使用 Google 登录”一类流程。72
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
control configurations, demonstrating this is feasible within existing web architecture [Authenticated Delegation and Authorized AI Agents]. Building on this, the JWT Authoriza-tion Grant Interaction Response draft maintains a clear separation between agent identity and user identity throughout the authorisation chain, using cryptographically signed grants and explicit user-consent flows. Adjacent research situates these mecha-nisms within a broader concept of agent infrastructure: a system of external protocols and systems that will serve to attribute actions to specific agents or their principals, shape agent interactions, and detect and remedy harmful behaviour [Infrastructure for AI Agents]. These patterns are being implemented by developers and infrastruc-ture providers: Anthropic ’s Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables on-behalf-of flows in which agents carry scoped, verifiable credentials, and its enterprise extension lets an organisation’s existing identity provider govern which tools an agent may reach on a user’s behalf [Model Context Protocol Enterprise-Managed Authorization]. Ope-nAI ’s ChatGPT agent implements a complementary mechanism at the network layer, cryptographically signing all outbound HTTP requests using HTTP Message Signatures standard (RFC 9421) so that any receiving service can verify that the request authenti-cally originated from the agent rather than an impersonator. Similarly, Cloudflare ’s Web Bot Auth applies components of the same standard to verify requests from signed agents, allowing receiving sites to verify request provenance through published cryptographic keys rather than identity-provider relationships. Rather than relying on any single mechanism, the current best practices on Agent IDs combine delegated authority, cryptographic authentication, and infrastructure-level attribution to create a regime of traceability by design.
控制配置，证明这在现有 Web 架构内是可行的 [Authenticated Delegation and Authorized AI Agents]。在此基础上，《JWT 授权授予交互响应》草案通过采用加密签名的授权授予和明确的用户同意流程，在整个授权链中始终清晰区分智能体身份与用户身份。相关研究将这些机制置于更广泛的智能体基础设施概念之中：这是一套由外部协议和系统构成的体系，用于将行为归属于特定智能体或其委托主体、塑造智能体之间的交互，并检测和纠正有害行为 [Infrastructure for AI Agents]。开发者和基础设施提供商正在实施这些模式：Anthropic 的 Model Context Protocol（MCP）支持代表用户执行操作的流程，在此类流程中，智能体携带限定权限范围且可验证的凭证；其企业扩展则允许组织现有的身份提供商管控智能体可以代表用户访问哪些工具 [Model Context Protocol Enterprise-Managed Authorization]。OpenAI 的 ChatGPT 智能体在网络层实现了一种互补机制，使用 HTTP 消息签名（HTTP Message Signatures）标准（RFC 9421）对所有出站 HTTP 请求进行密码学签名，使任何接收服务都能验证请求确实源自该智能体，而非冒充者。类似地，Cloudflare 的 Web Bot Auth 利用同一标准的部分组件来验证经过签名的智能体所发出的请求，使接收网站能够通过公开发布的密码学密钥验证请求来源，而无需依赖与身份提供方的关系。当前有关智能体 ID（Agent IDs）的最佳实践并不依赖任何单一机制，而是将委托权限、密码学身份认证和基础设施层面的来源归属相结合，建立一种在设计阶段就确保可追溯性的机制。
Principle 3: Auditability原则 3：可审计性
what is it其含义
Autonomous agents execute extended chains of actions that may be difficult to recon-struct after the fact. Auditability is the principle that an agent’s decisions and actions must remain reconstructible and verifiable by authorised parties throughout and after execution. It is tied to the mechanisms of logging, or building audit trails, as well as tamper-evidence and post-incident reconstruction of decisions and actions across the agent’s execution chain.
自主智能体会执行较长的行动链，事后可能难以重建这些行动链。可审计性是指，智能体的决策和行动在执行期间及执行结束后，都必须始终能够由获授权方重建和验证。这一原则涉及日志记录或审计轨迹构建机制，以及篡改可察觉机制和在事件发生后重建智能体整个执行链中的决策与行动。
Practical guidance for implementing Auditability
落实可审计性的实践指南
> Agent developers : build logging architecture that captures inputs, tool invocations, parameters, outputs, outcomes, and reasoning traces (goal decomposition, rejected plans, and the influence of retrieved information on decisions), together with the user-facing audit interfaces that let end users inspect what the agent did on their behalf.
> 智能体开发者：构建日志架构，记录输入、工具调用、参数、输出、结果和推理轨迹（目标分解、被否决的计划，以及检索信息对决策的影响），同时构建面向用户的审计界面，让最终用户能够查看智能体代表他们执行了哪些操作。
> Deployers : configure logging to the agent’s operational context, including tamper-evidence and log protection, and use monitoring and alerting on audit data to support incident response.
> 部署方：根据智能体的运行环境配置日志记录，包括篡改可察觉机制和日志保护，并对审计数据进行监控和告警，以支持事件响应。
> Shared responsibilities : in multi-agent deployments, keeping audit trails coherent across agents and organisational boundaries falls to whoever operates the orchestration layer. 73
> 共同责任：在多智能体部署中，确保跨智能体及跨组织边界的审计轨迹保持连贯，是编排层运营方的责任。73
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Governance context治理背景
While logging requirements apply broadly to AI systems, they are especially critical in the context of agents. Agents execute tool calls, delegate to subagents, and take actions in the real world, meaning that audit trails must reconstruct not merely a model output but an extended decision and action chain. The Organisation for Economic Co-op-eration and Development (OECD) AI Principles establish traceability as a foundation for accountability, emphasising the need to maintain records that enable “analysis and inquiry into the outcomes of an AI system”. China’s National Technical Committee 260 on Cybersecurity (TC260) AI Safety and Governance Framework 2.0 translates this into concrete operational requirements, mandating that operators “maintain operational logs for large model services, including system and user activities”, while the secu-rity recommendations for OpenClaw by the National Computer Network Emergency Response Technical Team/Coordination Center of China (CNCERT/CC) call for establish-ing a “complete operational log audit mechanism” for open source agentic applications. The EU AI Act’s Article 12, titled “Record-Keeping”, codifies the governance function most explicitly, requiring that high-risk AI systems “technically allow for the auto-matic recording of events (logs) over the lifetime of the system” in order to identify emerging risks, facilitate post-market monitoring, and enable operational oversight. Across jurisdictions, the regulators and standards bodies are converging on a common baseline: audit trails must be automatic, tamper-evident, and comprehensive enough to reconstruct an autonomous system’s decision chain for audit and incident response.
尽管日志记录要求普遍适用于 AI 系统，但在智能体场景中尤为关键。智能体会执行工具调用、向子智能体委派任务并在现实世界中采取行动，这意味着审计轨迹必须能够重建的不仅是模型输出，还包括较长的决策与行动链。经济合作与发展组织（OECD）的《人工智能原则》将可追溯性确立为问责的基础，强调需要保留能够支持“对 AI 系统的结果进行分析和调查”的记录。中国全国网络安全标准化技术委员会（TC260）的《人工智能安全治理框架》2.0 将这一原则转化为具体的运行要求，规定运营方必须“保留大模型服务的运行日志，包括系统和用户活动”；与此同时，中国国家计算机网络应急技术处理协调中心（CNCERT/CC）针对 OpenClaw 的安全建议要求为开源智能体应用建立“完整的运行日志审计机制”。欧盟《人工智能法案》第 12 条题为“记录保存”，最明确地将这一治理功能写入法律，要求高风险 AI 系统“在技术上允许在系统整个生命周期内自动记录事件（日志）”，以识别新出现的风险、促进上市后监测并支持运行监督。各司法管辖区的监管机构和标准制定机构正逐渐形成共同的基准要求：审计轨迹必须自动生成、能够显现篡改痕迹，并且足够全面，能够重建自主系统的决策链，以供审计和事件响应使用。
Illustrative Cases示例案例
Among emerging developer approaches, Google addresses auditability at the operator layer, establishing robust logging across the agent’s architecture: capturing inputs, tool invocations, parameters, outputs, and where feasible, intermediate reasoning steps [Google’s Approach for Secure AI Agents]. Critically, the framework recognises that logs themselves become attack surfaces, since they may contain sensitive tool parame-ters and user inputs, and must therefore be secured independently. Operator-grade logging alone, however, does not ensure full transparency. OpenAI ’s Practices for Governing Agentic AI Systems calls for providing users with an action ledger to enable the user to audit the agent’s actions without imposing the latency cost of step-by-step human approval. Together, the two approaches sketch complementary layers of auditability: secured internal logging infrastructure and user-facing ledgers built on top of it. Emerging frameworks address the integrity and actionability of audit data as distinct governance requirements. IBM ’s AGENTSAFE framework suggests that every agen-tic action, from plan formation to tool invocation, should be tied to a cryptographically anchored chain of evidence, making the audit logs tamper-proof, treating auditability as a “core governance function”. Elements of this infrastructure are already being implemented, for example, in Alibaba Cloud ’s records of all identity resource operations, includ-ing credential creation, modification, and retrieval through its ActionTrail service, applying tamper-evident logging to the agent identity layer specifically. The Coalition 74
在新兴的开发者实践中，Google 在运营方层面落实可审计性，在智能体的整个架构中建立健全的日志记录机制：记录输入、工具调用、参数、输出，以及在可行情况下记录中间推理步骤 [Google’s Approach for Secure AI Agents]。关键在于，该框架认识到，日志本身也会成为攻击面，因为其中可能包含敏感的工具参数和用户输入，因此必须单独对其实施安全保护。然而，仅有运营级日志记录并不能确保完全透明。OpenAI 的《智能体 AI 系统治理实践》倡导向用户提供行动台账，使用户能够审计智能体的行动，同时避免逐步要求人工批准所带来的延迟成本。这两种方法共同勾勒出可审计性的两个互补层面：受到安全保护的内部日志基础设施，以及建立在其之上的面向用户的台账。新兴框架将审计数据的完整性和可用于采取行动的程度视为两项独立的治理要求。IBM 的 AGENTSAFE 框架建议，从计划形成到工具调用，每一项智能体行动都应关联到一条以密码学为锚的证据链，使审计日志不可篡改，并将可审计性视为一项“核心治理职能”。这一基础设施的部分要素已开始实施，例如，阿里云通过其 ActionTrail 服务记录所有身份资源操作，包括凭证的创建、修改和检索，将可检测篡改的日志记录机制专门应用于智能体身份层。联盟 74
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
for Secure AI (CoSAI) Principles for Secure-by-Design Agentic Systems extend verifiabil-ity to the supply chain, calling for verifiable provenance for agent and model artifacts, integrated tools, and the data that defines agent behaviour. Finally, in order to make audit data actionable, the Shanghai AI Laboratory and Concordia AI ’s Frontier AI Risk Management Framework 1.5 emphasise the need for anomaly alerts and dashboard infrastructure that facilitate the use of audit trails in incident response and in post-inci-dent analysis. Auditability (as mandated by regulatory frameworks) is, therefore, implemented through operator-grade logging and user-facing ledgers (implemented at different levels of the deployment stack), tamper-evidence, causal attribution, and post-incident analysis infra-structure. Jointly, these capabilities serve the governance objective of ensuring that agentic activity remains reconstructible and verifiable. 75
安全人工智能联盟（CoSAI）的《安全设计智能体系统原则》将可验证性扩展至供应链，要求为智能体和模型制品、集成工具以及定义智能体行为的数据提供可验证的来源信息。最后，为使审计数据能够支持实际行动，上海人工智能实验室与 Concordia AI 的《前沿人工智能风险管理框架 1.5》强调，需要建立异常告警和仪表板基础设施，以便在事件响应和事后分析中使用审计轨迹。因此，监管框架所要求的可审计性，是通过面向运营方的专业级日志记录和面向用户的账本（在部署技术栈的不同层级实现）、篡改可检测机制、因果归因以及事后分析基础设施来实现的。这些能力共同服务于一项治理目标：确保智能体活动始终可重建、可验证。75
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
2. Testing and deployment2. 测试与部署
Testing and deployment is the lifecycle stage in which an agentic system’s capabilities and controls are validated against its specifications and the system is released into its operating environment , in accordance with ISO/IEC 42001:2023 and ISO/IEC 23894:2023. Agentic systems pose validation challenges that conventional AI evalua-tion frameworks were not designed to address: the difficulty of evaluating capability and autonomy in open-ended operating environments; new attack surfaces created by tool integration and autonomous decision-making; and emergent behaviours that arise from multi-step planning and interactions between agents. Nonetheless, com-pared to other lifecycle stages, testing and deployment for agentic systems is an area of active development with fewer established technical practices.
根据 ISO/IEC 42001:2023 和 ISO/IEC 23894:2023，测试与部署是生命周期中的一个阶段，在此阶段，依据智能体系统的规格要求验证其能力和控制措施，并将系统发布到运行环境中。智能体系统带来了传统人工智能评估框架在设计时未曾考虑的验证挑战：难以在开放式运行环境中评估能力和自主性；工具集成与自主决策产生新的攻击面；以及多步骤规划和智能体间交互引发涌现行为。尽管如此，与其他生命周期阶段相比，智能体系统的测试与部署仍是一个处于积极发展中的领域，成熟的技术实践相对较少。
Principle 4: Validated Deployment原则 4：经验证的部署
what is it其含义
Agentic systems operate across open-ended tasks and diverse ecosystems, and may exhibit emergent behaviours that are difficult to anticipate prior to deployment. Vali-dated deployment is the principle that agentic systems require evaluation and release criteria calibrated to their level of autonomy, the sensitivity of their operating domain, and the scope of capabilities they can exercise. This establishes deployment as a governed stage with clear criteria for release, update, and retraction of agentic systems.
智能体系统执行开放式任务，并在多样化的生态系统中运行，可能表现出部署前难以预见的涌现行为。经验证的部署这一原则要求，智能体系统的评估与发布标准应与其自主性水平、运行领域的敏感性以及可运用的能力范围相匹配。这使部署成为一个受治理的阶段，并为智能体系统的发布、更新和撤回确立明确标准。
Practical guidance for implementing Validated Deployment
实施经验证的部署的实践指南
Agent developers : build the evaluation infrastructure, including test environments and grading methodologies, as well as evaluation frameworks designed for assessing agents’ behaviour rather than just benchmarking model capabilities.
智能体开发者：构建评估基础设施，包括测试环境和评分方法，以及专门用于评估智能体行为的评估框架，而不只是对模型能力进行基准测试。
Deployers : set criteria appropriate to the agent’s intended operational context, including which autonomy level to grant and under what conditions to expand or restrict it.
部署方：制定适合智能体预期运行情境的标准，包括授予何种自主性水平，以及在什么条件下扩大或限制其自主性。
Third-party evaluators : independently verify that agents meet the deployment criteria by testing agents in dynamic, realistic environments that mirror real-world operating conditions.
第三方评估者：在模拟现实运行条件的动态、逼真环境中测试智能体，独立验证其是否满足部署标准。
Shared responsibilities : in multi-agent evaluation, detecting emergent interaction behaviours may require coordination across multiple developers.
共同责任：在多智能体评估中，检测涌现的交互行为可能需要多个开发者之间开展协调。
Governance context治理背景
Across major governance frameworks, validated deployment is emerging as a struc-tured obligation rather than an internal developer milestone. China’s TC260 AI Safety and Governance Framework 2.0 stipulates that safety classification should be based on scenario criticality, intelligence level, and scale of deployment. US NIST AI Risk Manage-ment Framework (AI RMF 1.0) similarly calls for minimum performance, as well as for 76
在各大治理框架中，经验证的部署正逐渐成为一项结构化义务，而非开发者的内部里程碑。中国 TC260《人工智能安全治理框架 2.0》规定，安全分级应以场景的关键程度、智能水平和部署规模为依据。美国 NIST《人工智能风险管理框架》（AI RMF 1.0）同样要求对最低性能，以及 76
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
risk and capability thresholds to be reviewed as part of explicit go/no-go deployment approval processes. The EU AI Act ’s Article 60 requires testing of high-risk AI sys-tems to follow a formal, regulated process—including registration, participant consent, and withdrawal plans—to ensure safety. These frameworks were designed to address generative AI systems broadly, but Singapore’s Government Technology Agency’s (GovTech) Agentic Risk and Capability Framework provides governance instruments designed specifically for agentic systems, introducing a capability-based approach that “enables flexible adaptation to new developments and emerging risks”. Across jurisdictions, a shared guideline is taking shape: the focus of risk management should not be on individual tools or underlying models, but on agents’ capabilities, autonomy, and operating context.
风险和能力阈值进行审查，并将其作为明确的部署放行／不放行审批流程的一部分。欧盟《人工智能法案》第 60 条要求，高风险人工智能系统的测试须遵循正式、受监管的流程，包括登记、参与者同意和退出计划，以确保安全。这些框架旨在广泛适用于生成式人工智能系统，而新加坡政府科技局（GovTech）的《智能体风险与能力框架》则提供了专为智能体系统设计的治理工具，引入一种基于能力的方法，“能够灵活适应新发展和新兴风险”。各司法管辖区正在形成一项共同准则：风险管理的重点应是智能体的能力、自主性及运行情境，而非单个工具或底层模型。
Illustrative Cases示例案例
Evaluating an agentic system’s capability and safety before deployment is important, but evaluating agentic systems requires different methods than for ordinary AI models. For example, AWS draws from their Bedrock AgentCore Evaluations to pro-pose that agentic evaluations need to extend beyond traditional accuracy metrics to include dimensions of agent quality, performance, responsibility, and cost. This approach specifically emphasises that each dimension should include human validation as a critical evaluation component. Anthropic’s roadmap for improving agentic evaluations sug-gests a methodical approach to grader selection: deterministic where possible, LLM grader where necessary, and human where additional validation is needed. Microsoft ’s response to the NIST/CAISI consultation argues that agentic systems require open-ended behavioural and scenario-based evaluation, while their work on evaluating AI agents in Microsoft Foundry frames evaluation as a layered practice, with the depth and frequency of each stage calibrated to the agent’s lifecycle and capability. A complementary regulatory framework applies the principle of proportionality to model evaluations, ensuring they provide meaningful risk information without imposing excessive burden on providers [The Science and Practice of Proportionality in AI Risk Evaluations]. Recent lab research has also examined how evaluation results should shape deployment decisions, matching agent capabilities against the risk profile of the intended operat-ing environment. The degree of autonomy granted, the sensitivity of the domain, and the scope of capabilities the agentic system can exercise all influence the deployment decisions. For example, as an agentic system’s autonomy, efficacy, goal complexity, and generality increase, verification becomes more challenging and human oversight requirements inten-sify [Characterizing AI Agents for Alignment and Governance]. AWS ’s Agentic AI Security Scoping Matrix operationalises this through the principle of “progressive autonomy deployment”, in which systems are integrated with mandatory human oversight and are designed to automatically reduce their autonomy level when security events are detected. GovTech’ s implementation guide for the Agentic Risk and Capability Framework
在部署前评估智能体系统的能力与安全性十分重要，但评估智能体系统所需的方法不同于评估普通 AI 模型的方法。例如，AWS 根据其 Bedrock AgentCore Evaluations 提出，智能体评估需要超越传统的准确性指标，纳入智能体质量、性能、责任和成本等维度。这一方法特别强调，每个维度都应将人工验证纳入其中，作为关键的评估组成部分。Anthropic 改进智能体评估的路线图提出了一种系统化的评分器选择方法：尽可能采用确定性评分器，必要时采用 LLM 评分器，需要额外验证时则由人工评判。Microsoft 在对 NIST/CAISI 征求意见的回应中主张，智能体系统需要开放式的行为评估和基于场景的评估；而其在 Microsoft Foundry 中评估 AI 智能体的工作则将评估视为一种分层实践，根据智能体的生命周期和能力调整各阶段评估的深度与频率。一项互补的监管框架将比例原则应用于模型评估，确保评估能够提供有意义的风险信息，同时不对提供方施加过重负担 [The Science and Practice of Proportionality in AI Risk Evaluations]。近期的实验室研究还探讨了评估结果应如何影响部署决策，将智能体的能力与预期运行环境的风险状况进行匹配。授予的自主程度、领域的敏感性，以及智能体系统能够运用的能力范围，都会影响部署决策。例如，随着智能体系统的自主性、有效性、目标复杂性和通用性提高，验证会变得更具挑战性，对人工监督的要求也会提高 [Characterizing AI Agents for Alignment and Governance]。AWS 的 Agentic AI Security Scoping Matrix 通过“渐进式自主性部署”原则将这一理念付诸实践：系统在强制性人工监督下集成，并被设计为在检测到安全事件时自动降低自身的自主级别。GovTech 的《智能体风险与能力框架》实施指南
takes a similar approach, using a detailed taxonomy of agentic capabilities – cognitive, interaction, and operational – to assess risks and scope deployment. 77
采取了类似的方法，利用详细的智能体能力分类——认知能力、交互能力和操作能力——来评估风险并界定部署范围。77
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Anthropic ’s Petri report observes that “the sheer volume and complexity of poten-tial behaviours far exceeds what researchers can manually test” once models are deployed as agents with broad tool access. Because agentic behaviours of interest, such self-pres-ervation and power-seeking, only surface across multi-turn trajectories inside realistic tool-use environments, Petri uses an auditor agent to construct and run those environ-ments. LLM judges then score transcripts across safety-relevant dimensions and surface the most concerning cases for human review. The tool was used in a joint evaluation exer-cise with OpenAI and by the UK AI Security Institute in its independent testing, an early instance of shared agentic evaluation infrastructure being adopted across developers and government evaluators. Finally, multi-agent deployments introduce a distinct category of complexity that current frameworks are only beginning to address. Similar to AWS ’s evaluations for real-world agent deployment that “measure…interagent communication patterns, coordination efficiency, and task handoff accuracy”, Perplexity ’s Response to NIST/ CAISI Request for Information 2025-0035 advocates further research into measur-ing multi-agent dynamic interaction with realistic environments rather than static test suites. Cooperative AI Foundation ’s report on Multi-Agent Risks from Advanced AI underscores a structural barrier to this work: commercial sensitivities can complicate coordination on testing interactions between proprietary agentic systems. These discussions of graduated autonomy, capability-based risk differentiation, and agent-specific evaluation demonstrate a shared recognition that validated deploy-ment for agentic systems cannot be seen as a one time approval decision; the principle requires ongoing re-assessment as the agent operates. This is especially consequential as agents move into high-stakes domains and operate at higher autonomy levels, where the interaction between operational context, capability scope, and risk tolerance demands adaptive deployment criteria throughout the system’s lifecycle.
Anthropic 的 Petri 报告指出，一旦模型被部署为能够广泛访问工具的智能体，“潜在行为的庞大数量和复杂性就远远超出了研究人员能够手动测试的范围”。由于自我保护和权力寻求等受到关注的智能体行为，只有在真实的工具使用环境中的多轮交互轨迹中才会显现，Petri 使用审计智能体来构建并运行这些环境。随后，LLM 评判器会从与安全相关的各个维度对交互记录进行评分，并筛选出最令人担忧的案例供人工审查。该工具曾用于与 OpenAI 联合开展的评估，也被英国 AI 安全研究所在独立测试中采用，这是开发者与政府评估机构共同采用共享智能体评估基础设施的早期案例。最后，多智能体部署引入了一类独特的复杂性，现有框架才刚刚开始应对这一问题。与 AWS 针对真实世界智能体部署开展的、旨在“衡量……智能体间通信模式、协调效率和任务交接准确性”的评估类似，Perplexity 在对 NIST/CAISI 信息征询 2025-0035 的回应中倡导进一步研究如何在真实环境中衡量多智能体的动态交互，而非使用静态测试套件。Cooperative AI Foundation 关于先进 AI 多智能体风险的报告强调了这项工作面临的一项结构性障碍：商业敏感性可能使针对专有智能体系统间交互测试的协调工作变得复杂。这些关于分级自主性、基于能力的风险区分和智能体专属评估的讨论体现了一种共同认识：智能体系统经验证后部署，不能被视为一次性的审批决定；这一原则要求在智能体运行过程中持续开展重新评估。随着智能体进入高风险领域并以更高的自主级别运行，这一点尤为重要，因为运行情境、能力范围与风险容忍度之间的相互作用，要求在系统整个生命周期中采用可适应变化的部署标准。
Principle 5: Adversarial Resilience原则 5：对抗韧性
what is it其含义
Agentic systems have attack surfaces beyond those present in foundation models. Agents’ multi-step planning can be hijacked through indirect prompt injection; their integration with external tools and autonomous decision-making expand both the attack surface and the scope of real-world consequences . The principle of adversarial resilience refers to the capacity of an agentic system to withstand, detect, and recover from adversarial exploitation of its capabilities. This enables developers and deployers to systematically identify vulnerabilities that emerge from the agent’s autonomy and build defences that hold under adversarial pressure. 78
智能体系统具有基础模型所不具备的额外攻击面。智能体的多步骤规划可能通过间接提示注入而遭到劫持；它们与外部工具的集成以及自主决策能力，既扩大了攻击面，也扩大了现实世界后果的影响范围。对抗韧性（adversarial resilience）原则是指智能体系统抵御、检测针对其能力的对抗性利用，并从中恢复的能力。这使开发者和部署方能够系统地识别智能体自主性所带来的漏洞，并构建在对抗压力下仍然有效的防御措施。78
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Practical guidance for implementing Adversarial Resilience
落实对抗韧性的实践指导
> Model developers: conduct model-level adversarial testing prior to release.
> 模型开发者：在发布前开展模型层面的对抗性测试。
> Agent developers: conduct agent-level red-teaming designed for agentic capabilities, including emergent behaviours, autonomous decision-making, tool use, and adversarial interactions between agents, and maintain it continuously as capabilities evolve.
> 智能体开发者：针对智能体能力开展智能体层面的红队测试，涵盖涌现行为、自主决策、工具使用以及智能体之间的对抗性交互，并随着能力的演进持续开展此类测试。
> Deployers: test the security of specific tool integrations, API configurations, and supply chain dependencies, including by simulating the compromise of tool ecosystems and communication protocols through techniques like dependency injection and external API interception.
> 部署方：测试具体工具集成、API 配置和供应链依赖项的安全性，包括通过依赖注入、外部 API 拦截等技术，模拟工具生态系统和通信协议遭到入侵的情形。
> Cloud platforms: offer testing infrastructure such as automated vulnerability scanning.
> 云平台：提供自动化漏洞扫描等测试基础设施。
> Shared responsibilities: coordinating threat intelligence across tool ecosystems and shared infrastructure to gain visibility that no individual party can achieve alone.
> 共同责任：协调工具生态系统和共享基础设施之间的威胁情报，以获得任何单一参与方都无法独自实现的可见性。
Governance context治理背景
US NIST/CAISI’s work on strengthening AI agent hijacking evaluations establishes that adversarial evaluation for agentic systems must continuously adapt to new architec-tures, as defences that are robust against previously tested attacks can remain vulnerable to novel strategies targeting new capabilities. This extends the OECD AI Principles ’foundational requirement that AI systems demonstrate robustness, defined as “the ability to withstand or overcome adverse conditions”, which, in agentic contexts, includes agents being targeted by other agents, not only by human adversaries. In 2026,
美国 NIST/CAISI 在强化 AI 智能体劫持评估方面的工作表明，针对智能体系统的对抗性评估必须持续适应新架构，因为能够有效抵御此前测试过的攻击的防御措施，仍可能无法抵御针对新能力的新型策略。这拓展了《OECD 人工智能原则》的一项基本要求，即 AI 系统应展现出稳健性，其定义为“承受或克服不利条件的能力”；在智能体情境中，这也包括智能体成为其他智能体的攻击目标，而不仅仅是人类攻击者的目标。在 2026 年，
China’s Ministry of Industry and Information Technology issued a security risk advisory on agent-specific vulnerabilities in OpenClaw, and CAICT subsequently launched a testing and verification project for red-teaming open source applications. This signals that regulatory bodies are beginning to treat adversarial resilience as a requirement for agentic systems.
中国工业和信息化部就 OpenClaw 中智能体特有的漏洞发布了安全风险提示，随后中国信息通信研究院（CAICT）启动了面向开源应用红队测试的测试验证项目。这表明，监管机构正开始将对抗韧性视为智能体系统的一项要求。
Illustrative Cases示例案例
Industry frameworks increasingly recognise that adversarial testing for agen-tic systems must go beyond conventional model-level red-teaming. The Cloud Security Alliance ’s Agentic AI Red Teaming Guide shows that the non-deterministic and autonomous nature of agentic systems creates vulnerabilities that traditional safeguards cannot address. The guide calls for proactive, agent-specific red-teaming as a continuous practice—a requirement that IBM ’s AGENTSAFE framework operationalises as “contin-uous red-team feedback loops”. Recent research highlights that traditional adversarial testing assumes that the model is interacting with a malicious human user rather than with other AI agents or adversarial environments [Multi-Agent Risks from Advanced AI]. Notably, Google DeepMind ’s work on AI Agent Traps extends the scope of threat modelling to attack classes that target human overseers by exploiting cognitive biases, includ-ing approval-fatigue and social engineering attacks targeting the human-in-the-loop. Mapping this new attack surface, the AI Agent Traps framework contends that securing agents against environmental manipulation will require “sustained collaboration between developers, security researchers, and policymakers”. As one example of 79
行业框架日益认识到，针对智能体系统的对抗性测试必须超越传统的模型层面红队测试。云安全联盟的《智能体 AI 红队测试指南》指出，智能体系统的非确定性和自主性会产生传统防护措施无法应对的漏洞。该指南呼吁将主动开展的、针对智能体的红队测试作为一项持续性实践；IBM 的 AGENTSAFE 框架则将这一要求落实为“持续的红队反馈循环”。近期研究强调，传统对抗性测试假定模型正在与恶意人类用户交互，而非与其他 AI 智能体或对抗性环境交互 [Multi-Agent Risks from Advanced AI]。值得注意的是，Google DeepMind 关于 AI Agent Traps 的工作将威胁建模的范围扩展至利用认知偏差来攻击人类监督者的攻击类别，包括审批疲劳攻击，以及针对人在回路中的人类参与者的社会工程攻击。AI Agent Traps 框架在梳理这一新攻击面时提出，要保护智能体免受环境操纵，将需要“开发者、安全研究人员和政策制定者之间的持续合作”。作为其中一个例子，79

existing implementation, Tencent Zhuque Lab ’s open source AI-Infra-Guard platform provides a multi-agent red-teaming framework that continuously scans for secu-rity vulnerabilities across infrastructure and agent connections—enabling automated security testing at scale. In addition to emerging agent-specific red-teaming practices, the tool ecosystems and communication protocols that enable agentic capabilities simultaneously create novel supply chain attack surfaces. Tencent’s analysis of Model Context Protocol (MCP) and Agent2Agent Protocol (A2A) security identifies several threat vectors at this layer: backdoors embedded in MCP servers, prompt injection and jailbreak attacks, and API key theft through compromised or impersonated servers. The Cloud Security Alliance
在现有实现中，腾讯朱雀实验室的开源 AI-Infra-Guard 平台提供了一个多智能体红队测试框架，可持续扫描基础设施和智能体连接中的安全漏洞，从而实现大规模自动化安全测试。除了新兴的智能体专用红队测试实践之外，支持智能体能力的工具生态系统和通信协议也同时形成了新的供应链攻击面。腾讯对模型上下文协议（MCP）和 Agent2Agent Protocol（A2A）安全性的分析识别出该层面的多种威胁途径：嵌入 MCP 服务器的后门、提示注入和越狱攻击，以及通过遭入侵或被冒充的服务器窃取 API 密钥。云安全联盟
provides corresponding red-teaming methodology, featuring simulations of malicious code insertion during the agent’s development and of attacks on external services and APIs that the agent depends on. Adversarial resilience for agentic systems is emerging as a governance prior-ity among practitioners who are recognising that agents’ autonomous capabilities and multi-agent coordination present new vulnerabilities. Technical practices, from threat taxonomies and testing frameworks to large-scale empirical validation and open source tooling, all serve to operationalise adversarial resilience as a continuous property of agentic systems in deployment.
提供了相应的红队测试方法，包括模拟在智能体开发过程中植入恶意代码，以及模拟针对智能体所依赖的外部服务和 API 的攻击。随着从业者认识到智能体的自主能力和多智能体协调会带来新的脆弱性，智能体系统的对抗韧性正成为治理的优先事项。从威胁分类体系和测试框架，到大规模实证验证和开源工具，各类技术实践都旨在将对抗韧性落实为已部署智能体系统的一项持续性属性。
Principle 6: Multi-Agent Stability原则 6：多智能体稳定性
what is it其含义
In multi-agent systems, failures can emerge from agent interactions themselves – through coordination failures, feedback loops, and cascades propagating across agent populations – rather than from any individual agent or external compromise.
在多智能体系统中，故障可能源于智能体之间的交互本身——通过协调失效、反馈循环以及在智能体群体中传播的级联效应产生——而非源于任何单个智能体或外部入侵。
Multi-agent stability refers to an agentic system’s resilience against these interac-tion-level failure modes, which single-agent risk mitigations cannot fully capture.
多智能体稳定性是指智能体系统抵御这些交互层面故障模式的韧性，而单智能体风险缓解措施无法全面涵盖这些故障模式。
Practical guidance for implementing Multi-Agent Stability
实施多智能体稳定性的实践指南
Agent developers: set protocols and rules for how agents delegate and share context, and threat-model how risks propagate through the system, including error transmission, accumulation of errors over time, correlation across shared supply chain components, and feedback loops.
智能体开发者：制定有关智能体如何委派任务和共享上下文的协议与规则，并对风险如何在系统中传播进行威胁建模，包括错误传播、错误随时间累积、共享供应链组件带来的相关性以及反馈循环。
Deployers: configure multi-agent systems within the constraints that developers set, and test for behaviours that emerge from agents interacting, even if agents have been individually evaluated as safe for deployment.
部署方：在开发者设定的约束范围内配置多智能体系统，并测试智能体交互所涌现的行为，即使这些智能体已经过单独评估并被认定可安全部署。
Shared responsibilities: designing system-wide safety controls calibrated to compounding and correlated failure modes; testing across deployments that share models, tools, or infrastructure. 80
共同责任：针对累积性和相关性故障模式设计相应的系统级安全控制措施；对共享模型、工具或基础设施的不同部署开展跨部署测试。80
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Governance context治理背景
Multi-agent stability is among the most recently developed principles in agentic AI governance. Standards bodies and cybersecurity authorities across major jurisdictions are converging on agent interactions as a core risk surface. The US NIST/CAIS I’s Request for Information on AI Agent Security treats multi-agent threats as a distinct risk domain, an approach that will continue in the planned NIST Interagency Reports under the Control Overlay for Securing AI Systems (COSAiS). The guidance on Careful Adoption of Agentic AI Services , co-authored by several cybersecurity agencies, empha-sises emergent behaviours and cascading failures in multi-agent environments as a key challenge in wider adoption of agentic systems. Singapore’s IMDA Model AI Gov-ernance Framework for Agentic AI similarly identifies this risk surface, noting that multi-agent coordination can bring about unpredictable outcomes and cascading systemic effects. China’s TC260 Research Report on Agent Safety Standardisation specifies a corre-sponding threat category for multi-agent systems that includes cascading hallucination propagation, conflict deadlock, and resource overload. Across these frameworks and tech-nical standardisation efforts, multi-agent stability is consolidating as a distinct governance principle.
多智能体稳定性是智能体 AI 治理中最新形成的原则之一。主要司法管辖区的标准机构和网络安全主管部门正逐渐形成共识，将智能体交互视为核心风险面。美国 NIST/CAIS I 的《AI 智能体安全信息征询》将多智能体威胁视为一个独立的风险领域，这一思路将在《保护 AI 系统的控制覆盖层（COSAiS）》框架下计划编制的 NIST 跨机构报告中延续。由多家网络安全机构共同编写的《审慎采用智能体 AI 服务》指南强调，多智能体环境中的涌现行为和级联故障是更广泛采用智能体系统所面临的一项关键挑战。新加坡 IMDA 的《智能体 AI 模型治理框架》同样识别了这一风险面，指出多智能体协调可能带来不可预测的结果和级联系统性影响。中国 TC260 的《智能体安全标准化研究报告》为多智能体系统规定了相应的威胁类别，包括幻觉的级联传播、冲突死锁和资源过载。在这些框架和技术标准化工作中，多智能体稳定性正逐渐确立为一项独立的治理原则。
Illustrative cases示例案例
Practices for ensuring the environmental stability of multi-agent systems are emerging across the research and standards communities. The OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Appli-cations 2026 addresses agentic cascading failures: failures that occur because a single fault propagates across agents and compounds into system-wide harm. Recom-mended mitigations include, among others, predefined blast-radius caps in testing and deployment, as well as validating every sensitive tool invocation against a poli-cy-as-code rule, to prevent compromised or drifting agents from triggering chain reactions across connected agentic systems. The Cooperative AI Foundation ’s Multi-Agent Risks from Advanced AI report identifies miscoordination, conflict, and collusion as three fail-ure modes and emphasises the need for secure interaction protocols and methods for detecting correlated and compounding failures. Similar mitigations are proposed in recent research that argues that individually safe agents can collectively form unsafe systems, through emergent dynamics and collective-action failures, even without adversarial compromise [Open Challenges in Multi-Agent Security]. OpenAI ’s response to the US NIST/CAISI consultation points at the distinct governance questions raised by multi-step trajectories and multi-agent interactions, arguing that incidents might result “from the specific order and combination of tool calls, rather than a singular failure in authentication or a compromised endpoint.” Industry research is mapping how risks aggregate and propagate across agent populations over time. Google DeepMind ’s AI Agent Traps framework characterises a class of systemic traps that include congestion dynamics that overwhelm shared resources, interdependence cascades that “amplify the initial move in a rapid, self-reinforc-ing spiral”, and synchronised behaviour of learning agents without explicit communication. 81
研究界和标准界正逐步形成确保多智能体系统环境稳定性的实践。《OWASP 智能体应用十大风险 2026》讨论了智能体级联故障：单个故障在智能体之间传播，并不断叠加，最终造成系统范围损害的故障。建议的缓解措施包括在测试和部署中预先设定影响范围上限，以及依据策略即代码（policy-as-code）规则验证每一次敏感工具调用，以防止遭到入侵或发生行为漂移的智能体在相互连接的智能体系统中触发连锁反应。Cooperative AI Foundation 的《先进 AI 的多智能体风险》报告将协调失当、冲突和合谋列为三种故障模式，并强调需要安全的交互协议，以及检测相关性和累积性故障的方法。近期研究也提出了类似的缓解措施，并指出，即使没有遭受对抗性入侵，单独来看安全的智能体也可能通过涌现动态和集体行动失效，共同构成不安全的系统 [Open Challenges in Multi-Agent Security]。OpenAI 对美国 NIST/CAISI 征询的回应指出，多步骤轨迹和多智能体交互引出了独特的治理问题，并认为事件可能源于“工具调用的特定顺序和组合，而非单一的身份验证失效或某个遭到入侵的端点”。业界研究正在梳理风险如何随时间在智能体群体中聚集和传播。Google DeepMind 的 AI Agent Traps 框架刻画了一类系统性陷阱，包括使共享资源不堪重负的拥塞动态、“以快速、自我强化的螺旋方式放大初始行动”的相互依赖级联效应，以及学习型智能体在没有显式通信的情况下出现的同步行为。81
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Microsoft ’s recent testing of multi-agent interactions at scale emphasises the need for information flow controls beyond securing individual agents, and identifies risks that arise only at the network level: from propagation of agent worms and amplification of false information to compromise of verification mechanisms and invisible attacks pass-ing through chains of unaware agents. Anthropic complements this with a discussion of trust escalation across agent boundaries and inter-agent persuasion risks, while its Managed Agents framework operationalises corresponding mitigations through bounded delegation depth, hard limits on a number of concurrent agents in a session, and context isolation between sub-agents. Finally, Tencent Cloud ’s Cube Sandbox extends this to the infrastructure layer, containing failures in two complementary ways: there is net-work-level isolation that prevents cross-agent contamination through external services; and each agent runs on its own operating system kernel, so that a failure in one sand-box cannot cascade to others that share the host. Across these approaches, multi-agent stability is consolidating as a distinct governance concern that requires methods and tools designed for the ecosystem level. Detect-ing and mitigating compounding failures across multi-agent deployments will require system-level interventions that no single party can implement alone [Scaling AI Safety Research for a Multi-Agent World], but which determine whether such systems remain governable at scale. 82
Microsoft 近期开展的大规模多智能体交互测试强调，除了保障单个智能体的安全之外，还需要实施信息流控制，并指出了仅在网络层面才会出现的风险：从智能体蠕虫的传播和虚假信息的放大，到验证机制遭到破坏，以及隐蔽攻击经由一连串毫不知情的智能体传递。Anthropic 对跨智能体边界的信任升级和智能体间的说服风险进行了讨论，作为补充；其 Managed Agents 框架则通过限定委派深度、对单个会话中的并发智能体数量设定硬性上限，以及隔离子智能体之间的上下文，将相应的缓解措施落实到实际运行中。最后，Tencent Cloud 的 Cube Sandbox 将这一思路扩展到基础设施层，通过两种互补的方式遏制故障：一是实施网络层面的隔离，防止通过外部服务发生跨智能体污染；二是让每个智能体在各自独立的操作系统内核上运行，从而使一个沙箱中的故障不会级联传播至共享同一宿主机的其他沙箱。在这些方法中，多智能体稳定性正逐渐成为一项独立的治理议题，需要专为生态系统层面设计的方法和工具。检测并缓解多智能体部署中不断叠加的故障，需要任何单一参与方都无法独自实施的系统级干预措施 [Scaling AI Safety Research for a Multi-Agent World]，而这些措施决定了此类系统在大规模运行时能否保持可治理性。82
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
3. Operation and Monitoring3. 运行与监测
Operation and monitoring is the lifecycle stage at which an agentic system per-forms tasks in its live environment and its behaviour is observed, assessed, and corrected. In alignment with ISO/IEC 42001:2023 and ISO/IEC 23894:2023, post-de-ployment risk management also covers ongoing repairs, updates, and support. The EU General-Purpose AI Code of Practice Commitment 3 mandates providers of models with systemic risk to continuously assess and mitigate risks after market placement, codifying post-deployment governance as a lifecycle obligation. In the context of high-risk AI systems, the EU AI Act Article 72 treats post-deployment monitoring as a prerequisite for compliance. For agentic systems, this stage presents distinct challenges: agents operate in environments that change unpredictably between and during sessions, take actions with real-world consequences, and require mechanisms for effective remedy when autonomous actions produce unintended outcomes.
运行与监测是生命周期中的一个阶段，在此阶段，智能体系统在实际运行环境中执行任务，其行为受到观察、评估和纠正。根据 ISO/IEC 42001:2023 和 ISO/IEC 23894:2023，部署后风险管理还涵盖持续的修复、更新和支持。欧盟《通用人工智能实践守则》承诺 3 要求具有系统性风险的模型的提供者在模型投放市场后持续评估并缓解风险，将部署后治理明确规定为一项生命周期义务。对于高风险 AI 系统，欧盟《人工智能法》第 72 条将部署后监测视为合规的先决条件。对于智能体系统，这一阶段面临独特的挑战：智能体运行环境在会话之间及会话期间会发生不可预测的变化，智能体采取的行动会对现实世界产生影响，并且当自主行动造成非预期结果时，需要有能够提供有效补救的机制。
Principle 7: Runtime Assurance原则 7：运行时保障
what is it其含义
As AI agents operate over extended periods, retaining information across sessions and integrating external inputs, the agent in operation may no longer behave as it did when it was approved for deployment. Runtime assurance is the principle that an agent’s accumulated state and actions continue to satisfy the properties that were validated, even as the agent’s inputs and operational environment continuously change. This enables developers and deployers to distinguish between an agent that is the same as when it was validated, and one that has drifted from its original state, and to enforce corrective action before the drift translates into harmful consequences.
随着 AI 智能体长时间运行、跨会话保留信息并整合外部输入，运行中的智能体的行为可能不再与其获准部署时相同。运行时保障（Runtime Assurance）是指，即使智能体的输入和运行环境不断变化，其累积状态和行动仍应持续满足已经过验证的属性。这使开发者和部署者能够区分仍与验证时保持一致的智能体和已偏离其原始状态的智能体，并在这种漂移转化为有害后果之前实施纠正措施。
Practical guidance for implementing Runtime Assurance
实施运行时保障的实践指南
Agent developers: build runtime protection against skill poisoning and manipulation, and against RAG poisoning; build memory checkpoints that enable rollback; and validate new information before it is written to long-term memory.
智能体开发者：构建运行时防护机制，抵御技能投毒与操纵，以及 RAG 投毒；建立支持回滚的记忆检查点；并在将新信息写入长期记忆之前对其进行验证。
Deployers: configure session isolation, set intervention thresholds for their operational context, and actively maintain policies governing memory retention, access, and rollback.
部署者：配置会话隔离，根据自身运行情境设定干预阈值，并积极维护关于记忆保留、访问和回滚的策略。
Cloud platforms: provide isolated runtime that contains each session’s state, memory, and resources and clears them on termination.
云平台：提供隔离的运行环境，将每个会话的状态、记忆和资源限制在其中，并在会话终止时予以清除。
Shared responsibilities: continuous monitoring and drift detection must run independently of the agent rather than rely on its own reporting; this may be built by the developer, provided by the platform, or operated independently. 83
共同责任：持续监测和漂移检测必须独立于智能体运行，而不能依赖智能体自身的报告；这些机制可以由开发者构建、由平台提供，或独立运行。83
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Governance context治理背景
Singapore’s IMDA Model AI Governance Framework for Agentic AI offers the most direct articulation of the runtime assurance principle, mandat-ing post-deployment assurance that an agentic system “works as expected and is not affected by model drift or other changes in the environment”. The EU AI Act Article 15 addresses a related concern for systems “that continue to learn after being put into ser-vice”, requiring measures to eliminate or reduce the risk of outputs influencing future operations through feedback loops. For agentic systems, such a regulatory stance encompasses corruption to the agent’s accumulated state and memory. For example, Chi-na’s TC260 is developing a Basic Specification for Agent Safety , which seeks to establish a security framework across an agent’s perception, planning, memory, and action layers— extending assurance requirements to an agent’s accumulated memory and state, not only its inputs and outputs.
新加坡资讯通信媒体发展局（IMDA）的《智能体人工智能治理示范框架》对运行时保障原则作出了最直接的阐述，要求在部署后确保智能体系统“按预期运行，且不受模型漂移或其他环境变化的影响”。欧盟《人工智能法》第 15 条针对“投入使用后继续学习”的系统提出了相关要求，规定必须采取措施，消除或降低输出通过反馈回路影响未来运行的风险。对于智能体系统，这种监管立场涵盖了智能体累积状态和记忆遭到破坏的情形。例如，中国的 TC260 正在制定《智能体安全基本规范》，旨在为智能体的感知、规划、记忆和行动各层建立安全框架，将保障要求扩展至智能体累积的记忆和状态，而不仅限于其输入和输出。
Illustrative Cases示例案例
A range of publicly available industry approaches to maintaining runtime assurance focus on continuous monitoring of agent conduct during operation.
一系列公开可获取的、用于维持运行时保障（runtime assurance）的行业方法，侧重于在智能体运行期间持续监测其行为。
IBM ’s AGENTSAFE framework advocates embedding drift detection and seman-tic telemetry into deployed agents by introducing auxiliary agents that operate in parallel with the primary agent. These auxiliary agents provide continuous oversight so that humans needn’t rely on the primary agent’s self-reporting. ByteD-ance ’s AGENTARMOR framework treats an agent’s runtime execution trace as a program that can be formally analysed for security. It does this by abstracting all agent actions into a structured graph representation. Cloud providers are converging on similar approaches to continuous operational over-sight. Volcengine , for example, uses a dedicated Agent Security Management Platform to monitor agent inputs and outputs in real time – a capability that becomes critical when agents execute proactively rather than waiting for human prompts. Z.AI ’s AutoGLM exe-cutes agent operations in cloud environments so that every action is replayable and subject to intervention in real time. Partnership on AI ’s Prioritizing Real-Time Failure Detection in AI Agents argues that failure detection should operate as layered controls at differ-ent stages of agent execution – before actions are taken, during execution, and across multi-step sequences where failures compound. Another crucial dimension of runtime assurance is that agents’ internal memory, con-text, and accumulated information must be protected. Recent work from Shanghai AI Lab demonstrates that in agents that autonomously improve through environ-mental interaction, memory-driven degradation in safety alignment can be triggered by specific incidents in the agent’s experience. Microsoft ’s research addresses the archi-tectural level, requiring “multiple controls around how memory is accessed and written to” and “trust boundaries between types and scopes of memory used” for all agentic systems. 84
IBM 的 AGENTSAFE 框架主张引入与主智能体并行运行的辅助智能体，将漂移检测和语义遥测嵌入已部署的智能体。这些辅助智能体提供持续监督，使人类无需依赖主智能体的自我报告。字节跳动的 AGENTARMOR 框架将智能体的运行时执行轨迹视为一个程序，可对其安全性进行形式化分析。该框架通过将智能体的所有行动抽象为结构化图表示来实现这一点。云服务提供商正趋于采用类似的方法，对运行过程进行持续监督。例如，火山引擎使用专门的智能体安全管理平台，实时监测智能体的输入和输出；当智能体主动执行任务而非等待人类提示时，这一能力就变得至关重要。Z.AI 的 AutoGLM 在云环境中执行智能体操作，使每个行动都可回放，并可接受实时干预。Partnership on AI 的《优先考虑 AI 智能体的实时故障检测》提出，应在智能体执行的不同阶段，将故障检测作为分层控制措施实施，包括采取行动之前、执行过程中，以及故障会累积的多步骤序列中。运行时保障的另一个关键维度是，必须保护智能体的内部记忆、上下文和累积信息。上海人工智能实验室的近期研究表明，对于通过与环境交互而自主改进的智能体，其经历中的特定事件可能触发由记忆驱动的安全对齐退化。Microsoft 的研究着眼于架构层面，要求所有智能体系统都具备“围绕记忆访问和写入方式的多重控制措施”，并“在所使用的不同类型和作用范围的记忆之间设置信任边界”。84
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
An example of this being implemented is Tencent Cloud ’s Agent Runtime , which isolates individual sessions through dedicated virtual machines, with full termination and memo-ry-clearing at the end of sessions. By synthesising these developer-led strategies, a holistic model of implementing runtime assurance emerges: independent continuous monitoring, memory hardening, state drift detection, and session isolation at the infrastructure level. These practices suggest that runtime assurance is crystallising as an overarching engineering principle for agen-tic systems, complementing pre-deployment evaluation and adversarial resilience.
腾讯云的 Agent Runtime 是落实这一要求的一个实例：它通过专用虚拟机隔离各个会话，并在会话结束时彻底终止会话、清空内存。综合这些由开发者主导的策略，可以形成一个实施运行时保障的整体模型：独立的持续监测、记忆加固、状态漂移检测，以及基础设施层面的会话隔离。这些实践表明，运行时保障正逐步确立为智能体系统的一项总体工程原则，与部署前评估和对抗韧性形成互补。
Principle 8: Interruptibility原则 8：可中断性
what is it其含义
As AI agents execute multi-step plans with real-world consequences, the ability to intervene becomes both more critical and more difficult: actions may be irreversible, errors compound across steps, and a sufficiently capable agent may resist or circum-vent shutdown to preserve its goals. Interruptibility means that human operators can safely pause, redirect, stop, and reverse an AI agent’s actions at any point, and that the agent cannot tamper with these mechanisms . This property is crucial for limiting unin-tended or harmful actions.
随着 AI 智能体执行会产生现实后果的多步骤计划，实施干预的能力变得更加关键，也更加难以实现：行动可能不可逆，错误会在各步骤间累积，而能力足够强的智能体可能为了维持自身目标而抵制或规避关闭。可中断性意味着，人类操作人员能够在任何时点安全地暂停、重定向、停止和撤销 AI 智能体的行动，并且智能体无法篡改这些机制。这一属性对于限制非预期或有害行动至关重要。
Practical guidance for implementing Interruptibility
实施可中断性的实践指南
Agent developers: build shutdown and override mechanisms that operate outside the agent’s reasoning loop as system-level privileges; provide means to redirect an agent mid-execution without terminating the whole workflow and to graduate intervention from throttling and pausing through isolation to full termination.
智能体开发者：构建以系统级权限运行、独立于智能体推理循环之外的关闭和接管机制；提供在执行过程中重定向智能体而无需终止整个工作流的手段，并支持从限流和暂停，到隔离，再到完全终止的逐级干预。
Deployers: determine intervention thresholds for their operational context and risk tolerance, proportionate to the severity of the detected anomaly.
部署方：根据自身运行场景和风险容忍度确定干预阈值，使干预程度与所检测异常的严重程度相称。
Shared responsibilities: whoever controls the execution environment is responsible for intervening to revert the agent’s effects on the environment when necessary (and not only halting its execution). This may be the deployer, the platform provider, or both.
共同责任：控制执行环境的一方有责任在必要时实施干预，撤销智能体对环境造成的影响，而不仅仅是停止其执行。这一方可能是部署方、平台提供商，也可能是双方。
Governance context治理背景
Global frameworks agree that effective human oversight is necessary for high-risk and autonomous systems. This is codified in the EU AI Act ’s Article 14, which mandates that high-risk AI systems be designed to allow natural persons to oversee their functioning. The regulation explicitly requires that human operators must have the ability to “inter-vene in the operation... or interrupt the system through a ‘stop’ button” and, crucially, to “decide not to use the high-risk AI system or otherwise disregard, override or reverse the output”. This regulatory stance mirrors China’s TC260 AI Safety and Governance Frame-work 2.0 , which mandates the need for “circuit breakers” and “one-click control” for highly autonomous operations. Singapore IMDA ’s case study applying its Model AI Governance Framework for Agentic AI to OpenClaw explicitly recommends that critical safeguards, 85
全球各类框架一致认为，高风险系统和自主系统必须接受有效的人类监督。欧盟《人工智能法案》第 14 条将这一要求纳入法律，规定高风险 AI 系统的设计必须允许自然人监督其运行。该法规明确要求，人类操作人员必须能够“干预其运行……或通过‘停止’按钮中断系统”，而且至关重要的是，能够“决定不使用高风险 AI 系统，或以其他方式忽略、推翻或撤销其输出”。这一监管立场与中国 TC260《人工智能安全治理框架》2.0 相呼应，后者明确要求为高度自主的操作设置“熔断机制”和“一键管控”功能。新加坡 IMDA 将其《智能体人工智能模型治理框架》应用于 OpenClaw 的案例研究明确建议，关键防护措施，85
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
including kill switches, must run on a control plane separate from the agent that the agent cannot tamper with, for example in the infrastructure or orchestration layer. Together, these frameworks establish a baseline: human control must be persistent, capa-ble of overriding algorithmic decisions, architecturally independent of the agent it governs, and commensurate with the system’s level of autonomy and risk.
包括紧急停止开关，必须运行在独立于智能体且智能体无法篡改的控制平面上，例如基础设施层或编排层。这些框架共同确立了一项基准：人类控制必须持续存在，能够推翻算法决策，在架构上独立于其所管控的智能体，并与系统的自主程度和风险水平相匹配。
Illustrative Cases示例案例
Beyond high-level regulation, developer protocols have converged on requiring non-tamperability, as leading labs explicitly prohibit agents from interfering with their own shutdown mechanisms. Huawei ’s HarmonyOS agent security policy treats this as a rigid operating system constraint, forbidding agents from inducing system malfunctions –such as disabling “back” keys or process termination functions – that would trap a user within the agentic environment. This aligns with OpenAI ’s Practices for Governing Agentic AI Systems , which asserts that agents must be architecturally incapable of halting or tampering with a user’s attempt to shut them down. In both documents, interruptibility is created through hard-coded system privileges that reside outside the agent’s reasoning loop. While the fundamental requirement for control is universal, the technical imple-mentation of interruptibility is evolving from simple termination to sophisticated, graduated containment strategies. IBM ’s AGENTSAFE framework advances the field by introducing a “containment ladder”, which rejects the simple “allow vs. kill” dichot-omy in favour of proportional responses. Depending on the severity of the anomaly, this framework recommends graduating from rate-limiting tool calls and isolating sensitive resources to, in critical cases, activating a non-recoverable kill switch. Partnership on AI ’s Prioritizing Real-Time Failure Detection in AI Agents offers a complementary framing, distinguishing between three response types – stop, escalate to a human, or retry with a revised plan – applied at different stages of the agent’s execution, from pre-ac-tion validation to intervention between steps. Similarly, Anthropic emphasises a dynamic control model in Claude Code, where humans can not only stop the agent but “redirect its approach” in real time. This enables humans to maintain oversight without necessarily aborting the entire workflow. Finally, the scope of interruptibility is expanding to include retroactive remediation and state recovery. The Shanghai AI Laboratory and Concordia AI ’s Frontier AI Risk Management Framework 1.5 argues that the ability to stop an agent is insufficient with-out mechanisms to reverse its impact. The framework advocates for mandatory “undo” and immediate rollback mechanisms that can be initiated upon detecting anoma-lous behaviour or coordination failures. Similarly, Google Cloud ’s review of lessons learned from existing agentic applications calls for “agent undo stacks” – bundling agent actions into chunks that can be reversed as a single unit. Taken together, these approaches emphasise interruptibility as a holistic system of non-tamperable oversight, propor-tional intervention, and reversible action. 86
除高层监管要求外，开发者规范也已趋于一致，要求控制机制不可被篡改，因为领先实验室明确禁止智能体干扰自身的关闭机制。华为的 HarmonyOS 智能体安全策略将此视为一项严格的操作系统约束，禁止智能体引发系统功能异常，例如禁用“返回”键或进程终止功能，从而将用户困在智能体环境中。这与 OpenAI 的《智能体人工智能系统治理实践》一致，该文件主张，必须从架构上确保智能体无法阻止或干扰用户关闭它们的尝试。在这两份文件中，可中断性均通过硬编码的系统权限来实现，这些权限位于智能体的推理循环之外。尽管控制的基本要求具有普遍性，但可中断性的技术实现正从简单的终止操作发展为复杂的分级遏制策略。IBM 的 AGENTSAFE 框架通过引入“遏制阶梯”推动了这一领域的发展，摒弃简单的“允许与终止”二分法，转而采用与情况严重程度相称的响应。该框架建议根据异常的严重程度逐级采取措施，从限制工具调用频率和隔离敏感资源，到在危急情况下启用不可恢复的紧急停止开关。Partnership on AI 的《优先考虑人工智能智能体的实时故障检测》提供了一种互补的分析视角，区分了三种响应类型——停止、升级交由人工处理，或修改计划后重试——并将其应用于智能体执行过程的不同阶段，从行动前验证到步骤之间的干预。同样，Anthropic 在 Claude Code 中强调一种动态控制模式，人类不仅可以停止智能体，还可以实时“调整其处理方式”。这使人类能够持续进行监督，而不必终止整个工作流程。最后，可中断性的范围正在扩展，涵盖事后补救和状态恢复。上海人工智能实验室与 Concordia AI 的《前沿人工智能风险管理框架》1.5 认为，如果没有逆转智能体所造成影响的机制，仅具备停止智能体的能力是不够的。该框架倡导强制设置“撤销”和即时回滚机制，以便在检测到异常行为或协调失效时启动。同样，Google Cloud 对现有智能体应用经验教训的回顾呼吁采用“智能体撤销栈”，即将智能体的行动打包成可作为一个整体撤销的操作块。综合来看，这些方法强调，可中断性是一个由不可篡改的监督、适度干预和可逆行动构成的整体系统。86
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Principle 9: Legibility原则 9：可理解性
what is it其含义
Compared to conventional AI systems, AI agents’ decisions are more complex and have wider real-world consequences, making them less understandable and transparent to humans. Legibility refers to the capacity to represent an agent’s decision-mak-ing process, including its planning logic, tool selections, and intermediate reasoning steps, in terms accessible to deployers, operators, users, and affected exter-nal parties. Where auditability asks whether an agent’s actions can be reconstructed, the principle of legibility ensures they can be understood. This establishes the foundation for post-incident forensics, accountability, and, ultimately, societal trust in agentic systems.
与传统人工智能系统相比，人工智能智能体的决策更为复杂，对现实世界的影响也更为广泛，因此对人类而言更难理解，透明度也更低。可理解性（Legibility）是指以部署方、操作人员、用户及受影响的外部各方能够理解的方式，呈现智能体决策过程的能力，包括其规划逻辑、工具选择和中间推理步骤。可审计性关注能否重建智能体的行动，而可理解性原则则确保这些行动能够被理解。这为事件发生后的取证、问责，以及最终建立社会对智能体系统的信任奠定了基础。
Practical guidance for implementing Legibility
落实可理解性的实践指南
Agent developers: build explanation interfaces that make the agent’s planning logic, tool selections, and intermediate reasoning steps comprehensible to reviewers (rather than simply listing their actions in opaque logs), and combine plan summaries, tool traces, and compliance indicators into a coherent account of each stage of execution.
智能体开发者：构建解释界面，使审查人员能够理解智能体的规划逻辑、工具选择和中间推理步骤（而非仅在晦涩的日志中罗列其行动），并将计划摘要、工具调用轨迹和合规指标整合为对各执行阶段的连贯说明。
Deployers: configure which consequential decisions are actively flagged to users as they occur, and at what threshold of consequence.
部署方：配置哪些具有重要影响的决策应在发生时主动向用户提示，以及触发提示的影响程度阈值。
Shared responsibilities: where tasks are distributed between different agents, there should be cross-industry conventions for how agents interact with, and explain their actions to, other agents, the user, the hosting cloud, and other relevant actors.
共同责任：当任务分配给不同智能体时，应制定跨行业惯例，规范智能体如何与其他智能体、用户、托管云平台及其他相关参与方交互，并向其解释自身行动。
Governance context治理背景
Legibility is among the most well-established requirements in frontier AI governance. In the EU AI Act , aside from Article 13’s general mandate for transparency and interpret-ability, Article 86 grants affected external parties the right to meaningful explanations of “the main elements of the decision taken” by AI systems. US NIST AI RMF 1.0 draws a distinction between explainability (“a representation of the mechanisms underlying AI systems’ operation”) and interpretability (“the meaning of AI systems’ output in the context of their designed functional purposes”), noting that jointly, these define the perceived trustworthiness of autonomous systems. China’s TC260 AI Safety and Governance Framework 2.0 follows a similar logic, mandating constant improve-ment in AI systems’ explainability and predictability, including their “internal structure, reasoning logic, technical interfaces, and output results of AI systems”. For agentic sys-tems, satisfying these requirements means explaining not only agents’ outputs, planning, and tool selection, but also real-world outcomes produced by autonomous actions.
可理解性（legibility）是前沿 AI 治理中最为确立的要求之一。在《欧盟 AI 法案》中，除第 13 条对透明度和可解释性的一般要求外，第 86 条还赋予受影响的外部相关方获得有意义解释的权利，解释内容涉及 AI 系统“所作决策的主要要素”。美国 NIST AI RMF 1.0 区分了可解释性（explainability，“对 AI 系统运行底层机制的表述”）与可诠释性（interpretability，“AI 系统的输出在其设计功能目的背景下的含义”），并指出，两者共同界定了自主系统在人们感知中的可信程度。中国 TC260《人工智能安全治理框架》2.0 遵循类似逻辑，要求持续提升 AI 系统的可解释性和可预测性，涵盖“AI 系统的内部结构、推理逻辑、技术接口和输出结果”。对于智能体系统，满足这些要求意味着不仅要解释智能体的输出、规划和工具选择，还要解释自主行动在现实世界中产生的结果。

In recent research, there is a growing consensus that conventional explainability and interpretability methods are limited when applied to the agent lifecycle, from goal for-mation through environmental interaction to outcome evaluation [Interpreting Agentic 87
近期研究逐渐形成共识：将传统的可解释性与可诠释性方法应用于智能体从目标形成、环境交互到结果评估的整个生命周期时，这些方法存在局限 [Interpreting Agentic 87

Systems: Beyond Model Explanations to System-Level Accountability]. Empiri-cal work from the Vector Institute highlights an additional need for process-time and outcome-time explanations to support multi-step decision-making in an agentic set-ting [From Features to Actions: Explainability in Traditional and Agentic AI Systems].
Systems: Beyond Model Explanations to System-Level Accountability]。Vector Institute 的实证研究强调，还需要在过程进行时和结果产生时提供解释，以支持智能体场景中的多步决策 [From Features to Actions: Explainability in Traditional and Agentic AI Systems]。
Anthropic ’s research on chain-of-thought faithfulness suggests that reasoning traces are not always faithful to the model’s underlying reasoning, a finding reinforced by
Anthropic 关于思维链忠实性的研究表明，推理轨迹并不总能忠实反映模型的底层推理过程，这一发现也得到了以下研究的支持：
Google DeepMind ’s work on measuring chain-of-thought legibility and coverage. Ali-baba ’s Artificial Intelligence Governance Research Center white paper argues that agents should be integrated with visualisations and interaction interfaces that make the rationale behind each decision accessible to users and regulators. Anthrop-ic ’s Trustworthy agents framework further highlights that legibility requires coverage across the full agent stack, from model through harness, tools, and execution environment, not only the reasoning layer. To standardise what a sufficient explanation should contain, one proposed solution is the Minimal Explanation Packet. This bundles plan summaries with tool input-output traces and policy compliance indicators, and is designed to make an agent’s reasoning process legible across each stage of its execution rather than only at the point of final output. Several engineering efforts are converging on building the observability infrastructure that is needed to make agentic actions continuously legible. OpenTelemetry ’s work on Evolving Standards and Best Practices in AI agent observability builds upon Goog-le ’s Agents white paper and defines cross-industry semantic conventions for agent actions that are framework- and vendor-neutral. The conventions are designed so that telem-etry can support both operational monitoring and reconstructing an agent’s decision sequence in a way that is legible for human oversight. Shanghai AI Lab ’s AgentDoG: A Diagnostic Guardrail Framework proposes an explainable AI module for trac-ing agentic actions to specific planning steps, tool selections, or context misinterpretations. The principle of legibility also underlies agentic security commitments issued by China’s AI Industry Development Alliance , in which a coalition of major developers commits not only to presenting users with the full execution chain, but to ensuring that critical operations are actively flagged to users, “guaranteeing that application behaviour is perceptible”. This commitment highlights that execution transparency alone is insufficient for sys-tems where users cannot reasonably monitor every autonomous step. The commitment is to actively surface consequential decisions as they are being made rather than simply making them available for later review.
Google DeepMind 关于衡量思维链可理解性和覆盖度的研究。阿里巴巴人工智能治理研究中心的白皮书主张，应为智能体集成可视化功能和交互界面，使用户与监管机构能够了解每项决策背后的依据。Anthropic 的可信智能体框架进一步强调，可理解性要求覆盖智能体的完整技术栈，从模型到运行框架、工具和执行环境，而不仅限于推理层。为规范充分的解释应包含哪些内容，一项提出的解决方案是最小解释包（Minimal Explanation Packet）。它将计划摘要、工具输入输出轨迹和政策合规指标打包在一起，旨在使智能体推理过程在执行的每个阶段都可被理解，而非仅在最终输出时才可被理解。多项工程工作正趋向于构建可观测性基础设施，以使智能体行动能够持续被理解。OpenTelemetry 关于 AI 智能体可观测性标准演进与最佳实践的工作，以 Google 的《智能体》白皮书为基础，为智能体行动定义了独立于框架和供应商的跨行业语义约定。这些约定旨在使遥测数据既能支持运行监控，也能以便于人类监督理解的方式重建智能体的决策序列。上海人工智能实验室的 AgentDoG: A Diagnostic Guardrail Framework 提出了一种可解释 AI 模块，用于将智能体行动追溯至具体的规划步骤、工具选择或上下文误读。可理解性原则也是中国人工智能产业发展联盟发布的智能体安全承诺的基础；在该承诺中，由主要开发者组成的联盟不仅承诺向用户展示完整执行链，还承诺主动向用户提示关键操作，“保障应用行为可感知”。这一承诺凸显出，对于用户难以逐一监控每个自主步骤的系统，仅有执行透明度是不够的。该承诺要求在具有重大影响的决策作出时主动向用户呈现，而非仅使这些决策可供事后审查。
Principle 10: Human Oversight原则 10：人类监督
what is it其含义
As agents execute longer and more complex action chains, it becomes impossible for humans to approve each action. Human oversight requires structured human decisions at defined intervention points, calibrated to the risk, reversibility, and sensitivity of the action, and includes in-the-loop approval checkpoints, on-the-loop monitoring, 88
随着智能体执行的行动链越来越长、越来越复杂，人类已不可能逐一批准每项行动。人类监督要求在明确设定的干预节点作出结构化的人类决策，并根据行动的风险、可逆性和敏感性进行调整，其中包括人在回路中的审批检查点、人在回路上的监控、88
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
batch review, and AI-assisted oversight . This enables graduated autonomy: if agents demonstrate reliability, approval requirements can be relaxed, ensuring that expanded agent autonomy is earned rather than granted by default.
批量审查以及 AI 辅助监督。这使渐进式自主成为可能：如果智能体表现出可靠性，就可以放宽审批要求，从而确保智能体自主权的扩大是凭借表现赢得的，而非默认授予的。
Practical guidance for implementing Human Oversight
实施人类监督的实践指南
> Agent developers: build oversight interfaces, including human and AI-assisted oversight modes, and mechanisms through which agents must pause and request human input; use demonstrated agent performance data to determine when approval requirements can be relaxed, rather than setting autonomy levels statically.
> 智能体开发者：构建监督界面，包括人工监督和 AI 辅助监督模式，以及要求智能体暂停并请求人类输入的机制；根据智能体实际表现的数据确定何时可以放宽审批要求，而不是静态设定自主程度。
> Deployers: select which combination of oversight modes applies to their operational context, and calibrate intervention to the risk, reversibility, and complexity of each action.
> 部署方：选择适合其运行环境的监督模式组合，并根据每项行动的风险、可逆性和复杂性调整干预程度。
> Shared responsibilities: where agents operate across organisational boundaries or delegate to sub-agents, whoever operates the orchestration layer should maintain coherent oversight across the chain.
> 共同责任：当智能体跨组织边界运行或将任务委派给子智能体时，编排层的运营方应在整个链条中维持协调一致的监督。
Governance context治理背景
Human oversight is an actively developing principle in agentic AI governance. Singa-pore ’s IMDA Model AI Governance Framework calls on organisations to “define significant checkpoints or action boundaries that require human approval, especially before sen-sitive actions are executed”, specifying four trigger categories: high-stakes decisions, irreversible actions, outlier behaviour, and user-defined boundaries. Both Singapore ’s CSA
人类监督是智能体式 AI 治理中一项正在积极发展的原则。新加坡 IMDA 的《AI 治理模型框架》呼吁组织“界定需要人类批准的重要检查点或行动边界，尤其是在执行敏感行动之前”，并明确了四类触发情形：高风险决策、不可逆行动、异常行为以及用户定义的边界。新加坡 CSA 的
Draft Addendum on Securing Agentic AI and China ’s TC260 Research Report on Agent Safety Standardisation identify the overwhelming importance of human oversight – through exploitation of cognitive limitations or compromised interaction frameworks – as a distinct agentic risk vector. The EU AI Act Article 14, though limited to high-risk systems, establishes functional requirements for human oversight, including the ability for humans to disregard and override system outputs. Across these frame-works, a central governance challenge is emerging: how to operationalise meaningful human control as agent autonomy increases.
《保障智能体式 AI 安全的附录草案》和中国 TC260 的《智能体安全标准化研究报告》均将人类监督的压倒性重要性——通过利用认知局限或遭到破坏的交互框架——认定为一种独特的智能体式风险途径。欧盟《AI 法案》第 14 条虽然仅适用于高风险系统，但确立了人类监督的功能性要求，包括人类能够不采纳并推翻系统输出。在这些框架中，一个核心治理挑战正在浮现：随着智能体自主程度的提高，如何将有实质意义的人类控制落实到实际运行中。
Illustrative cases示例案例
Major agent platforms have independently converged on systems of structured human oversight that move beyond binary approve-or-reject gates. OpenAI ’s Operator System Card describes a three-tier model: user confirmations before actions with real-world consequences; a “watch mode” where the agent requires active human supervision on sensitive sites such as email; and outright task limitations, where the agent declines high-risk operations entirely. Amazon Web Services ’ Bedrock Agents implements two tiers of human validation – user confirmation (for straightforward approval of proposed actions), and return of control (for scenarios requiring the human operator to modify parameters or contribute additional context before execution). LangChain ’s middleware framework uses a similar structure, with three decision types – approve, edit, or reject – applied per tool call against a configurable policy. These implementations converge on a shared 89
主要智能体平台各自独立发展，最终都采用了结构化的人类监督体系，超越了简单的批准或拒绝二元关卡。OpenAI 的 Operator 系统卡描述了一种三层模型：在执行会产生现实后果的行动之前要求用户确认；在电子邮件等敏感网站上采用要求人类主动监督智能体的“监视模式”；以及直接限制任务，即智能体完全拒绝执行高风险操作。Amazon Web Services 的 Bedrock Agents 实现了两层人工验证——用户确认（用于直接批准所提议的行动），以及交还控制权（用于需要人类操作员在执行前修改参数或补充上下文的场景）。LangChain 的中间件框架采用类似结构，依据可配置策略，对每次工具调用应用三种决策类型——批准、编辑或拒绝。这些实现方式都遵循一种共同的 89
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
logic: agents must be able to pause before consequential actions, present structured information to a human decision-maker, and resume only on the basis of an explicit, recorded decision. Yet the industry’s own evidence suggests that per-action oversight is already showing strain. Microsoft’s response to the NIST/CAISI consultation arguing that only higher-impact or uncertain actions should be escalated to human reviewers, citing AI Approvals in Copilot Studio as one example. Both Perplexity and Anthropic , in their respective responses, identify approval fatigue as a core vulnerability: as users habit-uate to confirmation prompts, they start to approve actions without scrutiny, and the control stops providing its intended check. Claude Mythos Preview System Card equally finds that greater agent autonomy makes casual oversight harder while creating natural incentives for users to provide less of it. Google DeepMind ’s work on agent traps takes this further, anticipating attacks specifically engineered to induce approval fatigue or present benign-looking summaries that exploit automation bias in human reviewers [AI Agent Traps]. Anthropic ’s empirical measurement of agent autonomy in deployed sys-tems suggests that experienced users auto-approve more frequently but also interrupt agents more often, while agents themselves initiate check-ins at higher rates as task complexity increases, indicating that oversight shifts in form, rather than disappears, alongside experience and task demands. Several sources now point toward graduated autonomy as the response, framing human oversight not as a single mechanism but as a set of complementary tools that opera-tors select based on task length and stakes. These include reviewing plans before execution, human modification of proposed actions, agents surfacing uncertainty, flagging of irrevers-ible actions, and the ability to redirect agents mid-task. 90
逻辑：智能体必须能够在采取会产生重大后果的行动之前暂停，向人类决策者提供结构化信息，并且只有在获得明确且有记录的决策后才能继续。然而，行业自身的证据表明，逐项行动监督已经开始显现压力。Microsoft 在对 NIST/CAISI 征求意见的回应中主张，只有影响较大或存在不确定性的行动才应提交人类审核者处理，并以 Copilot Studio 中的 AI Approvals 为例。Perplexity 和 Anthropic 在各自的回应中均将审批疲劳视为核心薄弱点：随着用户逐渐习惯确认提示，他们开始不加审查地批准行动，这一控制措施也就无法再发挥其预期的制衡作用。Claude Mythos Preview 系统卡同样发现，智能体自主程度越高，随手进行的监督就越困难，同时也会自然形成促使用户减少此类监督的激励。Google DeepMind 关于智能体陷阱的研究进一步指出，未来可能出现专门设计的攻击，以诱发审批疲劳，或呈现看似无害的摘要，利用人类审核者的自动化偏见 [AI Agent Traps]。Anthropic 对已部署系统中智能体自主程度的实证测量表明，经验丰富的用户更频繁地采用自动批准，但也更经常打断智能体；与此同时，随着任务复杂性的增加，智能体自身主动请求确认的频率也会提高，这表明监督会随着经验和任务要求的变化而改变形式，而非消失。目前，多项来源都将渐进式自主视为应对之策，将人类监督定位为一组由操作员根据任务时长和风险程度选择的互补工具，而非单一机制。这些工具包括在执行前审查计划、由人类修改所提议的行动、由智能体明确呈现不确定性、标记不可逆行动，以及在任务进行过程中重新引导智能体的能力。90
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
4. Conclusion & Open Questions4. 结论与开放问题
Agentic AI risk management is a nascent field, and the governance practices that will shape it are still in development. Yet across frontier labs, cloud platforms, govern-ance frameworks, industry consortia, and academic research, a recognisable set of technical principles is taking shape for managing agent-specific risks. There is convergence within both governance frameworks and technical practice.
智能体 AI 风险管理是一个新兴领域，塑造这一领域的治理实践仍在发展之中。然而，在前沿实验室、云平台、治理框架、行业联盟和学术研究中，一套用于管理智能体特有风险的、清晰可辨的技术原则正在形成。治理框架和技术实践都呈现出趋同态势。
Different jurisdictions are identifying similar governance principles, and frontier labs and major platforms have independently implemented similar controls. Together, they are building a coherent set of safety measures across the agentic lifecycle. Different safety principles have different levels of maturity. Where principles are inherited from established security-engineering approaches, developers have more interoperable implementations to draw on; but many open problems remain, especially around multi-agent security and agentic supply chain risks. Agentic risk management requires defence-in-depth and a multi-stake-holder approach. Developers, deployers, platform providers, and the open source ecosystem each bear primary responsibility for a different subset of controls, and no single actor controls the full stack. Standardisation and interoperability protocols , including Model Context Protocol, Agent-to-Agent communication, the Open Agent Auth framework, and the Agent Payments Protocol, are emerging as the infrastructure layer on which cross-or-ganisational agentic deployments will depend. Their governance properties are becoming as consequential as their technical ones. Governance lags behind how autonomy is granted and adjusted in agentic systems.
不同司法辖区正在确立相似的治理原则，前沿实验室和主要平台也已各自独立实施了类似的控制措施。它们共同构建了一套贯穿智能体全生命周期的连贯安全措施。不同安全原则的成熟程度各不相同。对于沿袭成熟安全工程方法的原则，开发者有更多具备互操作性的实现方案可供借鉴；但仍有许多尚未解决的问题，尤其是在多智能体安全和智能体供应链风险方面。智能体风险管理需要纵深防御和多利益相关方参与的方法。开发者、部署者、平台提供商和开源生态系统分别对不同的一部分控制措施承担主要责任，没有任何单一参与方能够控制整个技术栈。标准化与互操作性协议，包括 Model Context Protocol、Agent-to-Agent 通信、Open Agent Auth 框架和 Agent Payments Protocol，正逐渐成为跨组织智能体部署所依赖的基础设施层。这些协议的治理属性正变得与其技术属性同样重要。治理落后于智能体系统中自主权的授予和调整方式。
As agents move from constrained, supervised deployment toward autonomy earned through demonstrated performance, governance frameworks and regulation have yet to catch up.
随着智能体从受约束、受监督的部署方式转向凭借已证明的表现获得自主权，治理框架和监管仍未跟上这一变化。
Future work on Agentic Risk Management智能体风险管理的未来工作
The controls mapped here address agents that are compromised, misused, or malfunctioning. The effective pursuit of misaligned goals by agents function-ing exactly as engineered presents a broader systemic risk that is not addressed in this companion report Equally, even if the principles mapped here are satisfied, this does not preclude risks that propagate through societal systems to people uninvolved in an agent’s operation—a dimension of agentic risk where further empirical research is needed. Several other gaps remain: Multi-agent governance is a pressing challenge. Real-world deployments increas-ingly spawn sub-agents and inter-agent interactions that require a systemic risk management approach. 91
此处梳理的控制措施针对的是遭到入侵、被滥用或发生故障的智能体。完全按工程设计运行的智能体若能有效追求与预期不一致的目标，就会带来更广泛的系统性风险，而本配套报告并未涉及这一风险。同样，即使满足了此处梳理的原则，也不能排除风险通过社会系统传播至未参与智能体运行的人员的可能性——智能体风险的这一维度仍需进一步开展实证研究。仍存在其他若干缺口：多智能体治理是一项紧迫的挑战。现实世界中的部署越来越多地产生子智能体及智能体之间的交互，因此需要采用系统性的风险管理方法。91
> COMPANION REPORT ON AGENTIC RISK MANAGEMENT
> 智能体风险管理配套报告
Tool supply chain risks extend beyond code dependencies to tool descriptions, remotely hosted APIs, memory stores, and orchestration protocols. Current practices treat tools as static attack surfaces, leaving the problem of configuration errors and risky connectors partly unaddressed. Self-improving agents pose a distinct category of risks. Agents that autono-mously evolve through interactions with their environment will require risk assessment and mitigation approaches that treat the agent as a moving target with continuously shifting properties. Identifying agentic risk sources remains an open problem. This companion report focuses on operationalising mitigations for known risk categories rather than on systematically surfacing new risks as agent capabilities and deploy-ment contexts evolve. Development of insurance and liability for agents could incentivise the adoption of safety practices, but methods for pricing agent failures and attributing responsibil-ity are still nascent. A key priority is post-deployment research on how users employ agents, when and how oversight shifts, and where risk management practices hold up under real use. Retirement and decommissioning are not covered by the lifecycle stages mapped here. Future work should study how to safely retire agents by deleting or exporting necessary data, revoking credentials, terminating sub-agents and back-ground tasks, releasing cloud and network resources, and preserving audit evidence.
工具供应链风险超出了代码依赖的范围，还涉及工具描述、远程托管的 API、记忆存储和编排协议。当前实践将工具视为静态攻击面，配置错误和高风险连接器的问题仍有部分未得到解决。自我改进的智能体带来了一类独特的风险。对于通过与环境交互而自主演化的智能体，需要采用将其视为属性持续变化的动态目标的风险评估与缓解方法。识别智能体的风险来源仍是一个尚未解决的问题。本配套报告侧重于将针对已知风险类别的缓解措施付诸实施，而非随着智能体能力和部署情境的演变，系统性地发现新风险。发展面向智能体的保险与责任制度可以激励安全实践的采用，但对智能体失效进行保险定价和划分责任的方法仍处于起步阶段。一项关键优先事项是开展部署后研究，探究用户如何使用智能体、监督在何时以及如何发生转变，以及风险管理实践在哪些方面能够经受实际使用的检验。此处梳理的生命周期阶段未涵盖退役和停用。未来工作应研究如何安全地使智能体退役，包括删除或导出必要数据、撤销凭据、终止子智能体和后台任务、释放云与网络资源，以及保留审计证据。
The value of Sharing Best Practices共享最佳实践的价值
This companion report establishes a shared agentic AI risk management baseline: a map of where independent actors have already arrived at similar answers, where they have not, and where the hardest questions still sit. No single developer, deployer, platform provider, regulator, or research institution holds the full picture; these actors therefore have a mutual interest in making their controls compatible and creating a unified operation-alisation of the risks. The goal is to enable secure adoption without constraining the benefits that agentic AI systems can deliver. 92
本配套报告确立了智能体 AI 风险管理的共同基线：梳理各独立主体在哪些方面已得出相似答案、在哪些方面尚未达成一致，以及最棘手的问题仍集中于何处。任何单一开发者、部署者、平台提供商、监管机构或研究机构都无法掌握全貌；因此，推动各自控制措施相互兼容，并形成统一的风险操作化方法，符合这些主体的共同利益。目标是在不限制智能体 AI 系统所能带来的益处的前提下，实现安全采用。92
